While recent large language models (LLMs) have powered impressive one-to-one conversational agents, their seamlessly integration into multi-participant conversations remains unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce GCAgent, an LLM-driven system for enhancing group chats communication with both entertainment- and utility-oriented dialogue agents. The system comprises three tightly integrated modules: Agent Builder, which customizes agents to align with users' interests; Dialogue Manager, which coordinates dialogue states and manage agent invocations; and Interface Plugins, which reduce interaction barriers by three distinct tools. Through extensive experiment, GCAgent achieved an average score of 4.68 across various criteria and was preferred in 51.04\% of cases compared to its base model. Additionally, in real-world deployments over 350 days, it increased message volume by 28.80\%, significantly improving group activity and engagement. Overall, this work presents a practical blueprint for extending LLM-based dialogue agent from one-party chats to multi-party group scenarios.

</details>


### [55] [X-RAY: Mapping LLM Reasoning Capability via Formalized and Calibrated Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05290)
*Gao Tianxi,Cai Yufan,Yuan Yusi,Dong Jin Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-RAY是一个可解释的推理分析系统，通过形式化校准和验证的探针来映射LLM的推理能力，将推理能力建模为可提取结构的函数。评估从初级到高级的数学、物理和化学问题，揭示了LLM推理的系统性不对称性：对约束细化稳健，但对解空间重构敏感。该框架无污染，支持推理模型的训练和测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要强调任务级准确性，往往将模式匹配与推理能力混为一谈，LLM的推理能力仍然难以理解。

Method: X-Ray通过形式化工具生成具有受控结构变化的探针，通过形式化校准和验证实现增量结构信息的精确分离。将推理能力建模为可提取结构的函数，通过约束交互、推理深度和解决方案空间几何等形式化属性进行操作。

Result: 分析揭示了LLM推理中系统性的不对称性：模型对约束细化（附加条件缩小现有解空间）相对稳健，但对解空间重构（修改解流形的底层结构形式）急剧下降。经过校准的形式化探针可以区分在标准基准测试中看似无法区分的模型，并揭示结构上可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: X-Ray能够区分在标准基准测试中看起来无法区分的模型，并揭示结构上可解释的失败模式。该框架无污染，支持推理模型的训练和测试。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve promising performance, yet their ability to reason remains poorly understood. Existing evaluations largely emphasize task-level accuracy, often conflating pattern matching with reasoning capability. We present X-RAY, an explainable reasoning analysis system that maps the LLM reasoning capability using calibrated, formally verified probes. We model reasoning capability as a function of extractable \textit{structure}, operationalized through formal properties such as constraint interaction, reasoning depth, and solution-space geometry. X-Ray generates probes via formal tools with controlled structural variations, enabling precise isolation of incremental structural information through formal calibration and verification. We evaluate state-of-the-art LLMs on problems ranging from junior-level to advanced in mathematics, physics, and chemistry. Our analysis reveals a systematic asymmetry in LLM reasoning: models are relatively robust to constraint refinement, where additional conditions shrink an existing solution space, but degrade sharply under solution-space restructuring, where modifications alter the underlying structural form of the solution manifold. Moreover, calibrated formal probes differentiate models that appear indistinguishable on standard benchmarks and reveal failure modes that are structurally interpretable rather than opaque. Beyond evaluation, our framework is contamination-free and supports the training and testing of reasoning models.

</details>


### [56] [STRUCTUREDAGENT: Planning with AND/OR Trees for Long-Horizon Web Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05294)
*ELita Lobo,Xu Chen,Jingjing Meng,Nan Xi,Yang Jiao,Chirag Agarwal,Yair Zick,Yan Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出STRUCTUREDAGENT框架，解决大语言模型agent在长时域网页任务中面临的记忆有限、规划能力弱和贪心行为等问题。该框架通过动态AND/OR树的分层规划器和结构化记忆模块，在WebVoyager、WebArena等基准上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有web agent在复杂长时域任务中表现不佳，主要因为：(1) 上下文记忆有限，难以追踪历史；(2) 规划能力不足；(3) 贪心行为导致过早终止。这些问题限制了agent完成信息检索等任务的能力。

Method: 提出分层规划框架STRUCTUREDAGENT，包含两个核心组件：(1) 在线分层规划器，使用动态AND/OR树进行高效搜索；(2) 结构化记忆模块，追踪和维护候选解决方案以提升约束满足。该框架还可产生可解释的分层计划，便于调试和人工干预。

Result: 在WebVoyager、WebArena和自定义购物基准上的实验表明，STRUCTUREDAGENT在长时域网页浏览任务上相比标准LLM-based agent提升了性能。

Conclusion: STRUCTUREDAGENT通过分层规划和结构化记忆有效解决了长时域web agent的挑战，验证了hierarchical planning在复杂 sequential decision-making 任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agentic systems for sequential decision-making. Such agents must perceive their environment, reason across multiple time steps, and take actions that optimize long-term objectives. However, existing web agents struggle on complex, long-horizon tasks due to limited in-context memory for tracking history, weak planning abilities, and greedy behaviors that lead to premature termination. To address these challenges, we propose STRUCTUREDAGENT, a hierarchical planning framework with two core components: (1) an online hierarchical planner that uses dynamic AND/OR trees for efficient search and (2) a structured memory module that tracks and maintains candidate solutions to improve constraint satisfaction in information-seeking tasks. The framework also produces interpretable hierarchical plans, enabling easier debugging and facilitating human intervention when needed. Our results on WebVoyager, WebArena, and custom shopping benchmarks show that STRUCTUREDAGENT improves performance on long-horizon web-browsing tasks compared to standard LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [57] [WebChain: A Large-Scale Human-Annotated Dataset of Real-World Web Interaction Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05295)
*Sicheng Fan,Rui Wan,Yifei Leng,Gaoning Liang,Li Ling,Yanyi Shang,Dehan Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebChain 是目前规模最大的开源真实网站人工标注交互轨迹数据集，包含 31,725 条轨迹、317,993 步，覆盖 428 个领域网站，核心是"三重对齐"（视觉截图/全页截图、无障碍树与DOM结构、像素级坐标与边界框及CSS选择器的动作标注）。作者配套提出 WebChainBench 评测基准与"Dual Mid-Training"（空间定位中训练 + CoT-SFT 中训练，再接长链RLVR 后训练）训练范式，在 WebChainBench 与 AndroidControl、GUI-Act-Web、GUI-Odyssey、OmniAct 等公开 GUI 基准上取得 SOTA 或显著提升，旨在打破专有数据垄断、推动可复现的 web agent 研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器是 GUI Agent 最重要且最真实的交互场景，但当前 web agent 领域面临严重的轨迹数据瓶颈：一是现有开源人工标注数据集（Mind2Web、WebLINX 等）规模过小（仅 2.3k 轨迹左右），无法验证模型的规模化效应；二是合成/自采集方法（Explorer、OS-Genesis）受反爬、验证码和隐私与认证限制，无法获取需要登录的高价值工作流（如银行、电商结算）；三是大量前沿工作依赖专有数据集，导致关键结论不可复现、阻碍社区共识形成。同时，VLA 类模型存在空间幻觉与定位错误，需要细粒度的高层意图与精确边界框/DOM 元素对齐的定位数据；RL 在开放网页任务上又面临稀疏奖励与巨大探索空间问题，强烈依赖高质量的"热启动"初始策略。因此需要大规模、真实网站、人工标注、多模态对齐且完全开源的数据集与标准化评测基准。

Method: 1) 数据构建三阶段流水线：Stage 1 约束式任务合成——先对目标网站做静态分析提取"结构化功能 schema"（领域语义 + 交互逻辑，如排序开关、分面筛选、条件依赖），再以此 schema 为条件用生成式 LLM 合成可执行任务，并按复杂度分层（简单信息检索、多约束导航、条件依赖任务）；Stage 2 人在环轨迹采集——使用 WebChain Builder 工具，标注者在真实网站上执行任务，工具被动记录动作前后 DOM 快照、动作类型（click/type/scroll）、视口像素坐标与目标元素边界框、XPath/CSS 选择器与文本，得到 (S,A,R,S') 序列；Stage 3 上下文富化——视觉定位稠密化（解析整个视口，标注所有可交互元素的边界框/类型/文本，形成稠密的布局感知分割与隐式负样本）与合成 CoT 推理链（用强 VLM 基于目标、历史状态与当前截图生成"思考"式自然语言理由）。2) 训练与评测：统一奖励加权优化，奖励 r_t = α·r_type + (1-α)·r_content（点击需落在 GT 框内，输入需为 GT 文本的词法超集）；定义空间定位与长程规划两种任务形式；提出 Dual Mid-Training——先用 SGRL（空间定位导向的 RLVR）做定位中训练并配合 CoT-SFT 提升规划，再接 LCRL（长链 RLVR）后训练解耦空间感知与时序规划；构建 WebChainBench（1.2k 交互步，含 WCB-S 与 WCB-L，按短/中/长轨迹均衡）；并做数据规模化消融（4k/20k/full 子集）、RP（Reasoner Prompting）与 VGD（视觉定位稠密化）四组对照、CoT-SFT 消融。

Result: 数据集规模领先（31725 轨迹 / 317,993 步 / 428 领域，均长 10.02 步、中位 9 步、平均 1.07 分钟），在网站数、AX 树、时间戳等模态维度优于 Mind2Web、WebLINX、WebArena、GUIAct。数据规模化实验显示 WCB-L 成功率与人工校验数据量正相关（full-150k 明显优于 4k 基线）。定位任务上 RP 与 VGD 单独均带来增益、两者结合最佳；RP 初始化的检查点在 WCB-L 上反而更差，说明定位与规划存在感知需求差异，"non-RP + VGD + LCRL" 是最强长程配置。CoT-SFT 中训练使 WCB-L 从 0.487 提升到 0.603，加上 SGRL 达 0.629–0.632，最佳组合 0.658。外部基准上，WebChain-LCRL-7B +SGRL+CoT-SFT 总体 SR 达 81.4（3B 为 77.3），显著超过 GUI-R1-3B/7B（70.5/74.2）与零样本 Qwen2.5-VL（47.0/70.9）；在 AndroidControl-High、GUI-Odyssey、OmniAct-Desktop/Web 等长程/跨应用任务上提升尤为明显，表明良好零样本迁移到移动端、桌面端与 Web 端。

Conclusion: WebChain 通过真实网站人工标注与"视觉—结构—动作"三重对齐，提供了当前最大规模的开源 web 交互轨迹语料，配合完全开源的采集工具与 WebChainBench，推动 web agent 研究从"专有数据依赖"走向可复现、可评测的社区共享范式。实验表明数据规模与长程任务成功率存在可预测的正相关，并发现"Dual Mid-Training"（把空间定位与时序规划解耦为两段中训练再接 RLVR）优于标准 SFT 单一范式，可在新长程网页任务上刷新 SOTA。作者同时强调伦理合规（隐私保护、收集脱敏与审查、不发布个人身份信息），并鼓励报告安全指标。局限与后续方向包括：数据偏向英文/特定网站与人工成本高、AX 树与 DOM 体积带来存储与训练开销、任务复杂度分层与 CoT 由 LLM 合成带来的噪声、以及评测基准规模（1.2k 步）相对有限等。

Abstract: We introduce WebChain, the largest open-source dataset of human-annotated trajectories on real-world websites, designed to accelerate reproducible research in web agents. It contains 31,725 trajectories and 318k steps, featuring a core Triple Alignment of visual, structural, and action data to provide rich, multi-modal supervision. The data is collected via a scalable pipeline that ensures coverage of complex, high-value tasks often missed by synthetic methods. Leveraging this dataset, we propose a Dual Mid-Training recipe that decouples spatial grounding from planning, achieving state-of-the-art performance on our proposed WebChainBench and other public GUI benchmarks. Our work provides the data and insights necessary to build and rigorously evaluate the next generation of scalable web agents.

</details>


### [58] [UniSTOK: Uniform Inductive Spatio-Temporal Kriging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05301)
*Lewei Xie,Haoyu Zhang,Juan Yuan,Liangjun You,Yulong Chen,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UniSTOK 是一个即插即用的归纳式时空克里金框架，针对"观测传感器自身存在块状缺失"这一现实场景，通过两个互补机制改进任意 ISK 骨干网络：①RSR（可靠性引导的信号调节）在空间传播前依据时间连续性与空间支撑度对每个条目做可靠性打分，放大可靠观测、抑制插补伪影，并注入有界的注意力残差；②RBC（残差偏置校准）在主预测器收敛后，以"预测值分箱残差原型 + 上下文自适应幅度"对最终输出做后验纠偏，修正低值高估/高值低估的值依赖偏置。实验在 4 个真实数据集、9 个骨干上验证，块状缺失下 MAE 平均相对提升约 6.9%、RMSE 约 5.3%，额外可训练参数仅 3,861 个，并优于"先插补后克里金"两阶段流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归纳式时空克里金（ISK）在交通、环境监测等场景中被广泛用于从未布设传感器的位置推断信号，但现实中已布设传感器也常因设备故障、通信中断或维护而出现不完整观测（文中举例北京环路传感器缺失率达 20%–25%）。作者通过预实验发现三点动机：(1) 相同缺失率下，块状（block-wise）缺失比随机缺失造成更大的克里金性能退化，因为它破坏了局部时间连续性，使观测传感器作为空间锚点的可靠性下降；(2) 常见的"先插补再克里金"两阶段方案存在目标错配——插补重建误差更小并不必然带来更好的下游克里金，作者在 7 种插补方法 × 6 个 ISK 骨干上的实验显示两阶段排名 Spearman 相关仅为 −0.22（METR-LA）和 −0.05（PEMS-BAY），甚至为负；(3) 插补误差具有值依赖偏置：真实传感数据分布不均衡使优化被高频区间主导，模型倾向给出保守估计，导致低值区高估、高值区低估（如 ImputeFormer 全局均值偏差仅 +1.27，但低值区 +6.32、高值区 −1.53），且该偏置在克里金后被进一步放大（IGNNK 低值条件偏差由无缺失参考的 5.35 升至 11.57–12.20）。因此核心挑战是：在缺失观测下抑制不可靠信息的空间传播，并校准值依赖的残差偏置。

Method: 整体框架为"主预测器 + 后置校准"两阶段结构，骨干网络内部结构完全不变，保持即插即用特性。

（1）RSR（Reliability-guided Signal Regulation）：基于观测掩码 M 计算条目级可靠性。时间可靠性 r^T_{i,τ}=1−log(1+δ_{i,τ})/log(1+t)，其中 δ 为双向最近观测时间距离；空间可靠性 r^S_{i,τ}=Σ_j A_ij M_{j,τ}/(Σ_j A_ij+ε)，即同时刻邻居观测支撑度；融合为 R_{i,τ}=√(r^T·r^S+ε)。在此基础上引入"可靠性引导的注意力残差"：以可靠性汇总生成时间/空间 query、由掩码生成 key，构造可靠性感知亲和度 S^T、S^S，聚合出上下文残差证据 X^T、X^S，经轻量融合网络与 tanh 限幅得到 ΔX=η·tanh(Φ_Δ([X^T;X^S]))。信号调节系数 γ=1+α·tanh((R−μ_R)/(s_R+ε))，受调节输入 X̃=γ·X+ΔX。

（2）共享骨干双视图与自适应门控融合：原始输入 X 与受调节输入 X̃ 并行送入同一骨干（参数共享）得到 Ŷ^b 与 Ŷ^r，再由条目级门控 G=σ(Φ_g([Ŷ^b;Ŷ^r;R])) 逐元素融合为 Ŷ^g=(1−G)⊙Ŷ^b+G⊙Ŷ^r，最后经输出 MLP Φ_o 得到 Ŷ。

（3）RBC（Residual Bias Calibration）：主预测器（含 RSR、共享骨干、融合）收敛后冻结。每个 epoch 在线收集训练集残差 E=Ŷ−Y，按预测值分为 K=100 个箱统计各箱均值残差原型 Ē_k，并以验证最优 epoch e* 为中心的峰值加权（ω_e=β^{|e−e*|}）跨 epoch 聚合为固定原型表。校准阶段仅训练少量参数：以高斯核软检索得到 E^p=Σ_k w_k Ē_k，再由节点态 z^V、时间态 z^T 与 |E^p| 经轻量 MLP+sigmoid 产生自适应幅度 a∈[0,1]，输出 Ŷ^cal=Ŷ−a·E^p。损失分两阶段：主预测器用掩码 MAE；校准器用平衡分箱 MAE（各值域等权，避免高频区主导）。

理论：定理 4.1 证明可靠性场的有界性（√ε≤R≤√(1+ε)）、对两个分量的严格单调性，以及块缺失长度 L 下的敏感性衰减（r^T ≤ 1−log(1+⌊L/2⌋)/log(1+t)）；定理 4.2 在 Lipschitz 条件下经三阶段（分箱原型大数律收敛→离散化 O(h) 逼近误差→三角不等式分解）证明软检索残差依概率收敛到真实条件偏置函数 β(Ŷ)，从而为渐近无偏校准提供依据。

Result: 1）骨干增强（RQ1）：块状缺失 20% 设定下，UniSTOK 对 9 个骨干（Basic：STGCN/GMAN/DCRNN；Basic kriging：IGNNK/INCREASE/SATCN；Advanced：KITS/STA-GANN/DarkFarseer）在 METR-LA、PEMS-BAY、NREL-AL 上几乎全部一致提升：MAE 平均相对改善 8.0%/5.4%/8.6%（整体 6.9%），RMSE 8.1%/2.8%/8.7%（整体 5.3%），单点最大改善如 KITS 在 PEMS-BAY 的 MAPE 提升 22.2%、DarkFarseer 在 METR-LA 的 MAE 提升 12.5%。
2）优于两阶段流水线（RQ2）：以 ImputeFormer+STA-GANN 为两阶段基线，UniSTOK 在三个数据集 MAE 全面更低（METR-LA 5.84 vs 6.04，PEMS-BAY 3.37 vs 3.84，NREL-AL 1.76 vs 1.91）。
3）消融（RQ3）：w/o RSR、w/o RBC 与原始骨干三者均退化，原始骨干最差；去掉 RBC 的退化幅度大于去掉 RSR，说明残差偏置是主要瓶颈。
4）超参敏感性（RQ4）：K、η、α 在合理范围内性能平稳，中等 K 与保守的 η、α 更优；对单一超参不敏感。
5）参数—精度权衡（RQ5）：插件总可训练参数仅 3,861（RSR/门控/融合/幅度四部分轻量 MLP，残差原型为非训练 buffer，不随节点数与样本数增长），各骨干 "+Ours" 点在参数量维度几乎水平对齐但 MAE 箭头全部向下，说明增益非来自模型容量扩张。
6）扩展性：随机缺失下平均 MAE 改善 7.0%、RMSE 7.4%；无缺失设定下 MAE 改善 7.0%，说明方法同时缓解缺失污染并校准了克里金本身的内在偏置；在真实块缺失数据集 NZ-Highway（61 站，原始缺失率 31.20%）上 9 个骨干的 MAE/RMSE 均有改善（如 KITS MAE 85.09→78.16，INCREASE RMSE 115.37→110.71），但幅度小于合成缺失数据集。可视化显示 RSR 输入差分呈双向调整（对缺失密集区偏负抑制、对可靠区局部增强），门控 G 呈结构化局部激活；RBC 后预测分布更贴近真值分布，且幅度大多接近 0、仅少数条目强校正，说明其为选择性保守校准。

Conclusion: 论文将"观测节点存在块状不完整观测下的归纳式时空克里金"形式化为一个实用问题，提出 UniSTOK：以 RSR 在空间传播前按可靠性调节输入、以共享骨干双视图加条目级门控做自适应融合、以 RBC 在主预测器收敛后以值条件残差原型做后验偏置校准，配合定理 4.1（可靠性场有界/单调/敏感性衰减）与定理 4.2（软残差检索的一致性）给出理论依据，并在 4 个真实数据集、9 个骨干上取得一致提升且额外参数开销可忽略。作者指出未来工作可扩展到多变量信号、自适应图结构与更广泛的缺失模式。

需要提示的两点：(1) 摘要（arXiv 摘要）与正文方法描述不一致——摘要描述的是"双分支输入（原始观测 + 拼图增强的代理信号，仅在缺失处合成）+ 缺失掩码调制 + 双通道注意力融合"，与正文的 RSR/共享骨干双视图/门控融合/RBC 机制不同，读者应以正文为准并注意版本差异；(2) 附录中的对比实验显示，消融所用的骨干模型本身在块缺失下相比无缺失已有明显退化（如 IGNNK 在 NREL-AL 块缺失下 MAE 退化约 26.9%），且两阶段流水线中的"最优插补方法"随骨干与数据集变化、无普适最优，这支持了本文"插补误差不是可靠代理目标"的立论，同时也说明 UniSTOK 的增益主要来自偏置校准而非填补精度本身。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal kriging aims to infer signals at unobserved locations from observed sensors and is critical to applications such as transportation and environmental monitoring. In practice, however, observed sensors themselves often exhibit heterogeneous missingness, forcing inductive kriging models to rely on crudely imputed inputs. This setting brings three key challenges: (1) it is unclear whether an value is a true signal or a missingness-induced artifact; (2) missingness is highly heterogeneous across sensors and time; (3) missing observations distort the local spatio-temporal structure. To address these issues, we propose Uniform Inductive Spatio-Temporal Kriging (UniSTOK), a plug-and-play framework that enhances existing inductive kriging backbones under missing observation. Our framework forms a dual-branch input consisting of the original observations and a jigsaw-augmented counterpart that synthesizes proxy signals only at missing entries. The two branches are then processed in parallel by a shared spatio-temporal backbone with explicit missingness mask modulation. Their outputs are finally adaptively fused via dual-channel attention. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets under diverse missing patterns demonstrate consistent and significant improvements.

</details>


### [59] [Building AI Coding Agents for the Terminal: Scaffolding, Harness, Context Engineering, and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05344)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首个针对开源、终端原生（CLI）交互式编码代理 OpenDev 的综合技术报告。系统以 Rust 编写、采用"复合 AI 系统"范式：四层架构（入口 UI / 代理 / 工具与上下文 / 持久化）、按工作流绑定 LLM 的多模型路由（action/thinking/critique/vision/compact 五个角色及回退链）、以只读 Planner 子代理替代 Plan Mode 状态机的"双代理"规划/执行分离、35 个内置工具 + 基于 MCP 的惰性工具发现、五级纵深防御安全体系，以及以自适应上下文压缩（ACC）、事件驱动系统提醒（24 个模板）、ACE 记忆管线为核心的一等公民上下文工程。作者自陈其贡献不在算法创新，而在公开生产级代理的设计决策、权衡与经验教训（如 schema 级安全优于运行时校验、注意力衰减需靠即时注入提醒对抗）。主要局限是缺乏 SWE-bench / Terminal-Bench 等系统量化评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 编码助手正从复杂 IDE 插件向终端原生（CLI）代理迁移：终端是版本控制、构建、部署与远程 SSH 的天然操作中心，能支撑长时程（long-horizon）自主任务。但持续终端操作对前沿模型仍很困难——Terminal-Bench 显示前沿 agent 解决率不足 65%，LongCLI-Bench 多类别编程任务通过率低于 20%。作者归纳出三项工程挑战：(1) 有限上下文窗口下如何管理会超出 token 预算的长会话；(2) agent 可执行任意 shell 命令时如何防止破坏性操作；(3) 如何在不让提示预算爆炸的前提下扩展能力。学术与工业现状的缺口同样关键：SWE-Agent/OpenHands 面向评测而非交互式日常使用，OpenHands 为浏览器 UI，Aider/Goose/OpenCode/Crush/Gemini CLI 均为 CLI 原生但无技术报告，Claude Code 既非开源也无报告——因此终端原生代理的设计空间仍未被充分探索。作者由此提出三个开放问题：多模型架构如何在成本/延迟/能力间权衡；何种安全机制既防破坏又不损效率；如何在有限上下文内维持长会话。论文定位是分享工程设计决策与经验，而非提出算法突破。

Method: **1. 总体架构与设计原则** 四层架构（Entry & UI、Agent、Tool & Context、Persistence），三条原则：关注点分离（模型选择、上下文管理、安全、工具调度各自可独立替换）、渐进式降级（token/迭代/网络资源耗尽时优雅退化）、透明优于魔法（每个动作可观测可覆盖）。核心范式为"复合 AI 系统"：系统非单一模型，而是多个 agent 与工作流的结构化集合，每个工作流独立绑定用户配置的 LLM，形成 session→agent→workflow→LLM 四层，实现按工作流的模型选择与成本/延迟/能力优化。

**2. Scaffolding（首个提示前装配）** BaseAgent 抽象基类 + AgentInterface 运行时协议；**急切构建**（__init__ 内即完成 system prompt 与 tool schema 构建，消除首调用延迟与 MCP 发现竞态），工具变更时由 refresh_tools() 重建；仅 MainAgent 一个具体类，所有 agent（含子代理与用户自定义）行为差异完全由 allowed_tools、_subagent_system_prompt、is_subagent 三个构造参数决定，取代早期多类继承（消除菱形依赖）；AgentFactory 三阶段严格有序装配（Skills→Subagents→Main agent，子代理注册必须先于主 agent，因 spawn_subagent 描述由已注册 agent 动态生成）；子代理以 SubAgentSpec 注册→工具白名单解析→构建受限 schema→形成 CompiledSubAgent；依赖注入上主 agent 得 AgentDependencies（7 字段），子代理仅得 SubAgentDeps（3 字段），以缺失 session_manager/console/config 形成隔离边界。

**3. Harness（运行时编排）** 围绕扩展 ReAct 循环（预检与压缩→thinking→self-critique→action→工具执行→后处理）配置七大子系统：提示组合引擎、工具注册表、安全体系、上下文工程、记忆与会话、子代理编排、输入注入队列与成本追踪。规划模式被重构为"Planner 子代理 + present_plan 单工具"的 schema 级只读方案，取代早期四工具状态机（会卡在只读态）；主 agent 全程留在 Normal Mode。thinking 分四档（OFF/LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH），HIGH 内置 self-critique（原独立的第五档因语义混淆被合并）；ReAct 结束有四条路径（显式完成、隐式完成、错误恢复预算耗尽、迭代上限），结束前校验未完成 todo 与待注入消息。输入边界采用双路径派发："/"前缀走 REPL 命令处理器（9 类 handler，无需 LLM），自然语言走代理循环。

**4. 多模型路由** 五个模型角色（action/thinking/critique/vision/compact）及逐级回退链；provider 客户端惰性初始化；模型能力元数据本地缓存（24h TTL、stale-while-revalidate、离线可启动）。

**5. 工具系统** ToolRegistry + 12 类 handler；ToolSchemaBuilder 汇聚静态内置、已发现 MCP、子代理三类 schema；10 个生命周期事件与 PreToolUse 阻塞（exit 2）/参数改写/审批覆盖；运行时审批三档（Manual/Semi-Auto/Auto）+ 四类规则（Pattern/Command/Prefix/Danger），高危规则优先级 100 不可覆盖，规则分用户全局与项目两级持久化。文件操作含 read_file（head-tail 截断 3 万字符、per-line 2 千字符、FileTimeTracker 陈旧读检测 50ms 容差）、write_file（拒绝覆盖）、edit_file（**九级模糊匹配责任链**，覆盖空白/缩进/转义/锚点等漂移，返回实际命中文本以保原格式，命中后经 LSP touch_file 并回报 Error 级诊断）；shell 执行六阶段流水线（安全门→命令准备→16 条服务器正则自动转后台→PTY 后台/管道前台分叉→3 万字符 head-tail 截断与 100ms 轮询→60s 空闲/600s 绝对超时 + InterruptToken 杀进程组）；web 层基于 Crawl4AI/Playwright 与 DuckDuckGo；LSP 语义层四层抽象（工具层/Symbol Retriever/LSP Server Wrapper/Solid Language Server），30+ 语言扩展名映射、两级缓存、JSON-RPC over stdio；任务层含 ask_user 结构化多选、todo 看板（单 doing 约束）、present_plan（approve_auto/approve/modify）、task_complete。

**6. 上下文工程（核心贡献）** (a) 条件化提示组合：21 个 markdown section，各带 condition 谓词与 priority，按 Filter→Sort→Load→Join 组装，thinking 模式仅加载 4 节；${VAR} 变量经集中注册表解析；provider 互斥条件节；compose_two_part() 为 Anthropic 做稳定/动态分块并附 cache_control，缓存节约占 80–90% 总量。(b) 工具结果优化：按工具类型摘要（文件→"Read file (142 lines, 4,831 chars)"、搜索→命中数、目录→条目数、命令→行数、错误→截断 200 字符并分类前缀），超 8,000 字符输出卸载至 scratch 文件并保留 500 字符预览 + 引用路径，截断提示按代理是否具 spawn_subagent 能力自适应。(c) 双记忆架构：episodic（每 5 条消息从完整历史重新生成 500 字符摘要，避免摘要漂移）+ working（最近 6 轮原文逐字复现）。(d) 事件驱动系统提醒：8 类检测器 + 24 个模板分 6 类，按 9 步时序注入为 role: user 消息（实验显示优于 role: system），配 one-shot 标志与预算（MAX_TODO_NUDGES=2、MAX_NUDGE_ATTEMPTS=3）。(e) 错误恢复：按 6 类错误模式匹配模板注入可操作指引，预算 3 次。(f) ACC 五级渐进压缩：70% 告警 / 80% 观察遮罩 / 85% 快速剪枝 / 90% 激进遮罩 / 99% 全量 LLM 摘要，附 Artifact Index 与历史归档路径使其近乎无损。(g) ACE 记忆管线：BulletSelector（有效性 0.5 + 新近性 0.3 + 语义相似 0.2 加权）→ Reflector（每 5 消息）→ Curator（DeltaBatch 增删改标）→ playbook 落盘。

**7. 检索与装配** 四层：锚点驱动的工具选择（符号名→find_symbol、字符串→text_search、结构→ast_search、路径→list_files）、Code Explorer 多步 agentic search、ContextPicker 六源装配（系统提示+playbook、项目/用户规则、@file 与图像、会话历史、系统提醒、当前查询，每片带 ContextPiece 溯源/优先级/token 成本）、最后经压缩强制预算并用 API 返回的 prompt_tokens 校准。

**8. 持久化** JSON/JSONL/纯文本，无外部数据库；用户级 ~/.opendev/ 与项目级 ~/.opendev/projects/{编码路径}/ 隔离；会话元数据与转录分离、fcntl.flock 独占锁 + 临时文件原子 rename、定时自动保存、git 影子快照逐步撤销。

Result: 论文以工程量化数据与经验性发现支撑各设计选择：(1) ACC 将观察类内容的峰值上下文占用降低约 54%，在典型 30 轮会话中往往完全避免触发紧急全量摘要；(2) 工具结果摘要化把单次长测试输出（可达 3 万 token）压到通常 100 token 以内，使会话典型长度从 15–20 轮延长到 30–40 轮；(3) MCP 惰性发现将外部工具 schema 的基线开销从高达 40% 降至近于零（<5%），初始上下文零外部 schema；(4) Anthropic 提示缓存对稳定部分实现约 88% 输入 token 成本下降；(5) 九级模糊匹配链消除了"content not found"这一最大编辑失败来源；(6) 基于 (tool, args) MD5 指纹、20 次滑动窗口、3 次重复的 doom-loop 检测在 3 次重复内即捕获死循环（优于迭代上限与连续读计数等粗粒度机制），并以"系统警告→审批暂停"两级升级，因模型可忽略注入文本但无法绕过真实执行中断；(7) 观察到的规律性失败模式包括：超过 15 次工具调用后稳定出现注意力衰减（提前宣布完成、放弃错误恢复、无尽探索），30+ 工具调用后系统提示约束几乎失效；role: user 提醒合规率明显高于 role: system；每 5 消息从完整历史重新生成 episodic 摘要可避免增量摘要的漂移累积。此外明确了若干反模式：早期懒加载 prompt 造成首调用延迟与 MCP 发现竞态；早期多类 agent 继承产生菱形依赖；早期把写工具给子代理造成上下文污染与 todo 竞态；早期把全部 MCP schema 塞入提示导致 40% 开销；早期用 requests 抓取无法处理 JS 渲染页面；早期用 subprocess.run 固定超时必然杀掉开发服务器；自动依赖检测不可靠，故交由 LLM 显式指定 parallel/serial。论文明确承认**缺少系统性量化评测**：未在 SWE-bench、Terminal-Bench、LongCLI-Bench 上验证。

Conclusion: 论文以公开 OpenDev 的完整技术报告形式，论证了终端原生 AI 编码代理的可工程性与关键设计规律：其一，按工作流绑定 LLM 的复合架构带来模型无关性——适配新模型只需改配置而非改代码；其二，**schema 级安全强制（从规划代理 schema 中移除写工具）比运行时权限检查更稳健**，可直接消灭一整类绕过尝试，且无状态机即无"卡在只读态"风险；其三，上下文管理必须是一等工程约束，而非事后优化——ACC 的"active→faded→archived"观测状态机有效缓解上下文膨胀；其四，三层上下文架构（静态系统提示 + 即时系统提醒 + 长期持久化）有效对抗长会话注意力衰减；其五，工具设计应吸收 LLM 的不精确性（模糊编辑匹配、陈旧读检测、错误分类恢复、遗忘型 doom-loop 检测）。作者同时给出反思与展望：未来方向包括在既有基准上做量化评测、按任务复杂度自适应配置资源（当前 70% 压缩阈值、3 次 nudge、thinking 档位均为全局固定常量）、将 ACE playbook 扩展到跨项目迁移与层级化组织、用依赖图/调用图等结构化代码表示增强记忆检索、从层级委派走向多代理点对点协作与共享黑板、用学习/RL 自动发现并定时注入有效的提醒模式，以及向 IDE 插件延伸形成 CLI–IDE 混合形态。总体定位是：面向开源、可审计、生产可用的终端优先自主软件工程系统的一份额外蓝图与诚实的设计复盘，而非新算法贡献。

Abstract: The landscape of AI coding assistance is undergoing a fundamental shift from complex IDE plugins to versatile, terminal-native agents. Operating directly where developers manage source control, execute builds, and deploy environments, CLI-based agents offer unprecedented autonomy for long-horizon development tasks. In this paper, we present OPENDEV, an open-source, command-line coding agent engineered specifically for this new paradigm. Effective autonomous assistance requires strict safety controls and highly efficient context management to prevent context bloat and reasoning degradation. OPENDEV overcomes these challenges through a compound AI system architecture with workload-specialized model routing, a dual-agent architecture separating planning from execution, lazy tool discovery, and adaptive context compaction that progressively reduces older observations. Furthermore, it employs an automated memory system to accumulate project-specific knowledge across sessions and counteracts instruction fade-out through event-driven system reminders. By enforcing explicit reasoning phases and prioritizing context efficiency, OPENDEV provides a secure, extensible foundation for terminal-first AI assistance, offering a blueprint for robust autonomous software engineering.

</details>


### [60] [Ailed: A Psyche-Driven Chess Engine with Dynamic Emotional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05352)
*Diego Armando Resendez Prado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种Personality x Psyche分解框架，通过音频信号链技术为象棋引擎添加人类行为特征（压力下的tilt和过度自信），实验表明该方法可独立于底层模型产生行为变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决象棋引擎虽然超越人类水平但缺乏人类行为特征的问题。人类棋手在时钟压力下会犯错误（如tilt和过度自信），但传统引擎无法模拟这种行为变异性。研究目标是创建类似人类的行为变异性。

Method: 提出Personality x Psyche分解框架：1) Personality是静态预设，决定引擎性格特征；2) Psyche是动态标量ψ_t∈[-100,+100]，每步后根据五个位置因素重新计算；3) 音频信号链（噪声门、压缩器/扩展器、五段均衡器、饱和限幅器）实时重塑走子概率分布；该方法适用于任何输出概率的引擎，无需搜索，仅保留ψ_t状态。

Result: 在12,414场对局中测试（对手为Maia2-1100），使用两种训练数据相差约2,800倍的概率源。结果显示：1) 顶级走子一致率呈现相同的单调梯度（从压力到过度自信约20-25个百分点差距），表明行为变异来自信号链而非底层模型；2) 过度自信时与 vanilla Maia2一致率为66%；3) 压力下竞技得分从50.8%降至30.1%。

Conclusion: 成功通过信号链方法在象棋引擎中创建了类似人类的行为变异性（如tilt和过度自信），但研究未包含人类受试者验证。

Abstract: Chess engines passed human strength years ago, but they still don't play like humans. A grandmaster under clock pressure blunders in ways a club player on a hot streak never would. Conventional engines capture none of this.
  This paper proposes a personality x psyche decomposition to produce behavioral variability in chess play, drawing on patterns observed in human games. Personality is static -- a preset that pins down the engine's character. Psyche is dynamic -- a bounded scalar ψ_t \in [-100, +100], recomputed from five positional factors after every move. These two components feed into an audio-inspired signal chain (noise gate, compressor/expander, five-band equalizer, saturation limiter) that reshapes move probability distributions on the fly. The chain doesn't care what engine sits behind it: any system that outputs move probabilities will do. It needs no search and carries no state beyond ψ_t.
  I test the framework across 12,414 games against Maia2-1100, feeding it two probability sources that differ by ~2,800x in training data. Both show the same monotonic gradient in top-move agreement (~20-25 pp spread from stress to overconfidence), which tells us the behavioral variation comes from the signal chain, not from the model underneath. When the psyche runs overconfident, the chain mostly gets out of the way (66% agreement with vanilla Maia2). Under stress, the competitive score falls from 50.8% to 30.1%. The patterns are reminiscent of tilt and overconfidence as described in human play, but I should be upfront: this study includes no human-subject validation.

</details>


### [61] [PACE: A Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine for 9-1-1 Call-taker Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05361)
*Zirong Chen,Hongchao Zhang,Meiyi Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个个性化自适应课程引擎，用于9-1-1接警员培训，通过维护学员技能状态的概率信念、建模个体学习和遗忘动态，以及推荐平衡新技能获取与现有技能保持的训练场景来增强培训师的决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 9-1-1接警员培训需要掌握一千多种相互依赖的技能，涵盖多种事件类型和协议细节。当前全国劳动力短缺已威胁到培训能力，而有效教学仍需培训师根据每个学员不断发展的能力来定制目标，这种个性化需求在当前实践中无法规模化。

Method: 1) 维护学员技能状态的概率信念；2) 建模个体学习和遗忘动态；3) 在结构化技能图上传播证据以加速诊断覆盖；4) 使用上下文 bandits 选择针对学员准备解决的能力缺口的场景。

Result: PACE实现比最新框架快19.50%的时间到能力水平，终端掌握度提高10.95%。与执业培训官员的协同研究显示，在实际案例中PACE与专家教学判断的吻合率达95.45%。在估计情况下，PACE将周转时间从11.58分钟减少到仅34秒，减少达95.08%。

Conclusion: PACE作为协同驾驶系统，能够有效扩展个性化培训，显著提高培训效率和学员掌握度，与专家判断高度一致。

Abstract: 9-1-1 call-taking training requires mastery of over a thousand interdependent skills, covering diverse incident types and protocol-specific nuances. A nationwide labor shortage is already straining training capacity, but effective instruction still demands that trainers tailor objectives to each trainee's evolving competencies. This personalization burden is one that current practice cannot scale. Partnering with Metro Nashville Department of Emergency Communications (MNDEC), we propose PACE (Personalized Adaptive Curriculum Engine), a co-pilot system that augments trainer decision-making by (1) maintaining probabilistic beliefs over trainee skill states, (2) modeling individual learning and forgetting dynamics, and (3) recommending training scenarios that balance acquisition of new competencies with retention of existing ones. PACE propagates evidence over a structured skill graph to accelerate diagnostic coverage and applies contextual bandits to select scenarios that target gaps the trainee is prepared to address. Empirical results show that PACE achieves 19.50% faster time-to-competence and 10.95% higher terminal mastery compared to state-of-the-art frameworks. Co-pilot studies with practicing training officers further demonstrate a 95.45% alignment rate between PACE's and experts' pedagogical judgments on real-world cases. Under estimation, PACE cuts turnaround time to merely 34 seconds from 11.58 minutes, up to 95.08% reduction.

</details>


### [62] [Legal interpretation and AI: from expert systems to argumentation and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05392)
*Václav Janeček,Giovanni Sartor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了人工智能与法律研究领域中法律解释的三种研究进路：专家系统（法律知识工程）、论证理论（论证结构与辩证交互）以及机器学习（通过语言模型自动生成解释建议）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术的发展，AI与法律研究面临如何处理法律解释问题的挑战，需要梳理不同方法论在该领域的应用与发展脉络。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法，从法律知识工程、论证理论和机器学习三个维度分析法律解释的表示与应用。

Result: 专家系统致力于将人类生成的法律解释精确转移至知识库以实现一致性应用；论证研究关注解释性论证的结构表示及其辩证交互，以评估论证框架内解释性主张的可接受性；机器学习则通过通用与专用语言模型自动生成解释性建议与论证，现已在法律实践中得到越来越广泛的应用。

Conclusion: AI与法律研究在法律解释问题上形成了三条主要技术路径，分别侧重于知识表示、论证结构建模和自动化生成，这些方法各有优势，未来可能呈现融合发展趋势。

Abstract: AI and Law research has encountered legal interpretation in different ways, in the context of its evolving approaches and methodologies. Research on expert system has focused on legal knowledge engineering, with the goal of ensuring that human-generated interpretations can be precisely transferred into knowledge-bases, to be consistently applied. Research on argumentation has aimed at representing the structure of interpretive arguments, as well as their dialectical interactions, to assess of the acceptability of interpretive claims within argumentation frameworks. Research on machine learning has focused on the automated generation of interpretive suggestions and arguments, through general and specialised language models, now being increasingly deployed in legal practice.

</details>


### [63] [Judge Reliability Harness: Stress Testing the Reliability of LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05399)
*Sunishchal Dev,Andrew Sloan,Joshua Kavner,Nicholas Kong,Morgan Sandler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Judge Reliability Harness开源库，用于构建验证套件测试LLM评判器的可靠性。评估发现当前SOTA评判器均不存在跨基准的普遍可靠性，对文本扰动敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM评分在AI基准测试中的广泛应用，亟需工具来有效评估这些方法的可靠性。

Method: 给定基准数据集和LLM评判器配置，生成可靠性测试以评估二元判断准确性和有序评分表现，涵盖自由响应和智能体任务格式。对四个SOTA评判器在安全、说服、滥用和智能体行为四个基准上进行实验。

Result: 发现不同模型和扰动类型之间存在显著的性能差异，没有任何一个评判器在所有基准上表现一致可靠。评判器对简单文本格式变化、释义、冗长性变化和标签翻转等敏感，一致性存在问题。

Conclusion: 开发了Judge Reliability Harness开源库，用于测试LLM评判器的可靠性。评估发现四个SOTA评判器在四个基准测试上表现存在显著差异，没有任何一个评判器在所有基准上表现一致可靠。LLM评判器存在对文本格式变化、释义、冗长性变化和标签翻转等干扰因素敏感的一致性问题。

Abstract: We present the Judge Reliability Harness, an open source library for constructing validation suites that test the reliability of LLM judges. As LLM based scoring is widely deployed in AI benchmarks, more tooling is needed to efficiently assess the reliability of these methods. Given a benchmark dataset and an LLM judge configuration, the harness generates reliability tests that evaluate both binary judgment accuracy and ordinal grading performance for free-response and agentic task formats. We evaluate four state-of-the-art judges across four benchmarks spanning safety, persuasion, misuse, and agentic behavior, and find meaningful variation in performance across models and perturbation types, highlighting opportunities to improve the robustness of LLM judges. No judge that we evaluated is uniformly reliable across benchmarks using our harness. For example, our preliminary experiments on judges revealed consistency issues as measured by accuracy in judging another LLM's ability to complete a task due to simple text formatting changes, paraphrasing, changes in verbosity, and flipping the ground truth label in LLM-produced responses. The code for this tool is available at: https://github.com/RANDCorporation/judge-reliability-harness

</details>


### [64] [Dissociating Direct Access from Inference in AI Introspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05414)
*Harvey Lederman,Kyle Mahowald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究AI模型的内省机制，发现其通过两种可分离机制检测思维注入：概率匹配和直接访问内部状态，其中直接访问机制是内容无关的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内省是基本的认知能力，但其机制尚不明确。之前的研究表明AI模型可以进行内省，但具体机制未知。

Method: 在大规模开源模型中复现Lindsey等人(2025)的思维注入检测范式，通过实验分离两种检测机制。

Result: 模型通过两种可分离机制检测注入表征：(i)概率匹配(从提示的异常感知推断)；(ii)直接访问内部状态。直接访问机制是内容无关的——模型能检测到异常发生但无法可靠识别其语义内容。两类模型都会虚构高频、具体的概念(如"apple")，正确概念猜测需要更多token。

Conclusion: 这种内容无关的内省机制与哲学和心理学中的主流理论一致。

Abstract: Introspection is a foundational cognitive ability, but its mechanism is not well understood. Recent work has shown that AI models can introspect. We study their mechanism of introspection, first extensively replicating Lindsey et al. (2025)'s thought injection detection paradigm in large open-source models. We show that these models detect injected representations via two separable mechanisms: (i) probability-matching (inferring from perceived anomaly of the prompt) and (ii) direct access to internal states. The direct access mechanism is content-agnostic: models detect that an anomaly occurred but cannot reliably identify its semantic content. The two model classes we study confabulate injected concepts that are high-frequency and concrete (e.g., "apple'"); for them correct concept guesses typically require significantly more tokens. This content-agnostic introspective mechanism is consistent with leading theories in philosophy and psychology.

</details>


### [65] [Distributed Partial Information Puzzles: Examining Common Ground Construction Under Epistemic Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05450)
*Yifan Zhu,Mariah Bradford,Kenneth Lai,Timothy Obiso,Videep Venkatesha,James Pustejovsky,Nikhil Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DPIP是一个协作建筑任务，用于研究多模态、多方设置中的共同基础建立问题，并提供了带注释的多模态数据集，评估了LLM和DEL两种建模范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在建立共同基础（共享信念和互认事实）方面存在挑战，尤其是在多模态、多方协作场景中，合作者携带不同信息。

Method: 引入分布式部分信息谜题(DPIP)协作建筑任务，收集多模态交互数据集（ speech, gesture, action），进行时间对齐和注释。评估两种范式：(1)提示LLM从多模态更新推断共享信念；(2)基于动态认知逻辑(DEL)的公理化管道。

Result: 在注释数据上的结果表明，DPIP对现代LLM追踪任务进展和信念状态的能力构成挑战。

Conclusion: DPIP是一个具有挑战性的基准测试，可用于评估AI系统在多模态协作中建立和维护共同基础的能力。

Abstract: Establishing common ground, a shared set of beliefs and mutually recognized facts, is fundamental to collaboration, yet remains a challenge for current AI systems, especially in multimodal, multiparty settings, where the collaborators bring different information to the table. We introduce the Distributed Partial Information Puzzle (DPIP), a collaborative construction task that elicits rich multimodal communication under epistemic asymmetry. We present a multimodal dataset of these interactions, annotated and temporally aligned across speech, gesture, and action modalities to support reasoning over propositional content and belief dynamics. We then evaluate two paradigms for modeling common ground (CG): (1) state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs), prompted to infer shared beliefs from multimodal updates, and (2) an axiomatic pipeline grounded in Dynamic Epistemic Logic (DEL) that incrementally performs the same task. Results on the annotated DPIP data indicate that it poses a challenge to modern LLMs' abilities to track both task progression and belief state.

</details>


### [66] [Towards Provably Unbiased LLM Judges via Bias-Bounded Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05485)
*Benjamin Feuer,Lucas Rosenblatt,Oussama Elachqar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出A-BB框架解决LLM法官的偏见问题，通过形式化方法保证减少偏见影响，在Arena-Hard-Auto评估中实现61-99%的排名相关性保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型从简单聊天机器人演进到更复杂的工作流程，正在接近AI系统用于自主、自我维护反馈循环的临界点。任何自主AI系统都依赖于自动化、可验证的奖励和反馈；在真实标签稀疏或非确定性的环境中，LLM-as-a-Judge是一个实用的奖励来源。然而，LLM法官虽持续改进，但文献中尚未引入能够强制执行具有强保证标准的系统，特别是当偏见向量未知或被 adversarially 发现时。

Method: 提出平均有界偏见(average bias-boundedness, A-BB)算法框架，通过形式化保证来减少LLM法官中任何可测量偏见的影响/伤害。评估在Arena-Hard-Auto上使用四个LLM法官进行。

Result: 在(tau=0.5, delta=0.01)的偏见有界保证下，保持了61-99%与原始排名的相关性，大多数法官-偏见组合超过80%。

Conclusion: A-BB框架能够为LLM-as-Judge设置中的偏见问题提供强保证，有效减少可测量偏见带来的伤害，同时保持较高的排名相关性。

Abstract: As AI models progress beyond simple chatbots into more complex workflows, we draw ever closer to the event horizon beyond which AI systems will be utilized in autonomous, self-maintaining feedback loops. Any autonomous AI system will depend on automated, verifiable rewards and feedback; in settings where ground truth is sparse or non-deterministic, one practical source of such rewards is an LLM-as-a-Judge. Although LLM judges continue to improve, the literature has yet to introduce systems capable of enforcing standards with strong guarantees, particularly when bias vectors are unknown or adversarially discovered. To remedy this issue, we propose average bias-boundedness (A-BB), an algorithmic framework which formally guarantees reductions of harm/impact as a result of any measurable bias in an LLM judge. Evaluating on Arena-Hard-Auto with four LLM judges, we achieve (tau=0.5, delta=0.01) bias-bounded guarantees while retaining 61-99% correlation with original rankings across formatting and schematic bias settings, with most judge-bias combinations exceeding 80%. The code to reproduce our findings is available at https://github.com/penfever/bias-bounded-evaluation.

</details>


### [67] [The Spike, the Sparse and the Sink: Anatomy of Massive Activations and Attention Sinks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05498)
*Shangwen Sun,Alfredo Canziani,Yann LeCun,Jiachen Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了Transformer中大规模激活和注意力锚点这两个现象虽然经常同时出现，但实际上是功能不同的现象。前者作为全局隐式参数，后者调节局部注意力偏好的短程依赖。Pre-norm架构是导致两者同时出现的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer语言模型中两个反复出现的现象：大规模激活（massive activations，单个token在少数通道中表现出极端异常值）和注意力锚点（attention sinks，某些token吸引与其语义相关性无关的不成比例的注意力）。先前工作观察到这些现象经常同时出现且涉及相同的token，但它们的功能角色和因果关系尚不清楚。

Method: 通过系统实验进行研究

Result: 表明同时出现主要是现代Transformer设计的架构artifact，两个现象服务于相关但不同的功能。大规模激活在全局范围内运作：它们诱导跨层持续存在的近似恒定的隐藏表示，有效作为模型的隐式参数。注意力锚点在局部范围内运作：它们调节跨头的注意力输出并使个体头偏向于短程依赖。识别出pre-norm配置是关键选择使得两者同时出现，表明移除它会导致两个现象解耦。

Conclusion: 两个现象的同时出现主要是架构设计的产物，服务于相关但不同的功能。Pre-norm配置是连接这两个现象的关键设计选择。

Abstract: We study two recurring phenomena in Transformer language models: massive activations, in which a small number of tokens exhibit extreme outliers in a few channels, and attention sinks, in which certain tokens attract disproportionate attention mass regardless of semantic relevance. Prior work observes that these phenomena frequently co-occur and often involve the same tokens, but their functional roles and causal relationship remain unclear. Through systematic experiments, we show that the co-occurrence is largely an architectural artifact of modern Transformer design, and that the two phenomena serve related but distinct functions. Massive activations operate globally: they induce near-constant hidden representations that persist across layers, effectively functioning as implicit parameters of the model. Attention sinks operate locally: they modulate attention outputs across heads and bias individual heads toward short-range dependencies. We identify the pre-norm configuration as the key choice that enables the co-occurrence, and show that ablating it causes the two phenomena to decouple.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [From Spark to Fire: Modeling and Mitigating Error Cascades in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04474)
*Yizhe Xie,Congcong Zhu,Xinyue Zhang,Tianqing Zhu,Dayong Ye,Minfeng Qi,Huajie Chen,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型多智能体系统(LLM-MAS)中错误传播与放大的问题，提出了传播动力学模型来表征放大风险，并设计了基于家谱图的无中心治理层来抑制错误放大，防御成功率从0.32提升至0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS在复杂协作场景中应用广泛，但协作机制可能导致细微的不准确在迭代中逐渐固化为系统级的虚假共识。错误通过消息依赖传播和放大，风险难以追溯，现有防护措施往往依赖单智能体验证或需修改协作架构，可能削弱有效信息流动。

Method: 1. 提出针对LLM-MAS的传播动力学模型，将协作抽象为有向依赖图，提供早期风险准则来表征放大风险；2. 在六个主流框架上开展实验，识别三类漏洞：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性；3. 设计攻击实验，注入单个原子错误种子即可导致广泛故障；4. 引入基于家谱图的治理层，作为消息层插件实现，无需修改协作架构即可抑制内生和外生错误放大。

Result: 识别出三类漏洞：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性；注入单个原子错误种子即可导致系统级大规模故障；治理层将防御成功率从0.32提升至0.89以上，显著缓解了轻微错误的级联传播。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM-MAS中错误传播的内在机制和放大风险，通过家谱图治理层提供了无需修改协作架构的防护方案，为构建更可靠的多智能体系统提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly applied to complex collaborative scenarios. However, their collaborative mechanisms may cause minor inaccuracies to gradually solidify into system-level false consensus through iteration. Such risks are difficult to trace since errors can propagate and amplify through message dependencies. Existing protections often rely on single-agent validation or require modifications to the collaboration architecture, which can weaken effective information flow and may not align with natural collaboration processes in real tasks. To address this, we propose a propagation dynamics model tailored for LLM-MAS that abstracts collaboration as a directed dependency graph and provides an early-stage risk criterion to characterize amplification risk. Through experiments on six mainstream frameworks, we identify three vulnerability classes: cascade amplification, topological sensitivity, and consensus inertia. We further instantiate an attack where injecting just a single atomic error seed leads to widespread failure. In response, we introduce a genealogy-graph-based governance layer, implemented as a message-layer plugin, that suppresses both endogenous and exogenous error amplification without altering the collaboration architecture. Experiments show that this approach raises the defense success rate from a baseline of 0.32 to over 0.89 and significantly mitigates the cascading spread of minor errors.

</details>


### [69] [Strategic Interactions in Multi-Level Stackelberg Games with Non-Follower Agents and Heterogeneous Leaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04628)
*Niloofar Aminikalibar,Farzaneh Farhadi,Maria Chli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究提出三层Stackelberg框架解决传统模型忽略非跟随者的问题，通过电动汽车充电基础设施案例展示该框架如何捕捉异质竞争者与非跟随者对战略激励和均衡结果的定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常忽略不直接参与市场竞争但同样造成和适应拥塞的非跟随者智能体，其行为重塑拥塞模式进而影响领导者和跟随者的决策，导致系统性扭曲的均衡预测。

Method: 提出一个三层Stackelberg博弈框架，包含异质领导者（决策视野和可行行动不同）、战略性跟随者以及非跟随者智能体，捕捉基础设施决策、竞争与均衡拥塞之间的双向耦合。

Result: 模型说明明确考虑非跟随者和异质竞争者会定性地改变战略激励和均衡结果。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于移动、能源和计算市场中更广泛的拥塞耦合多智能体系统类别，为理解和优化涉及非跟随者的复杂交互系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in congested systems is commonly modelled using Stackelberg games, where competing leaders anticipate the behaviour of self-interested followers. A key limitation of existing models is that they typically ignore agents who do not directly participate in market competition, yet both contribute to and adapt to congestion. Although such non-follower agents do not generate revenue or respond to market incentives, their behaviour reshapes congestion patterns, which in turn affects the decisions of leaders and followers through shared resources.
  We argue that overlooking non-followers leads to systematically distorted equilibrium predictions in congestion-coupled markets. To address this, we introduce a three-level Stackelberg framework with heterogeneous leaders differing in decision horizons and feasible actions, strategic followers, and non-follower agents that captures bidirectional coupling between infrastructure decisions, competition, and equilibrium congestion.
  We instantiate the framework in the context of electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure, where charging providers compete with rivals, while EV and non-EV traffic jointly shape congestion. The model illustrates how explicitly accounting for non-followers and heterogeneous competitors qualitatively alters strategic incentives and equilibrium outcomes. Beyond EV charging, the framework applies to a broad class of congestion-coupled multi-agent systems in mobility, energy, and computing markets.

</details>


### [70] [SCoUT: Scalable Communication via Utility-Guided Temporal Grouping in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04833)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SCoUT通过时间分组和智能体抽象解决部分可观测多智能体强化学习中通信学习的挑战。该方法使用Gumbel-Softmax对软智能体组进行重采样，通过组感知 Critic 降低复杂度，并利用反事实通信优势实现精确的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的多智能体强化学习(MARL)中，通信可以改善协调，但学习何时以及与谁通信面临两大挑战：1) 需要在众多可能的发送者-接收者对中选择；2) 任何单条消息对未来奖励的影响难以分离。

Method: 提出SCoUT方法，包含三个核心组件：1) 时间分组：每K个环境步骤通过Gumbel-Softmax重采样软智能体组，作为可微分的接收者先验；2) 组感知Critic：基于分组分配预测每组价值并映射到智能体基线，降低Critic复杂度和方差；3) 三头策略：环境动作、发送决策和接收者选择；4) 反事实通信优势：通过解析移除每个发送者对接收者聚合消息的贡献，获得精确的通信学习信号。

Result: 方法在抽象中未明确陈述具体性能结果。

Conclusion: SCoUT在执行时丢弃所有中心化训练组件，仅运行每个智能体的策略，保持去中心化执行。

Abstract: Communication can improve coordination in partially observed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but learning \emph{when} and \emph{who} to communicate with requires choosing among many possible sender-recipient pairs, and the effect of any single message on future reward is hard to isolate. We introduce \textbf{SCoUT} (\textbf{S}calable \textbf{Co}mmunication via \textbf{U}tility-guided \textbf{T}emporal grouping), which addresses both these challenges via temporal and agent abstraction within traditional MARL. During training, SCoUT resamples \textit{soft} agent groups every \(K\) environment steps (macro-steps) via Gumbel-Softmax; these groups are latent clusters that induce an affinity used as a differentiable prior over recipients. Using the same assignments, a group-aware critic predicts values for each agent group and maps them to per-agent baselines through the same soft assignments, reducing critic complexity and variance. Each agent is trained with a three-headed policy: environment action, send decision, and recipient selection. To obtain precise communication learning signals, we derive counterfactual communication advantages by analytically removing each sender's contribution from the recipient's aggregated messages. This counterfactual computation enables precise credit assignment for both send and recipient-selection decisions. At execution time, all centralized training components are discarded and only the per-agent policy is run, preserving decentralized execution. Project website, videos and code: \hyperlink{https://scout-comm.github.io/}{https://scout-comm.github.io/}

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [71] [iScript: A Domain-Adapted Large Language Model and Benchmark for Physical Design Tcl Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04476)
*Ning Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Senlin Shu,Lei Qi,Jiaqi Lv,Wensuo Wang,Tianhao Zhao,Chao Zhang,Zhaoliang Yang,Xiangyu Li,Zhaorui Su,Jingshan Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出iScript，一个针对Innovus Tcl脚本生成的领域适配Qwen3-8B模型，以及iScript-Bench基准测试。为解决训练数据不足问题，引入了多阶段数据合成pipeline，产生10K条(需求, CoT, 脚本)数据集。通过两阶段训练策略和两步验证框架，iScript在EDA脚本生成任务上超越了现有LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代EDA流程严重依赖Tcl脚本，但通用LLM在该领域表现不佳，原因包括：极端数据稀缺、领域特定语义、以及物理设计所需的高可靠性

Method: iScript模型采用两阶段训练策略（领域自适应预训练+监督微调）；多阶段数据合成pipeline（命令提取+静态linting+需求回溯+CoT生成）；两步验证框架（静态语法验证+基于LLM的功能评估）

Result: iScript在iScript-Bench上展示了比当前最先进的LLM更高的pass@k分数

Conclusion: Domain adaptation and data synthesis are effective for EDA scripting tasks

Abstract: Modern EDA flows rely heavily on Tcl scripting, yet general LLMs perform poorly in this domain due to extreme data scarcity, domain-specific semantics, and the high reliability required in physical design. We present iScript, a domain-adapted Qwen3-8B model for Innovus Tcl script generation, and iScript-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark covering five task categories and three difficulty levels. To overcome the lack of training data, we introduce a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline that integrates command extraction, static linting, requirement back-inference, and Chain-of-Thought generation, producing a 10K-tuple (requirement, CoT, script) dataset. iScript is trained through a two-stage strategy combining domain-adaptive pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. To evaluate script correctness efficiently, we further propose a two-step verification framework consisting of static syntax verification and LLM-based functional evaluation. On our benchmark, iScript shows higher pass@k scores than currently state-of-the-art LLMs on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain adaptation and data synthesis for EDA scripting tasks.

</details>


### [72] [CLARC: C/C++ Benchmark for Robust Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04484)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Weike Fang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLARC，一个用于代码检索的 C/C++ 基准测试集，通过自动化 pipeline 构建并引入多种挑战性场景（标识符匿名化、低级语言编译），揭示了当前 SOTA 模型在语义理解方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准测试主要聚焦于 Python 语言，且缺乏对模型鲁棒性的深层考验，仅停留在表层的词汇线索层面。

Method: 构建了CLARC基准测试集，包含1,245个评估对和5,472个训练对，采用自动化 pipeline 确保代码可编译性，并根据依赖复杂度对代码片段进行分类。引入挑战性设置：标识符匿名化、编译至 Assembly 和 WebAssembly，以 stress-test 检索模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 对6个 SOTA 代码检索模型进行评估，结果显示在挑战性条件下模型检索效果急剧下降，暴露了模型对词汇特征的持续依赖而非真正的代码语义理解。

Conclusion: 实验表明当前模型在代码语义理解方面存在明显不足，依赖于表层的词汇特征而非深层的语义推理。在更具挑战性的场景（如标识符匿名化、低级语言编译）下，模型检索效果显著下降，这揭示了现有代码检索模型的脆弱性。

Abstract: Efficient code retrieval is critical for developer productivity, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on Python and rarely stress-test robustness beyond superficial lexical cues. To address the gap, we introduce an automated pipeline for code search datasets and present CLARC, a C/C++ benchmark built from real-world GitHub repositories. CLARC contains 1,245 query-code pairs for evaluation and 5,472 pairs for training. The benchmark incorporates LLM-generated natural language queries validated through rigorous human scoring and hypothesis testing. To analyze contextual requirements effectively, our pipeline starts by ensuring code compilability. It then categorizes code snippets by dependency complexity, distinguishing whether the code relies on custom-defined types or helper functions. The pipeline also enables CLARC to stress-test retrieval robustness by introducing challenging settings, including identifier anonymization and compilation to low-level languages like Assembly and WebAssembly. Under these conditions, our evaluation of six state-of-the-art models reveals sharp drops in retrieval effectiveness. The experimental results highlight the models' persistent reliance on lexical features rather than code semantic understanding. Our dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ClarcTeam/CLARC.

</details>


### [73] [Industrial Survey on Robustness Testing In Cyber Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04587)
*Christophe Ponsard,Abiola Paterne Chokki,Jean-François Daune*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项针对瓦隆尼亚地区多个行业的工业调查，旨在评估CPS鲁棒性的当前实践状态，涵盖鲁棒性在需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、失效模式和工具方面的理解与应用，识别行业实践与最新方法论之间的差距，并通过与文献中类似调查对比来验证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在现代工业中扮演关键角色，涉及制造、能源、交通和医疗等领域，其失效可能导致严重经济、运营和安全后果，因此确保CPS鲁棒性至关重要，但目前对其实际应用状态缺乏全面了解。

Method: 采用工业问卷调查方法，对瓦隆尼亚地区多个行业的CPS鲁棒性实践进行了广泛调研，涵盖需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、失效模式及可用工具等方面，并将结果与文献中类似工业调查进行对比分析。

Result: 识别出CPS鲁棒性面临的关键挑战和行业实践与最先进方法论之间的主要差距，并通过对标同类工业调查验证了发现的普遍性和区域性特征。

Conclusion: 该调查揭示了行业实践与最新方法论之间存在显著差距，为未来CPS鲁棒性研究提供了实践基础和改进方向。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) play a critical role in modern industrial domains, including manufacturing, energy, transportation, and healthcare, where they enable automation, optimization, and real-time decision-making. Ensuring the robustness of these systems is paramount, as failures can have significant economic, operational, and safety consequences. This paper present findings from an industrial survey conducted in Wallonia, covering a wide range of sectors, to assess the current state of practice in CPS robustness. It investigates robustness from how it is understood and applied in relationship with requirements engineering, system design, test execution, failure modes, and available tools. It identifies key challenges and gaps between industry practices and state-of-the-art methodologies. Additionally, it compares our findings with similar industrial surveys from the literature.

</details>


### [74] [Vibe Code Bench: Evaluating AI Models on End-to-End Web Application Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04601)
*Hung Tran,Langston Nashold,Rayan Krishnan,Antoine Bigeard,Alex Gu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 Vibe Code Bench（VCB），一个面向「零到一」端到端 Web 应用开发能力的基准：包含 100 个真实感的应用规格（50 私有验证集 + 50 保留测试集）、964 条浏览器端到端工作流、10,131 个子步骤，由自主浏览器代理（Browser Use + Claude Sonnet 4.5）对模型生成并部署的应用做点击式测试并按子步骤判定。在 9 家提供商的 16 个前沿模型上，最佳者 GPT-5.3-Codex 仅取得 61.8%（摘要版本记为 58.0%）的测试集准确率，说明可靠的端到端应用构建仍是前沿难题。核心发现：生成过程中的「自测」（浏览器调用量）与最终成绩强相关（Pearson r=0.72，控制延迟后仍为 0.72），模型进步主要来自「减少完全失败的应用」而非全局小幅提升；并通过三人外部标注对齐研究证明评测器的选择会实质影响结论（步级两两一致率 31.8%–93.6%），故评测器选择应作为一等报告事项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成已成为生成式 AI 影响最大的用例之一，但现有基准（HumanEval/MBPP 的函数级、SWE-Bench 系列的问题修复级、SWE-Lancer 的有范围接单级、LiveCodeBench 的竞赛题级、Terminal-Bench 的命令行级、WebArena/VisualWebArena 的既成网站导航级）测的都是"局部任务"，无法回答"vibe coding"最关键的承诺：非技术用户能否仅凭自然语言规格一次性构建出完整可部署的应用（含多文件代码、配置、部署与真实用户工作流）。同时，自动化浏览器评测本身的可信度缺乏外部人类校验，存在"评测器偏差"风险。

Method: 基准设计三原则：真实性（贴近非技术用户诉求）、可复现性（统一环境与 harness）、实现无关（按用户可见行为判定）。数据构造分四步：创意生成（消费级应用、YC 创业想法、咨询案例、企业内部需求）→ GPT-5 结构化输出生成初始规格及每任务 6–23 条 UI 工作流 → 两名 PM 按统一 rubric 两轮人工评审（Accept/Modify/Reject）→ 冻结前逐行审核并用 LLM 审计规格—测试错配、浏览器不可执行步骤、匿名认证不一致与测试间隔离违规，再人工修订。100 个规格按 Individual(24)/Solo Founder(45)/Enterprise Tool(31) 分类，28 个任务需接入 Stripe 或 MailHog（沙箱/测试模式）。生成 harness 基于 OpenHands 改造：每任务独立容器（含 Docker-in-Docker 以支持内层应用 Compose 栈）、终端与浏览器工具、Supabase/MailHog/Stripe 端点、Supabase Studio MCP(11) 与 Tavily MCP(4) 工具，统一 5 小时挂钟预算作为努力度归一化，强制 React+Vite+Tailwind+Supabase 技术栈与 Docker Compose 交付，配套详细系统提示（技术栈/UI-UX 规范/环境变量/Docker 网络与端口/禁止 mock/双 README）。评测流水线：启动 Compose 验证可达性 → 每个工作流新开 1920×1200 无头浏览器会话、Browser Use 代理以视觉模式执行预写子步骤并做 LLM-as-judge 判定 → 子步骤通过率 ≥90% 记该工作流通过 → 应用准确率=通过工作流占比；应用启动失败或无结构化输出则该应用全部工作流判失败；工作流间通过全新会话与唯一账号/数据隔离防状态泄漏。指标含准确率（50 个应用均值±SE）、成本（原生定价含缓存）、生成端到端延迟；附加 20 应用子集的推理强度与视觉消融、生成侧/评测侧方差分解（3 模型×6 应用×5 次生成与 5 次重评）、六类失败模式分类，以及 18 个应用/1401 子步的 3 名人类评审 vs 4 个模型评测器的步级两两一致率矩阵。

Result: 1) 排名：GPT-5.3-Codex 61.77±4.71%、Claude Opus 4.6 57.57±4.37%、GPT-5.2 与 Opus 4.6 Thinking 并列 53.50%、Sonnet 4.6 51.48%、GPT-5.2-Codex 37.91%、Gemini 3.1 Pro 32.03%；开源模型 GLM-5 23.36%、Gemini 3 Flash 20.20%、Kimi-K2.5 17.54%、Qwen3.5 Plus 15.74%、MiniMax M2.5 14.85%、DeepSeek V3.2 5.11%、Grok 4.1 Fast 1.20%。VCB 的区分度远高于 SWE-Bench（MiniMax M2.5 与 Opus 4.6 差距在 SWE-Bench 仅 2.8%，在 VCB 达 42.7%）。2) 成本/延迟：准确率与成本、延迟大体正相关但有例外（Opus 4.6 以低于 GPT-5.3-Codex 的成本与延迟接近其精度），GLM-5 因超长轨迹成为双高离群点，两前沿均呈边际收益递减。3) 难度/集成/域：Easy/Medium/Hard 平均 44.29/21.36/6.03%，五个开源模型在 Hard ≤2.5%；无集成→邮件+Stripe 全集成时 GPT-5.3-Codex 从 71.25% 降至 29.58%、全模型均值 34.18%→13.49%；域上 Individual 43.01% > Solo Founder 29.94% > Enterprise Tool 18.64%。4) 消融：GPT 与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 随推理强度上升而提升，Opus 4.6 因自适应思考基本无变化；关闭视觉使 GPT-5.3-Codex 与 Gemini 3.1 Pro 下降而 Opus 4.6 Thinking 几乎不变。5) 工具使用：22 个工具中约 95% 调用集中在 bash/编辑/浏览器/SQL/迁移五类，模型偏好裸 SQL 而非迁移工具，Tavily 仅 18.8% 的模型使用；浏览器调用与应用准确率 Pearson r=0.72（中位数 0.67，控制延迟后偏相关仍 0.72），编辑调用仅弱相关（r=0.09）；GPT-5.2 比 GPT-5.2-Codex 浏览器调用多近 3 倍（26.4 vs 9.4）、零分应用更少（9 vs 14）、满分应用更多（6 vs 1）。6) 错误分析：Top5 模型 12.8% 应用得 0 分 vs Bottom5 的 73.2%，后者另有 14.0% 应用完全无法启动，Top5 中满分应用仅 8.8%；行为失败分类为缺失功能 46.7%、授权问题 20.4%、校验/策略阻断 14.8%（多为 RLS 配置错误）、UI 渲染/导航 6.0%、数据一致性 1.9%、其他 10.2%；Grok 4.1 与 GPT-5.2-Codex 授权类失败偏高（32.3%、30.3%），Gemini 3.1 Pro 缺失功能占比最高（58.5%）。7) 方差：评测侧重跑稳定，生成侧占方差 92.5%/92.1%/73.5%，说明主结论稳健，仅近似并列模型需谨慎。8) 对齐：人—人一致 88.6%–93.6%；模型—模型从 33.1% 到 87.8%；模型—人池化均值 Claude Sonnet 4.5 86.4% > Sonnet 4.6 86.3% > Gemini 3.1 Pro 84.7% ≫ GPT-5.2 36.1%，故选用 Sonnet 4.5 作为标准评测器（尽管其单工作流成本 $1.48 高于 GPT-5.2 的 $0.093）。

Conclusion: 可靠的端到端「零到一」应用开发尚未解决：16 个前沿模型中最佳仅 61.8%，且随难度、外部服务集成（邮件/Stripe）与企业级角色复杂度上升而急剧退化。两点结论具有方法论意义：其一，模型能力的提升主要来自消除"完全失败"的应用而非各档位的渐进改善，因此评测与训练都应重点盯住零分应用；其二，生成期自测行为与成绩强相关（r=0.72），提示"边建边验"是当前最有效的可靠性杠杆。对基准研究本身而言，评测器选择必须作为一等公民被报告与披露——自动评测只有在选对模型（与人对齐度高）时才可信，不同评测器给出的排名可能实质不同（GPT-5.2 评测器一致率仅 36%）。局限包括：不评估代码质量/设计/安全性、单一 OpenHands harness、单一 React+Vite+Supabase 技术栈、仅覆盖浏览器端应用（CLI/桌面/移动未探索）。论文强调："问题已不再是 AI 能否写代码，而是能否构建软件"，VCB 旨在跟踪通向该标准的过程。

Abstract: Code generation has emerged as one of AI's highest-impact use cases, yet existing benchmarks measure isolated tasks rather than the complete "zero-to-one" process of building a working application from scratch. We introduce Vibe Code Bench, a benchmark of 100 web application specifications (50 public validation, 50 held-out test) with 964 browser-based workflows comprising 10,131 substeps, evaluated against deployed applications by an autonomous browser agent.
  Across 16 frontier models, the best achieves only 58.0% accuracy on the test split, revealing that reliable end-to-end application development remains a frontier challenge. We identify self-testing during generation as a strong performance predictor (Pearson r=0.72), and show through a completed human alignment study that evaluator selection materially affects outcomes (31.8-93.6% pairwise step-level agreement).
  Our contributions include (1) a novel benchmark dataset and browser-based evaluation pipeline for end-to-end web application development, (2) a comprehensive evaluation of 16 frontier models with cost, latency, and error analysis, and (3) an evaluator alignment protocol with both cross-model and human annotation results.

</details>


### [75] [RepoLaunch: Automating Build&Test Pipeline of Code Repositories on ANY Language and ANY Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05026)
*Kenan Li,Rongzhi Li,Linghao Zhang,Qirui Jin,Liao Zhu,Xiaosong Huang,Geng Zhang,Yikai Zhang,Shilin He,Chengxing Xie,Xin Zhang,Zijian Jin,Bowen Li,Chaoyun Zhang,Yu Kang,Yufan Huang,Elsie Nallipogu,Saravan Rajmohan,Qingwei Lin,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoLaunch 是一个基于大语言模型智能体（LM agent）的多阶段工作流，用于自动完成代码仓库的依赖安装、编译构建与测试结果抽取，支持任意编程语言与任意操作系统（Linux/Windows/Android/嵌入式）。作者在论文中形式化定义了"仓库构建与管理（repository build and test management）"问题，构建了包含 Preparation、Build、Management 三阶段的智能体流水线，在九种语言上达到约 78–80% 的构建成功率（较此前仅支持 Python/Linux 的 repo2run 提升 18%），并在此之上提出全自动的 SWE 数据集构建流水线，发布首个多语言、多平台、持续更新的 SWE-bench-Live/MultiLang（1077 个实例）与 SWE-bench-Live/Windows（65+ 实例）数据集，同时对主流 LM 与 SWE 智能体在该数据集上的表现做了评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建和测试软件仓库长期依赖大量人工：仓库配置千差万别、文档常不完整、不同平台有各自的依赖与编译错误，因此"把仓库跑起来"成为扩展自动化 SWE（软件工程）工作流的最大瓶颈。GitTaskBench 报告在 65% 的案例中，智能体甚至在开始解决问题之前就无法构建出可执行环境；同时把"环境构建"与"问题修复"合并为一个任务会混淆两类失败模式。另一方面，SWE-bench 系列基准的评测、以及基于执行反馈的拒绝采样微调与强化学习训练，都需要大规模可复现的可执行沙箱和结构化测试结果，人工产能远不足以支撑。此外，已有的规则式工具（Oss-Fuzz-Gen、Pipreqs、railpack 等）与早期智能体方案（Repo2Run、SWE-rebench、OpenSWE）要么依赖硬编码规则、要么局限于 Python/Linux 与单一语言（Python 的构建往往只需 `pip install .`、测试框架以 pytest 为主），无法覆盖 GitHub 上噪声化、多样化的真实仓库结构与多语言多平台场景。因此需要首个跨语言、跨平台的智能体化仓库构建与管理方案，并配套可规模化的 SWE 任务自动生成流水线。

Method: 问题形式化。将仓库构建与管理建模为状态转移问题：设环境状态集 𝒮、命令序列集 𝒞，状态转移函数 δ:𝒮×𝒞→𝒮 与验证函数 ε:ℛ×𝒮→{0,1}（0 表示构建成功）；三个阶段分别为：Preparation（从裸 OS 经发现序列确定可接受的 base 镜像 B=δ(S_∅,C_B)）、Build（选择构建命令序列 P 使 S_f=δ(B,P) 且 ε(R,S_f)=0）、Management（固定 (B*,𝒫*) 后抽取最小重构建子序列 𝒫_R⊆𝒫*，保证对更新后的仓库 R' 仍满足 ε=0；同时确定测试命令 C_test、生成日志解析器 Π:ℒ→𝒪^𝒯 将日志映射为 pass/fail/skip 的逐测试状态）。
三阶段多智能体流程：
1) Preparation Agent——扫描仓库文件树，读取配置/说明文件，选择基础镜像、拉起容器并拷贝仓库，将镜像名、相关文件内容与语言专属说明作为上下文传递；
2) Setup Agent（Build）——配备 Bash 工具（容器内执行）与 WebSearch 工具（检索外部知识解错），安装依赖、编译仓库，并寻找回归测试命令；若多数测试通过则交由 Verify Agent；
3) Verify Agent——从命令历史中识别能报告"单个测试用例"状态的测试命令，要求多数测试通过且核心功能/集成测试通过才提交容器为新镜像，否则回报 Setup Agent 重试直至上限，用于抑制 LM 幻觉（人工核验 100 个通过实例仅 2 例构建不完整，且被后续 agent 捕获）；
4) Organize Agent（Management）——过滤优化命令历史生成最小重构建命令集并回环验证；蒸馏最小测试命令集；生成 Python 测试日志解析器（优先使用 JSON/XML 等结构化输出以降低解析难度）；可选生成逐测试用例执行脚本；可选从镜像层确定性重建 Dockerfile。
支撑工具：各语言/平台的 base 镜像清单（官方镜像优先，否则自建 custom 镜像）与语言专属构建/测试说明（如 C/C++ on Windows 的 choco/CMake/Ninja/vcpkg/VSBuildTools 用法）。
数据集生成流水线（Crawl → Verification → Split → Launch → Validation）：筛选 star>1k 且主语言占比>60% 的仓库，爬取已合并 PR 及关联 issue；用推理型 LM 校验 test patch 是否超出 issue 描述（避免泄漏）、并把 issue 分类为通用/Linux 或 Windows 专属；运行 RepoLaunch 构建沙箱镜像并抽取重构建命令、测试命令与解析器；最后分别施加 test patch 与 test+solution patch，对比测试状态得到 PASS_TO_PASS 与 FAIL_TO_PASS（对需要编译的语言，"编译失败导致测试无法运行"也计入 FAIL_TO_PASS）。人类只需参与任务设计环节。

Result: （1）构建与管理能力：在 152 个人工校验的 SWE-bench 实例基准（SWE-bench-Verified/Multilingual/Pro，8 种语言，每仓库取时间跨度最远的三个 commit）上，RepoLaunch 构建成功率 78.9%、管理成功率 89.2%、验证成功率 91.6%、Manage&Val 81.7%，全面优于通用 SWE-agent（构建 62.5%、Manage&Val 50.5%）；与 repo2run（Python 59.5%）、CXXCrafter（C/C++ 68.8%）、pipreqs（Python 40.5%）、railpack（各语言 20–44%）相比取得 SOTA，声明较此前 Python/Linux-only 方案提升 18%。构建成本约 0.65 美元/实例（Python），低于 OpenSWE（19.1 美元），成功率远高于 swe-rebench（20–30%）。
（2）数据集规模：SWE-bench-Live/MultiLang 含 C/C++、C#、Java、Go、JS/TS、Rust 共 1077 个实例、431 个仓库，超过原 SWE-bench-Multilingual 的 300 实例/41 仓库；Windows 专属任务 600 个 issue 采样、最终 65+ 实例。各语言构建成功率约 70–92%（JS/TS 89.0%、C# 92.0%、C/C++ 82.4%、Java 78.0%、Go 72.3%、Rust 72.1%、Windows 70.5%）；整体平均每实例成本约 1.7 美元，C/C++ 最贵（2.68 美元），"make"/"go test -v" 类简单仓库约 0.15 美元但仍有约三分之一出现构建或测试错误，故坚持统一智能体方案而非规则化。
（3）跨平台迁移性：Android 92%（23/25，构建 92%、管理 100%）、嵌入式系统 88%（22/25，管理 100%）；因 Apple 政策未发布带 CLI 的 macOS/iOS 容器，未能验证。
（4）失败模式分析：对 100+100 条失败轨迹人工标注并用 GPT-5.2-Thinking-Medium 扩展标注（与人工比例偏差 ≤20%）。Build 阶段主因是命令超时/OOM（C/C++ 61.2%、Go 36.7%、Windows 20.4%，源于 30 分钟超时限制与仓库过大），其次是回归测试执行失败、依赖解析失败、构建/编译失败、基础镜像选择错误等；Management 阶段主因是测试日志解析失败（多数语言 40–64%）、无法逐测试输出状态、无法在 30 步内得出重构建命令。
（5）消融：语言专属 prompt、语言专属镜像、WebSearch 工具、字符串替换式文件编辑工具均带来显著增益，且在 Windows 上更明显（构建 60.5%→68.0%）；去掉语言专属镜像时 Linux 构建率降至 67.1%、Windows 降至 46.0%，平均步数显著上升；语言专属 prompt 主要改善 Management 阶段（引导输出 JSON/XML 结构化结果）。
（6）骨干模型鲁棒性：以 Claude-Sonnet-5 驱动可达 90.8% 构建、92.0% Manage&Val，显著优于同模型下的 SWE-agent（81.6%/64.5%）与 repo2run（69.0%）；换 Deepseek-v4-Pro、GPT-5-Medium 仍保持领先。
（7）下游评测：在 SWE-bench-Live/MultiLang & Windows 上评测 SWE-agent、OpenHands、ClaudeCode 及 Win-agent（作者实现的首个可在 Windows 容器运行的编码智能体）与 GPT-5.5/5.2、Claude-4.5-Sonnet、Gemini-3-flash、Deepseek-v4-Pro/v3.1-terminus。智能体维度 SWE-agent 29.6%、ClaudeCode 29.2%、OpenHands 27.9%；模型维度 GPT-5.5 35.8% 最高（相较旧模型在 Python 子集约 15% 明显提升），Deepseek-v4 30.0%、Claude-4.5 28.5%、GPT-5.2 25.1%、Gemini-3 24.2%、Deepseek-3.1 20.6%；LM 间差距远大于 agent 间差距；无单一模型/智能体全能，Java 最易（最佳组合 50.0%）、Rust 最难；Windows 上所有模型成功率均下降，Claude 相对稳健（30.0%）。编码智能体失败模式上，定位类失败（文件/行定位）为主因，"编辑-失败-再编辑"循环已从 GPT-4 时代的 23.4% 降至 4% 以下，达到最大步数的仅 5.41%。

Conclusion: RepoLaunch 首次实现了跨任意编程语言与任意操作系统的仓库自动构建与测试状态管理智能体：形式化了"仓库构建与管理"问题，给出可扩展的多智能体框架与系统性的失败模式分类，并在 9 种语言、Windows/Android/嵌入式平台上达到约 80% 的构建成功率与 91.6% 的验证成功率，超越所有规则式与既有智能体式基线，且 API 成本可控（开源工具已被广泛采用，月克隆量 2k+，并被 SWE-bench-Live、GLM-5、OS-bench 等后续工作用于自动生成任务）。基于它实现了"仅在任务设计阶段需要人工"的 SWE 数据集全自动构建流水线，产出首个多语言、多平台、可持续更新的 SWE-bench-Live/MultiLang 与 /Windows 数据集，为编码智能体与 LM 的评测与训练提供规模化基础设施，并暴露出新的评测洞察（LM 差距大于 agent 差距、Java 最易而 Rust 最难、Windows 普遍更难）。
局限与未来方向：多轮自由智能体 token 成本高，未来可先用硬编码解析器覆盖常见测试框架以减少 LM 调用；基于规则+LM 的 GitHub 任务筛选可能存在偏差（LM 校验精度约 92%），仍待更好方案；算力受限导致当前仅 1077+65 个实例，后续将持续扩充。作者建议进一步引入数据库检索构建 agent 记忆（经验学习），以及基于 launch 结果做拒绝采样微调/强化学习，并开放更多 agentic 工具与轨迹收集接口。

Abstract: Building software repositories typically requires significant manual effort. Recent advances in large language model (LLM) agents have accelerated automation in software engineering (SWE). We introduce RepoLaunch, the first agent capable of automatically resolving dependencies, compiling source code, and extracting test results for repositories across arbitrary programming languages and operating systems. To demonstrate its utility, we further propose a fully automated pipeline for SWE dataset creation, where task design is the only human intervention. RepoLaunch automates the remaining steps, enabling scalable benchmarking and training of coding agents and LLMs. Notably, several works on agentic benchmarking and training have recently adopted RepoLaunch for automated task generation.

</details>


### [76] [Behaviour Driven Development Scenario Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04729)
*Amila Rathnayake,Mojtaba Shahin,Golnoush Abaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了GPT-4、Claude 3和Gemini三个大语言模型在自动化生成行为驱动开发(BDD)场景方面的表现。研究构建了包含500个样本的数据集，采用文本相似度、语义相似度、LLM评估和人类专家评估相结合的多维框架。结果表明：虽然GPT-4在相似度指标上领先，但Claude 3在实际质量评估中表现最佳；LLM评估器(尤其是DeepSeek)比传统相似度指标更接近人类判断；提示词策略需要根据模型选择；输入质量特别是详细的需求描述是生成高质量场景的决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为驱动开发(BDD)场景的手动编写耗时费力，需要评估当前主流LLM在自动化BDD场景生成任务中的能力表现，以确定最佳模型和最优提示策略。

Method: 构建了包含500个用户故事、需求描述及对应BDD场景的数据集（来自4个专有软件产品）；采用多维评估框架结合文本/语义相似度指标、LLM评估和人类专家评估三种方法；针对不同模型测试了零样本、思维链和少样本三种提示词技术；比较了不同temperature和top_p参数设置的效果。

Result: 1) Claude 3生成的BDD场景在人类专家和LLM评估中获得最高评分；2) DeepSeek评估器与人类判断的相关性优于文本/语义相似度指标；3) 提示词技术效果具有模型特异性：GPT-4适合零样本、Claude 3适合思维链、Gemini适合少样本；4) 详细需求描述是生成高质量场景的关键，用户故事单独使用效果差；5) temperature=0和top_p=1.0是所有模型的最佳参数设置。

Conclusion: GPT-4在文本和语义相似度指标上表现最好，但Claude 3生成的BDD场景在人类专家和LLM评估器中获得了最高评价。DeepSeek作为LLM评估器与人类判断的相关性最强。提示词技术效果具有模型特异性，最佳配置为temperature=0和top_p=1.0。输入质量是决定BDD场景生成效果的关键因素，详细的需求描述是生成高质量场景的必要条件。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of three LLMs, GPT-4, Claude 3, and Gemini, for automated Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) scenarios generation. To support this evaluation, we constructed a dataset of 500 user stories, requirement descriptions, and their corresponding BDD scenarios, drawn from four proprietary software products. We assessed the quality of BDD scenarios generated by LLMs using a multidimensional evaluation framework encompassing text and semantic similarity metrics, LLM-based evaluation, and human expert assessment. Our findings reveal that although GPT-4 achieves higher scores in text and semantic similarity metrics, Claude 3 produces scenarios rated highest by both human experts and LLM-based evaluators. LLM-based evaluators, particularly DeepSeek, show a stronger correlation with human judgment than with text similarity and semantic similarity metrics. The effectiveness of prompting techniques is model-specific: GPT-4 performs best with zero-shot, Claude 3 benefits from chain-of-thought reasoning, and Gemini achieves optimal results with few-shot examples. Input quality determines the effectiveness of BDD scenario generation: detailed requirement descriptions alone yield high-quality scenarios, whereas user stories alone yield low-quality scenarios. Our experiments indicate that setting temperature to 0 and top_p to 1.0 produced the highest-quality BDD scenarios across all models.

</details>


### [77] [Public Sector Open Source Program Offices - Archetypes for how to Grow (Common) Institutional Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04891)
*Johan Linåker,Astor Nummelin Carlberg,Ciaran O'Riordan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了欧洲公共部门组织中开源项目办公室(OSPOs)的角色，通过对16个公共部门OSPO案例的访谈调查，识别出六种OSPO原型，并为公共部门组织和决策者提供关于利用开源软件实现战略目标、促进数字主权和改善数字服务的实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件(OSS)是支撑工业和公共数字服务数字基础设施的关键组成部分（超过90%），对欧洲公共部门具有重要意义。欧盟通过多项部长声明强调开源软件加速数字化转型、变革企业和培养数字技能人才的潜力。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对欧盟、挪威、列支敦士登和冰岛16个公共部门OSPO案例中的18名OSPO代表进行访谈调查。将这些案例交叉分析并归纳为六种OSPO原型。通过两个跟进焦点小组验证和丰富发现，参与者包括早期受访者和额外专家。

Result: 研究识别出六种不同的OSPO原型，揭示了其组织结构、责任和对OSS采用的贡献。这六种原型连同政策建议，为PSOs设计自身OSPOs提供了指导，考虑其特定背景、资源和政策目标。

Conclusion: 研究结果增强了对OSPO作为促进OSS采用的战略举措的理解。PSOs和决策者可获得关于利用OSS实现战略目标、促进数字主权、推动经济增长以及提高数字服务互操作性和质量的实践指导。

Abstract: Context: Open Source Software (OSS) is a crucial component of over 90\% of digital infrastructure underpinning industry and public digital services, facilitating collaborative software development and dissemination. Its significance in the European public sector has been emphasised through various Ministerial Declarations, highlighting its potential to accelerate digitalisation, transform businesses, and foster a digitally skilled population. Research Aim: This study aims to explore how the adoption, development, and collaboration on OSS can be enabled through organisational support functions or centres of competency, also known as Open Source Programme Offices (OSPOs) within Public Sector Organisations (PSOs) in the European Union, Norway, Liechtenstein, and Iceland. Methodology: A qualitative research approach was adopted, involving an interview survey of 18 OSPO representatives across 16 cases of public-sector OSPOs. These cases were cross-analysed and categorised into six OSPO archetypes. The findings were validated and enriched through two follow-up focus groups that included earlier interviewees and additional experts. Results: The study identified six distinct OSPO archetypes, providing insights into their organisational structures, responsibilities, and contributions to OSS adoption. The archetypes, along with policy recommendations, offer guidance on how PSOs can design their own OSPOs, taking into account their specific context, resources, and policy goals. Conclusions: The findings enhance the understanding of OSPOs as strategic endeavours aimed at promoting OSS adoption. The study offers practical guidance for PSOs and policymakers on leveraging OSS to achieve strategic objectives, foster digital sovereignty, drive economic growth, and improve the interoperability and quality of digital services.

</details>


### [78] [Why Do You Contribute to Stack Overflow? Understanding Cross-Cultural Motivations and Usage Patterns before the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05043)
*Sherlock A. Licorish,Elijah Zolduoarrati,Tony Savarimuthu,Rashina Hoda,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Pankajeshwara Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究 Stack Overflow 贡献者动机，探讨美国、中国和俄罗斯的区域差异及其与平台活动的关系。发现文化差异显著影响贡献者动机和行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LLM 时代，理解社区问答平台贡献者的参与动机对于维持知识共享生态系统至关重要。现有关于贡献者动机如何因不同国家文化而异的见解有限。

Method: 混合方法研究：结合 Stack Overflow 个人资料的定性内容分析与美国、中国和俄罗斯数据的定量语言分析。使用演绎内容分析识别 17 个动机类别，并应用相关分析识别动机与平台活动之间的关联。

Result: 识别出 17 个动机类别。贡献者的主要动机是广告机会和利他性问题解决。美国贡献者表现出更强的自我推广行为，中国贡献者表现出更强的学习导向参与。详细个人资料与广告和社交活动相关，而学习导向用户保持 minimal 自我呈现。

Conclusion: 研究发现不同文化背景的贡献者存在显著动机差异，美国贡献者更注重自我推广，中国贡献者更注重学习。详细个人资料与广告和社交活动相关，而学习导向用户倾向于保持 minimal 自我呈现。研究结果可为提升软件工程领域的跨文化参与提供策略指导。

Abstract: Understanding motivations of contributors for participating in community question and answer platforms is crucial for sustaining knowledge-sharing ecosystem, which is necessary to advance the discipline while also ensuring its longevity. This is particularly necessary in the age of LLMs, where data from such portals are used to train these models. Limited insights exist regarding how motivations of contributors vary across different national cultures. This research investigates Stack Overflow contributor motivations, analysing regional differences and relations to platform activity. We combined qualitative content analysis of Stack Overflow profiles with quantitative linguistic analysis of data from the United States, China, and Russia. Using deductive content analysis, we identified 17 motivational categories. We applied correlation analysis to identify associations between stated motivations and platform activities. Contributors are primarily motivated by advertising opportunities and altruistic problem solving desires. American contributors demonstrated stronger self promotional behaviours while Chinese contributors exhibited greater learning oriented engagement. Our correlation analysis showed that those with more detailed profiles tend to engage in advertising and social activities, while learning oriented users maintain minimal self presentation. Understanding these variations can inform strategies for enhancing cross cultural participation in software engineering.

</details>


### [79] [A Benchmarking Framework for Model Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05250)
*Philipp-Lorenz Glaser,Lola Burgueño,Dominik Bork*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对模型驱动工程(MDE)软件模型数据集的基准测试框架，用于系统评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集的质量、代表性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型驱动工程中的实证研究和基于LLM的研究依赖软件模型数据集，但这些数据集通常是临时创建的，缺乏质量保证，导致研究结果难以比较、数据集质量和代表性不明确、 reproducibility差且可能存在偏见。

Method: 提出一个MDE基准测试平台(Benchmark Platform for MDE)，提供统一的基础设施，使用定义的标准和指标系统地评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集。

Result: 建立了系统评估数据集质量、代表性和任务适用性的基准测试框架，为MDE领域提供了标准化的数据集评估基础设施。

Conclusion: 该基准测试框架有望提高MDE研究中数据集的可比性、透明度和可重复性，促进该领域的科学进步。

Abstract: Empirical and LLM-based research in model-driven engineering increasingly relies on datasets of software models, for instance, to train or evaluate machine learning techniques for modeling support. These datasets have a significant impact on solution performance; hence, they should be treated and assessed as first-class artifacts. However, such datasets are typically collected or created ad hoc and without guarantees of their quality for the specific task for which they are used. This limits the comparability of results between studies, obscures dataset quality and representativeness, and leads to weak reproducibility and potential bias. In this work, we propose a benchmarking framework for model datasets (i.e., benchmarking the dataset itself). Benchmarking datasets involves systematically measuring their quality, representativeness, and suitability for specific tasks. To this end, we propose a Benchmark Platform for MDE that provides a unified infrastructure for systematically assessing and comparing datasets of software models across languages and formats, using defined criteria and metrics.

</details>


### [80] [A framework for assessing the capabilities of code generation of constraint domain-specific languages with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05278)
*David Delgado,Lola Burgueño,Robert Clarisó*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）从文本规范生成领域特定语言（DSL）代码的能力，重点关注约束语言（OCL和Alloy），并与Python进行对比。实验表明LLM在Python上表现更好，较小的开源模型可能无法生成约束代码，而代码修复和多轮尝试可改善生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在支持软件开发任务（如代码补全和代码生成）方面表现出色，但在处理不太流行的编程语言（如领域特定语言DSL）时，其有效性显著下降。需要一个系统性的评估框架来分析LLM生成DSL代码的能力。

Method: 提出了一个通用评估框架，从格式正确性（well-formedness）和功能正确性（correctness）两个角度评估生成的代码。实验聚焦于约束语言OCL和Alloy，并与通用编程语言Python进行对比。评估了不同LLM（包括开源小模型）的表现，并分析了代码修复、多轮尝试、提示模板等因素的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）LLM在Python上的表现总体优于OCL和Alloy；2）较小上下文窗口的开源LLM可能无法生成约束代码，因为需要同时管理约束和领域模型；3）代码修复（让LLM修复错误代码）和多轮尝试（生成多个候选）可以改善生成质量；4）提示模板的选择影响较小。

Conclusion: 该评估框架可以系统性地分析代码生成的各个维度，帮助确定针对特定任务设置代码生成的最佳方式。研究为LLM在DSL代码生成领域的应用提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be used to support software development tasks, e.g., through code completion or code generation. However, their effectiveness drops significantly when considering less popular programming languages such as domain-specific languages (DSLs). In this paper, we propose a generic framework for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs generating DSL code from textual specifications. The generated code is assessed from the perspectives of well-formedness and correctness. This framework is applied to a particular type of DSL, constraint languages, focusing our experiments on OCL and Alloy and comparing their results to those achieved for Python, a popular general-purpose programming language. Experimental results show that, in general, LLMs have better performance for Python than for OCL and Alloy. LLMs with smaller context windows such as open-source LLMs may be unable to generate constraint-related code, as this requires managing both the constraint and the domain model where it is defined. Moreover, some improvements to the code generation process such as code repair (asking an LLM to fix incorrect code) or multiple attempts (generating several candidates for each coding task) can improve the quality of the generated code. Meanwhile, other decisions like the choice of a prompt template have less impact. All these dimensions can be systematically analyzed using our evaluation framework, making it possible to decide the most effective way to set up code generation for a particular type of task.

</details>

### [81] [Decorrelating the Future: Joint Frequency Domain Learning for Spatio-temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04418)
*Zepu Wang,Bowen Liao,Jeff,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FreST Loss，一种基于频谱的时空训练目标，利用联合傅里叶变换(JFT)将对时空图信号的监督扩展到联合时空频谱域，以更好地捕获复杂的时空依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接预测模型通常使用均方误差等点-wise目标函数，无法有效捕获图结构信号中复杂的时空依赖性。虽已有频域方法如FreDF缓解时间自相关问题，但往往忽略了空间维度和跨时空交互作用。

Method: 提出FreST Loss频率增强时空损失函数，通过联合傅里叶变换(JFT)将模型预测与真实值对齐到统一的频谱域，实现对空间和时间维度的联合解相关处理。

Result: 在6个真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，FreST Loss是模型无关的，能够持续提升基线方法的性能，更好地捕获整体时空动态。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明该方法降低了时域训练目标的估计偏差。FreST Loss为图结构时空预测提供了一种有效的频谱训练范式，具有良好的通用性和应用价值。

Abstract: Standard direct forecasting models typically rely on point-wise objectives such as Mean Squared Error, which fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies inherent in graph-structured signals. While recent frequency-domain approaches such as FreDF mitigate temporal autocorrelation, they often overlook spatial and cross spatio-temporal interactions. To address this limitation, we propose FreST Loss, a frequency-enhanced spatio-temporal training objective that extends supervision to the joint spatio-temporal spectrum. By leveraging the Joint Fourier Transform (JFT), FreST Loss aligns model predictions with ground truth in a unified spectral domain, effectively decorrelating complex dependencies across both space and time. Theoretical analysis shows that this formulation reduces estimation bias associated with time-domain training objectives. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that FreST Loss is model-agnostic and consistently improves state-of-the-art baselines by better capturing holistic spatio-temporal dynamics.

</details>


### [82] [Machine Learning for Complex Systems Dynamics: Detecting Bifurcations in Dynamical Systems with Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04420)
*Swadesh Pal,Roderick Melnik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平衡态知情神经网络"（EINNs）：与传统"固定参数、正向求解平衡点"的做法相反，将候选平衡态作为DNN输入，反向推断使其成为平衡点的参数值。通过学习到的参数景观（对u*的曲线）的局部极值（dλ/du*=0），即可定位鞍结分岔对应的临界转变阈值（CTCS），并构造分岔图。在生态双稳态模型（α-βu+rg(u)）、May (1977) 模型及Aβ–Ca²⁺神经退行性疾病模型上，EINNs给出的分岔结构与线性稳定性分析/传统数值扫描结果高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界转变（tipping points）在生态、气候、生物医学与经济系统中普遍存在，识别其阈值对早期预警和韧性管理至关重要。传统方法（线性稳定性分析、延拓法/continuation、大规模参数扫描与前向积分）需要细粒度采样参数空间并反复求解整个系统，在高维、强非线性、存在多平衡（含多稳态）的模型中计算代价高昂，难以实时或规模化应用；而已有基于DNN的工作多面向时序数据或特定离散分岔分类器，未直接解决"如何系统性地枚举平衡点并定位分岔"的问题。此外，正向的DNN解平衡点做法受输出节点数限制，容易出现重复解或漏解，难以覆盖同一参数值下的多个平衡态。

Method: 核心是"反向（反演）范式"：给定N个候选平衡态采样点{u*_j}（如u*∈[0,1.5]），训练深度前馈网络（tanh激活、4个隐层各10节点、Adam、lr=1e-3、MSE阈值2e-10）输出对应参数λ_j，损失为方程残差均方MSE=(1/N)Σ|f(u*_j;λ_j)|²。对两方程模型，输入u*，网络输出λ与v，并采用对v的min残差目标MSE=(1/N)Σ min_v{|f|²+|g|²}，再取极小点argmin恢复v*；n方程系统同样推广为对u_2..u_n的min残差形式MSE=(1/N)Σ min{Σ_k|f_k|²}。关键技巧是利用自动微分（PyTorch）计算dλ/du*，其零点的局部极大/极小即候选临界阈值；物理可行性通过丢弃负预测参数保证。验证方式为与传统参数扫描分岔图、线性稳定性分析对比。

Result: (1) 生态模型 du/dt=α-βu+r·u^p/(u^p+h^p)：EINNs预测的局部极值给出r₁=1.7869、r₂=2.6049，与传统扫描得到的两个鞍结分岔点（生成与湮灭一对稳定/鞍点）一致，并正确再现中间区间三平衡点的多稳态区间；(2) May (1977) 模型以β为分岔参数，EINNs定位两个临界阈值β₁、β₂，区间内三平衡、区间外唯一平衡，与线性稳定性分析一致；(3) Aβ–Ca²⁺双变量模型（以a₂为分岔参数，ε不影响平衡点位置）中，EINN分岔图与传统方法在定性特征与临界转变位置上强一致。总体表明该方法可在不依赖穷举模拟的情况下恢复即将发生转变的参数区域。

Conclusion: EINNs以数据驱动方式学习"平衡态→参数"的逆映射，绕开反复调用数值求解器，能在高维、隐式或解析难解的系统中系统性地绘制分岔图、恢复多稳态并精确捕捉临界阈值，具有作为通用平衡态推断/逆向设计工具的潜力（生态、生物系统的反应率推断、材料与边界参数的逆向设计、神经科学与参数辨识等）。局限与未来方向：对候选平衡态的采样策略和输入范围敏感（范围过宽会失效），训练数据之外的区域转变无法检出，需仔细设计网络与训练以避免过拟合、梯度消失或陷入局部极小；作者亦明确指出该方法不检测时序上的突变，也未计算与"临界慢化"相关的早期预警指标。作者提出将ML与ODE模型结合用于实时监测与预测，并继续探索更复杂系统中的转变类型与稳定性分类。

Abstract: Critical transitions are the abrupt shifts between qualitatively different states of a system, and they are crucial to understanding tipping points in complex dynamical systems across ecology, climate science, and biology. Detecting these shifts typically involves extensive forward simulations or bifurcation analyses, which are often computationally intensive and limited by parameter sampling. In this study, we propose a novel machine learning approach based on deep neural networks (DNNs) called equilibrium-informed neural networks (EINNs) to identify critical thresholds associated with catastrophic regime shifts. Rather than fixing parameters and searching for solutions, the EINN method reverses this process by using candidate equilibrium states as inputs and training a DNN to infer the corresponding system parameters that satisfy the equilibrium condition. By analyzing the learned parameter landscape and observing abrupt changes in the feasibility or continuity of equilibrium mappings, critical thresholds can be effectively detected. We demonstrate this capability on nonlinear systems exhibiting saddle-node bifurcations and multi-stability, showing that EINNs can recover the parameter regions associated with impending transitions. This method provides a flexible alternative to traditional techniques, offering new insights into the early detection and structure of critical shifts in high-dimensional and nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [83] [FedEMA-Distill: Exponential Moving Average Guided Knowledge Distillation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04422)
*Hamza Reguieg,Mohamed El Kamili,Essaid Sabir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedEMA-Distill 是一种纯服务端实现的联邦学习协议：客户端只上传在小型公开代理数据集上的 FP16 量化 logits（而非模型权重），服务端先做集成知识蒸馏（可选坐标级中位数/截尾均值等鲁棒聚合以抵御拜占庭客户端），再对全局权重施加指数滑动平均（EMA）以平滑轨迹。结果：在 CIFAR-10/100、FEMNIST、AG News 的 Dirichlet-0.1 强标签偏斜下，CIFAR-10 精度 80.4%（较基线最高 +5%）、CIFAR-100 63.0%（+6%），达到 70% 精度所需轮数减少 30–35%，每客户端每轮上行仅 0.09–0.46 MB，达到 70% 累计上行约 3.6 MB（相比 FedAvg 的 228 MB 降低约 63 倍），并在 25% 拜占庭攻击下保持 78% 精度，ECE 更低、客户端间更公平、设备能耗显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在真实部署中面临两大同时出现的挑战：(1) 客户端数据 non-IID 异质导致客户端漂移、收敛慢/振荡；(2) 上行带宽受限，频繁传输数十 MB 的模型权重不可行。现有两条技术路线各有所长但存在缺口：FedProx/SCAFFOLD/FedOpt(FedAvgM、FedAdam)/FedDyn 等"减漂移"方法提供时间稳定性，但仍需交换完整权重、无法支持异构架构；FedMD/FedDistill/FedDF/FedBE 等"输出级蒸馏"方法通信高效且天然支持模型异构，但在强标签偏斜下教师信号存在轮间方差大、时间不一致的问题，全局模型每轮从当前状态重训易振荡。因此需要一种同时解决稳定性与通信效率、并且能在客户端侧零改动的方案。此外，本文还关注拜占庭鲁棒性、校准、公平性与边缘设备能耗。
