Result: 理论：预期退化 μ_S 与各传感器停机率 (1-π_{x,i})、策略平滑度 L_π、critic 敏感度 L_Q 线性相关，传感器间相关性不直接进入均值项（仅通过边缘概率）；偏差项随混合时间 τ、有效视野 γ 与置信度 δ 增大而变宽，C_max = L_Q L_π ΣB_i 为整体缩放因子，故更快恢复（更大 π_x）、更快混合（更大谱隙）即更鲁棒。实验：全可观测下 MLP 往往取得最高回报，序列模型无稳定优势（复杂环境下更差），说明时序上下文在观测充足时是负担；60% 部分可观测下所有模型均退化，但 MLP 下降最剧烈（Hopper、Walker2d 尤为明显），GRU/LRU/GTrXL 等隐状态记忆模型鲁棒性仍差、低回报尾部更重，Transformer 策略在全部环境取得最高评估中位数且性能最稳定，UniTS 因“逐变量独立做序列注意力、跨变量交互延后”的归纳偏置失配而在所有设置下最差；Ant 在部分可观测下最难，序列模型难以把记忆迁移到高维状态。作者据此解释：无状态 Transformer 的自注意力可联合处理所有变量、动态跳过缺失片段直接检索相关历史，而循环动态依赖对输入均匀性与规则性假设在缺失数据下易失稳或丢失关键信息。

Conclusion: 时序序列建模（尤其是无状态自注意力的 Transformer）是在观测漂移/部分可观测条件下提升 PPO 鲁棒性的有原则且实用的机制：序列模型在完全可观测下收益有限甚至为负，但在严重传感器失效下能显著保持回报，其中 Transformer 全面优于 MLP、RNN/SSM/GTrXL/UniTS。理论上界说明鲁棒性由策略平滑性、critic 敏感度、传感器可用率与失效持久性共同决定，可据此为实际系统设计观测可靠性预算与架构选择。局限与后续方向：理论依赖掩码外生独立、增强链几何遍历等较强假设，且给出的是保守上界，尚未刻画序列模型相对 MLP 的结构性优势来源，也未显式建模时间注意力对缺失数据的容错机制；实验仅限 MuJoCo 连续控制，缺少真实机器人/自动驾驶验证，失效模型为参数化马尔可夫模型，可扩展到更复杂的非马尔可夫/空间相关失效与真实漂移场景。

Abstract: Real-world reinforcement learning systems must operate under distributional drift in their observation streams, yet most policy architectures implicitly assume fully observed and noise-free states. We study robustness of Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) under temporally persistent sensor failures that induce partial observability and representation shift. To respond to this drift, we augment PPO with temporal sequence models, including Transformers and State Space Models (SSMs), to enable policies to infer missing information from history and maintain performance. Under a stochastic sensor failure process, we prove a high-probability bound on infinite-horizon reward degradation that quantifies how robustness depends on policy smoothness and failure persistence. Empirically, on MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks with severe sensor dropout, we show Transformer-based sequence policies substantially outperform MLP, RNN, and SSM baselines in robustness, maintaining high returns even when large fractions of sensors are unavailable. These results demonstrate that temporal sequence reasoning provides a principled and practical mechanism for reliable operation under observation drift caused by sensor unreliability.

</details>


### [111] [Neuro-Symbolic Financial Reasoning via Deterministic Fact Ledgers and Adversarial Low-Latency Hallucination Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04663)
*Pedram Agand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VeNRA通过Universal Fact Ledger (UFL)实现确定性变量检索，结合Double-Lock Grounding算法提供数学边界约束，并训练3B参数的Sentinel模型利用Adversarial Simulation审计Python执行轨迹，解决标准RAG在金融领域因LLM算术能力不足和语义混淆导致的99%准确率等于0%信任的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG架构在高风险金融领域存在两个根本性局限：1）LLM固有的算术能力缺陷；2）密集向量检索的语义混淆（如将"Net Income"误映射为"Net Sales"）。在确定性领域，99%准确率意味着0%运营信任，因此需要实现零幻觉金融推理。

Method: 1）提出严格类型化的Universal Fact Ledger (UFL)，通过Double-Lock Grounding算法提供数学边界约束，将RAG从概率文本检索转向确定性变量检索；2）训练VeNRA Sentinel——3B参数的SLM，仅用1个token测试预算审计Python执行轨迹；3）采用Adversarial Simulation而非传统生成式幻觉数据集，通过程序化破坏黄金金融记录模拟"生态错误"（逻辑代码谎言、数值邻居陷阱）；4）使用单次分类范式优化延迟，结合可选后置思考用于调试；5）提出OOM-safe的Micro-Chunking loss算法解决Reverse-CoT训练中的Loss Dilution问题。

Result: 实现零幻觉金融推理，通过确定性变量检索和数学边界约束确保推理可靠性，Sentinel模型能够高效审计Python执行轨迹检测生产级生态错误。

Conclusion: VeNRA成功将RAG范式从概率文本检索转变为确定性变量检索，通过UFL、Double-Lock Grounding、Adversarial Simulation训练和Micro-Chunking loss等创新技术，解决了高风险金融领域的RAG失效问题，为合规性要求严格的金融应用提供了可验证推理的可行路径。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architectures fail in high-stakes financial domains due to two fundamental limitations: the inherent arithmetic incompetence of Large Language Models (LLMs) and the distributional semantic conflation of dense vector retrieval (e.g., mapping ``Net Income'' to ``Net Sales'' due to contextual proximity). In deterministic domains, a 99% accuracy rate yields 0% operational trust. To achieve zero-hallucination financial reasoning, we introduce the Verifiable Numerical Reasoning Agent (VeNRA). VeNRA shifts the RAG paradigm from retrieving probabilistic text to retrieving deterministic variables via a strictly typed Universal Fact Ledger (UFL), mathematically bounded by a novel Double-Lock Grounding algorithm. Recognizing that upstream parsing anomalies inevitably occur, we introduce the VeNRA Sentinel: a 3-billion parameter SLM trained to forensically audit Python execution traces with only one token test budget. To train this model, we avoid traditional generative hallucination datasets in favor of Adversarial Simulation, programmatically sabotaging golden financial records to simulate production-level ``Ecological Errors'' (e.g., Logic Code Lies and Numeric Neighbor Traps). Finally, to optimize the Sentinel under strict latency budgets, we utilize a single-pass classification paradigm with optional post thinking for debug. We identify the phenomenon of Loss Dilution in Reverse-Chain-of-Thought training and present a novel, OOM-safe Micro-Chunking loss algorithm to stabilize gradients under extreme differential penalization.

</details>


### [112] [Direct Estimation of Tree Volume and Aboveground Biomass Using Deep Regression with Synthetic Lidar Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04683)
*Habib Pourdelan,Zhengkang Xiang,Hugh Stewart,Cam Nicholson,Martin Tomko,Kourosh Khoshelham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"直接估计"范式：绕过单木分割与异速生长方程（allometry）这两个误差累积环节，用合成激光雷达点云训练点云深度回归网络（PointNet/PointNet++/DGCNN/PointConv），端到端地回归样地级材积，再按树种木材密度换算地上生物量（AGB）与碳储量。在澳大利亚维多利亚州两个农场（5个样地、293个瓦片）的ULS数据上验证：PointNet++（最远点采样FPS）相对实测值的偏差仅2%–20%，而单木分割+异速方程基线（TreeLearn 27%–55%、SegmentAnyTree 47%–72%、ForestFormer3D 60%–81%、CHM分割 64%–77%）及FullCAM（70%–85%）均严重低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林地上生物量（AGB）与碳储量估算是碳信用、碳市场和减缓气候策略的基础。现行主流做法是"间接链路"：先从激光雷达点云中分割单木、提取胸径/树高等生物物理参数，再用异速生长方程换算AGB并逐木汇总。该链路存在两类叠加误差：(1) 实例分割在树冠交叠、多层郁闭、遮挡、点密度变化下普遍存在欠分割/过分割；(2) 异速方程本身是基于有限破坏性样本拟合的经验近似，随树种组、立地条件、树形与测量噪声而失效。同时，直接用深度学习回归AGB又受制于缺乏大规模、高质量、带真值的实测数据（破坏性取样昂贵、样地小、时空覆盖有限）。因此需要一种既避免间接链误差、又不依赖大规模实测真值的方法。

Method: 方法流程为"合成数据生成→深度回归训练→真实点云推理→生物量/碳换算→基线对比"。（1）合成数据：在Blender 3.6.2中用7个3D桉树模型构建1200个矩形合成林分样地（考虑密度、实际树高、树种特征），用Trimesh + PyMeshFix将树干、枝条等木质部件分离并修复为水密流形网格，逐部件计算体积并求和得到样地真值材积。（2）点云模拟：用HELIOS++ v1.3以ULS模式（Riegl VUX-1UAV22，飞行高度35 m、速度2 m/s、PRF 200 kHz、扫描频率80 Hz、180°扫描角、最多5次回波），按与实测Hovermap ST相近的点密度模拟扫描生成合成点云。（3）预处理：每个样地统一降采样至2048点，对比随机采样（torch-geometric FixedPoints）与最远点采样（FPS）两种策略；计算样地面积/体积、点密度（面/体）、平均点间距等指标；用绕z轴7个角度旋转将数据集扩至9600样地，并在每epoch前做抖动增强。（4）模型：将PointNet、PointNet++、DGCNN、PointConv的分类头改为回归头预测连续材积；PyTorch实现，NVIDIA A40 24GB，AdamW、初始学习率1e-3、权重衰减1e-4、批大小16、200 epochs、余弦退火重启调度，损失MSE、评价指标MAPE，5折交叉验证（7680训练/1920验证）。（5）推理：将真实点云按与合成样地同尺寸切分为瓦片，降采样至2048点后预测材积，按分瓦片求和得样地级材积，再用桉树木材密度（Young, 2000）换算AGB，碳按0.5系数换算。（6）基线：以SegmentAnyTree、TreeLearn、ForestFormer3D三种深度学习单木分割+高-径异速方程（Williams et al., 2005）、CHM标记控制分水岭分割+同一异速方程、以及FullCAM为对照，参考真值来自Paul et al. (2016)的桉树异速方程（仅用于生成参考值，不作为模型输入）。

Result: 合成数据上：PointNet++最佳（RS: 验证误差0.151±0.016 m³，MAPE 1.438±0.178%；FPS: 0.190±0.010 m³，1.797±0.149%），DGCNN次之（1.627%/1.691%），PointConv（1.705%/2.307%），PointNet最差（8.999%/8.111%）且训练-验证曲线波动大、泛化差；两种降采样在合成集上表现相当。降采样特征：FPS使样地平均面积更大、点密度更低（合成集6.69 vs 7.03 pts/m²）、平均点间距约大50%–60%，空间覆盖更广、局部聚集更少。真实数据上：FPS一致给出高于RS的AGB估计且更接近实测，说明随机采样会过度代表局部密集区而低估体积；PointNet++给出方差最小的稳定预测（如MF2007.2为181±1 t/ha）。对比实测（222/296/184/242/223 t/ha），PointNet++(FPS)为200/237/181/225/205 t/ha，偏差2%–20%；所有间接基线均系统性大幅低估：CHM分割64%–77%、FullCAM 70%–85%、TreeLearn 27%–55%、SegmentAnyTree 47%–72%、ForestFormer3D 60%–81%。PointNet++(FPS)可输出瓦片级AGB空间分布（2.8–7.3 t/瓦片），体现林分空间异质性。

Conclusion: 结论：合成点云+深度回归的"直接估计"路线可行且显著优于所有间接基线，是作者所知首个不依赖点云单木分割与异速方程的AGB估算方法；最远点采样是保证合成—真实迁移效果的关键预处理（保留空间完整性、减轻系统性低估）；模型无需大规模实测数据即可训练，具备可扩展性，用于样地级到更大尺度的森林碳监测/碳信用核算。局限：仅在合成数据上训练，存在域偏移（传感器噪声、多路径/光束发散、不完美地面归一化、多层郁闭遮挡、点密度差异），未做域适应；合成场景仅桉树、仅木质部（缺叶片与枝叶等非木质组分，导致系统性低估）；异速方程本身也用于生成实测参考值，参考并非无偏；单点架构PointNet稳定性差；瓦片化推理的边界效应与不确定性传播尚未量化。未来方向：引入少量真实点云做校准/域适应，在合成中显式模拟噪声与遮挡，扩展到多树种、含叶片、不同林龄与郁闭度，量化从材积到AGB/碳的不确定性传播。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of forest biomass is crucial for monitoring carbon sequestration and informing climate change mitigation strategies. Existing methods often rely on allometric models, which estimate individual tree biomass by relating it to measurable biophysical parameters, e.g., trunk diameter and height. This indirect approach is limited in accuracy due to measurement uncertainties and the inherently approximate nature of allometric equations, which may not fully account for the variability in tree characteristics and forest conditions. This study proposes a direct approach that leverages synthetic point cloud data to train a deep regression network, which is then applied to real point clouds for plot-level wood volume and aboveground biomass (AGB) estimation. We created synthetic 3D forest plots with ground truth volume, which were then converted into point cloud data using a lidar simulator. These point clouds were subsequently used to train deep regression networks based on PointNet, PointNet++, DGCNN, and PointConv. When applied to synthetic data, the deep regression networks achieved mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) values ranging from 1.69% to 8.11%. The trained networks were then applied to real lidar data to estimate volume and AGB. When compared against field measurements, our direct approach showed discrepancies of 2% to 20%. In contrast, indirect approaches based on individual tree segmentation followed by allometric conversion, as well as FullCAM, exhibited substantially large underestimation, with discrepancies ranging from 27% to 85%. Our results highlight the potential of integrating synthetic data with deep learning for efficient and scalable forest carbon estimation at plot level.

</details>


### [113] [Engineering Regression Without Real-Data Training: Domain Adaptation for Tabular Foundation Models Using Multi-Dataset Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04692)
*Lyle Regenwetter,Rosen Yu,Cyril Picard,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出TREBDench数据集和嵌入引导的合成数据筛选方法，解决工程领域表格基础模型训练中合成数据与真实数据分布差距大的问题。通过筛选类工程合成数据进行持续预训练，显著提升了TabPFN 2.5在工程回归任务上的性能和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程应用中的预测建模长期受限于定制化模型和小型孤立的表格数据集，限制了大规模学习方法的应用。现有的表格基础模型使用的合成训练分布可能无法反映工程数据的统计结构，导致在工程回归任务上的迁移效果不佳。

Method: 1) 构建TREBDench数据集，包含83个带有专家工程/非工程标签的真实表格回归数据集；2) 使用TabPFN 2.5的数据集级嵌入在公共表示空间中研究领域结构；3) 提出嵌入引导的合成数据筛选方法：生成并识别"类工程"合成数据集，仅使用筛选后的合成任务对TabPFN 2.5进行持续预训练。

Result: 1) 工程数据集与非工程数据集部分可区分，标准程序化生成的合成数据集与工程数据集高度可区分，揭示了显著的合成-真实领域差距；2) 在35个工程回归数据集上，仅使用合成数据的自适应方法提升了预测准确性和数据效率：比TabPFN 2.5胜出29/35，比AutoGluon胜出27/35；3) 平均乘性数据效率提升分别为1.75倍和4.44倍。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了原则性的合成数据筛选方法可以将程序化生成器转化为领域相关的"数据引擎"，使基础模型能够在数据稀缺的科学和工业领域得到改进。提出的嵌入引导合成数据筛选方法在工程回归任务上显著提升了TabPFN 2.5的预测准确性和数据效率，为数据收集困难的领域的基础模型训练提供了新思路。

Abstract: Predictive modeling in engineering applications has long been dominated by bespoke models and small, siloed tabular datasets, limiting the applicability of large-scale learning approaches. Despite recent progress in tabular foundation models, the resulting synthetic training distributions used for pre-training may not reflect the statistical structure of engineering data, limiting transfer to engineering regression. We introduce TREDBench, a curated collection of 83 real-world tabular regression datasets with expert engineering/non-engineering labels, and use TabPFN 2.5's dataset-level embedding to study domain structure in a common representation space. We find that engineering datasets are partially distinguishable from non-engineering datasets, while standard procedurally generated datasets are highly distinguishable from engineering datasets, revealing a substantial synthetic-real domain gap. To bridge this gap without training on real engineering samples, we propose an embedding-guided synthetic data curation method: we generate and identify "engineering-like" synthetic datasets, and perform continued pre-training of TabPFN 2.5 using only the selected synthetic tasks. Across 35 engineering regression datasets, this synthetic-only adaptation improves predictive accuracy and data efficiency, outperforming TabPFN 2.5 on 29/35 datasets and AutoGluon on 27/35, with mean multiplicative data-efficiency gains of 1.75x and 4.44x, respectively. More broadly, our results indicate that principled synthetic data curation can convert procedural generators into domain-relevant "data engines," enabling foundation models to improve in data-sparse scientific and industrial domains where real data collection is the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [114] [Implicit Bias and Loss of Plasticity in Matrix Completion: Depth Promotes Low-Rankness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04703)
*Baekrok Shin,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以深度矩阵分解（深度线性网络）做矩阵补全为"试金石"，指出深度诱导的隐式低秩偏置的真正机制是"耦合训练动力学"（coupled dynamics）：深度≥3时中间层参数被所有观测项共享，动力学几乎必然耦合，因而低秩偏置随深度增强、并可收敛到rank-1；同时从理论上解释了"塑性损失"（loss of plasticity）——深层模型因低秩偏置而免疫，而depth-2在解耦动力学下预训练后即使后续数据使动力学耦合，也无法再回到低秩解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过参数化网络存在大量全局极小点，梯度下降会偏好其中某些解（隐式偏置）。矩阵补全中的低秩偏置是最典型的研究对象，但已有理论几乎都局限于深度2的分解模型：Bai et al. (2024) 用"数据连通性"（data connectivity）说明只有观测二部图连通时才可能收敛到低秩解，而这一框架无法解释实验中深度≥3的网络在观测图不连通（如仅观测对角元的2×2例子）时依然收敛到rank-1的现象。Arora et al. (2019) 关于"奇异值间隙随深度扩大"的论证依赖奇异向量已稳定的假设，不够严格；Menon (2024) 明确把"深度分解在仅对角观测下是否收敛到低秩解"列为未解问题。此外，Kleinman et al. (2024) 在矩阵补全中观察到塑性损失（数据增多后warm-start反而更差），但缺少理论解释。作者由此提出三个问题：深度≥3与depth-2在低秩偏置上的本质差异；能否严格刻画深度如何强化低秩偏置；塑性损失的成因及深度与它的关系。

Method: 问题设定：目标矩阵 W*∈R^{d×d}，观测集 Ω；模型为深度L的线性网络 W_{L:1}=W_L⋯W_1（d_l≥d，无显式秩约束），损失为观测项MSE，训练用梯度流（无限小步长GD）。关键定义：Definition 2（耦合/解耦动力学）——若存在Ω的非空不交划分Ω_1,…,Ω_K(K≥2)使得不同划分中观测项的梯度内积⟨∇_θ w_ij(t), ∇_θ w_pq(t)⟩=0对所有t≥0，则为解耦，否则为耦合。（depth-2时耦合≈连通；depth≥3时因中间层共享而普遍耦合。）技术路线：(1) depth-2热身——对M_C（连通）与M_D（不连通）写出梯度流方程，说明耦合→行/列对齐→近rank-1，并给出显式的错位度上界（Theorem 3.1）；(2) 为可分析性设计确定性初始化族 W_l(0)=diag(α,α/m,…)+off-diag α/m（介于αJ_d与αI_d之间，m控制初始数值秩），在块对角观测 Ω_block^{(s,n)}（d=sn，各块内元素相等w*>0）下分析；(3) 用一个块结构矩阵族 M(a,b,c)=I_n⊗D(a,b)+(J_n−I_n)⊗O(c)（对加法、乘法封闭且可交换）证明梯度流保持该结构，从而把多层因子矩阵的耦合动力学约化为少数标量参数，得到收敛奇异值的闭式或隐式刻画；(4) 塑性损失部分：对depth-2在仅对角观测下预训练给出闭式解（双曲函数形式），再在加入一个非对角观测使动力学变为耦合后分析后训练轨迹，证明其进入"惰性训练"（lazy training）区间——损失指数衰减但参数几乎不动、秩无法降低。证明工具：正半定矩阵分解、Gronwall不等式、Weyl不等式、Jacobian Lipschitz性与Telgarsky (2021) 的引理。实验验证：2×2/3×3全部23种观测模式的有效秩；数值求解隐式方程与GD对照；高斯初始化1000次重复；含噪/不等值对角观测；SGD、动量GD、Adam、RMSProp、Adagrad；CIFAR-10/100上ResNet-18/34/50/101与VGG-11/13/16/19的权重矩阵有效秩；ReLU MLP的高斯vs恒等初始化对比；以及100×100秩-5矩阵上的预训练/后训练实验。

Result: 1) 耦合是低秩偏置的机制。depth-2下：连通观测（M_C）产生耦合动力学，Theorem 3.1给出错位比例上界 ‖a_i⊥(∞)‖²/‖a_i(∞)‖² ≤ ‖A(0)‖_F²(√(‖b_1(0)‖₂⁴+4w₁₁*²+4w₂₁*²)+‖b_1(0)‖₂²)/(2w_{i1}*²)，即小初始化→近rank-1；不连通观测（M_D）下两对(a_i,b_i)完全解耦，闭式解为diag(w₁₁*,w₂₂*)，恒为rank-2。
2) 深度≥3几乎必然耦合。Proposition B.1：L≥3且初始化为绝对连续分布时，对任意Ω（|Ω|≥2），跨观测项梯度内积几乎必然非零→动力学耦合；例外是αI_d初始化（各因子矩阵保持对角）或全零初始化，此时为解耦。
3) Proposition 3.2（块对角观测下耦合/解耦的精确分类）：L=2且m>1时恒解耦；L≥3且1<m<∞时耦合；L≥3且m=∞（αI_d）时解耦。
4) Theorem 3.3（收敛奇异值）：L=2解耦时σ₁=w*d(m+d−1)²/[(m+d−1)²+(n−1)(m−1)²]，σ_i=w*d(m−1)²/[同分母]，与α无关→rank-n解；L≥3耦合时σ₁与σ_i由隐式方程 σ₁^{(2−L)/L}−((w*d−σ₁)/(n−1))^{(2−L)/L}=C_{α,m,L,d} 决定，且为α相关；L≥3且m=∞时σ₁=σ_i=s w*（rank-n，full-rank于s=1）。
5) Corollary 3.4：对L≥3且有限m，α→0时 srank(W_{L:1}(∞))→1，即收敛到近rank-1；而depth-2的srank=[(m+d−1)⁴+(m−1)⁴(n−1)]/(m+d−1)⁴与α无关、m大时≈n。数值与实验（Figure 2、4–12）一致：深度越大、初始化尺度越小，低秩偏置越强；该结论在含噪、非等值对角观测、SGD/动量/Adam/RMSProp/Adagrad下均成立，并在ResNet/VGG上以权重矩阵有效秩随深度下降的形式得到实证。理论结果解决Menon (2024) 的公开问题。
6) 塑性损失（Theorem 4.2、4.3）：预训练仅用对角观测（解耦）时，depth-2收敛到高秩解（如A(T₁)=B(T₁)=diag(√w*)）；后训练加入一个非对角观测后，即使动力学变为耦合，损失以速率 e^{−2w*(t−T₁)} 指数衰减，srank(W(∞)) ≥ 1+exp(−8w₁₂*/w*)，且未观测项w₂₁(t)<0（与真rank-1解w₂₁*=w*²/w₁₂*>0矛盾）；Theorem 4.3给出一般d×d情形：若预训练后损失足够小（≤σ_min⁶/(1152dσ_max²)），则进入惰性训练区间，损失指数衰减而A(t)的稳定秩被下界 ((‖A(T₁)‖_F−σ_min/(4√(2d)))/(‖A(T₁)‖₂+σ_min/(4√(2d))))² 锁定；对秩-1几何衰减型真值（W*_ij=w* c^{|i−j|}, c=O(1/d)）得 srank ≥ ((4d−1)/(4√d+1))²。实验中warm-start的depth-2模型秩与误差显著高于cold-start，深层模型则几乎免疫塑性损失。作者同时指出与Kleinman et al. (2024)的差异：后者固定迭代次数测"相对重建损失"且发现深度越深越严重，本文训练至收敛，发现深层因低秩偏置而受益。

Conclusion: 深度在矩阵补全中充当"低秩偏置的放大器"，其内在原因是结构性的耦合动力学而非数据连通性：depth-2的耦合由观测模式（连通性）决定，depth≥3则由中间层参数共享而与观测模式无关、几乎必然发生；耦合动力学通过强制行/列对齐促成rank-1收敛，其强度随深度L与初始化尺度α共同控制（α→0时稳定秩→1）。据此，"收敛到rank-1当且仅当动力学耦合"这一充要刻画解答了Menon (2024)的开放问题。塑性损失的机制亦由同一框架给出：depth-2模型在解耦预训练后进入惰性训练（损失快速下降但参数与秩被冻结），即使新增观测使动力学耦合也无法恢复低秩结构；而深层模型因天然的低秩偏置不受此困扰。作者认为，这些矩阵补全的理论结果对理解深度如何塑造一般深度网络的隐式偏置、以及解释实际深度网络中的塑性丢失具有更广泛的启示意义。局限：理论依赖梯度流与特定确定性初始化族（及块对角、元素相等的观测结构），对一般随机初始化仅作数值与实验验证（如Figure 10、18–30），噪声/不等值观测下的理论仅有实验支撑。

Abstract: We study matrix completion via deep matrix factorization (a.k.a. deep linear neural networks) as a simplified testbed to examine how network depth influences training dynamics. Despite the simplicity and importance of the problem, prior theory largely focuses on shallow (depth-2) models and does not fully explain the implicit low-rank bias observed in deeper networks. We identify coupled dynamics as a key mechanism behind this bias and show that it intensifies with increasing depth. Focusing on gradient flow under block-diagonal observations, we prove: (a) networks of depth $\geq 3$ exhibit coupling unless initialized diagonally, and (b) convergence to rank-1 occurs if and only if the dynamics is coupled -- resolving an open question by Menon (2024) for a family of initializations. We also revisit the loss of plasticity phenomenon in matrix completion (Kleinman et al., 2024), where pre-training on few observations and resuming with more degrades performance. We show that deep models avoid plasticity loss due to their low-rank bias, whereas depth-2 networks pre-trained under decoupled dynamics fail to converge to low-rank, even when resumed training (with additional data) satisfies the coupling condition -- shedding light on the mechanism behind this phenomenon.

</details>


### [115] [Probabilistic Dreaming for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04715)
*Gavin Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 "ProbDreamer"——在 DreamerV3 骨架上把类别型隐状态换回高斯隐状态，并用粒子滤波（K 个粒子）+ 隐空间束搜索（N 个候选动作）+ 基于自由能的轨迹剪枝改造"做梦"式想象训练。在 MPE SimpleTag（三人追捕、追逐/拦截双模态策略）上，"Lite" ProbDreamer（K=2, N=1）比标准 Dreamer 平均得分提升 4.5%、回合回报方差降低 28%；但"Full"变体（束搜索 + 高粒子数）性能急剧下降，说明剪枝机制与集成不确定性估计均存在缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Dreamer 虽然建模了完整的隐状态分布，但在想象（dreaming）阶段每个时间步只采样单一隐状态并只rollout一条轨迹，限制了对全部"因"（causes）的并行探索；同时 DreamerV3/V4 转向离散类别隐变量以处理多模态，牺牲了连续隐变量平滑的梯度性质与稠密表征，而单峰高斯在面对互斥未来（如"向左"与"向右"）时会把两者平均成一个不存在的"中间"路径。作者因此希望用概率方法同时解决：并行探索多个隐状态、保留互斥未来的多个假设，同时保留连续隐变量的良好梯度性质，并维持计算可行性。

Method: 骨干 BaseDreamer：基于 DreamerV3 结构，但将类别隐分布替换为高斯（v1/v2 式），其余（RSSM 确定性隐状态 h_t 与随机隐状态 z_t、后验编码器、先验、解码器、奖励与终止预测头）保持一致；训练用重建损失、动力学 KL 损失、表征损失三项标准损失，每轮采 10^3 真实环境步后做 2·10^4 步想象训练，actor-critic 交替进行。
ProbDreamer 三项改动：(1) 粒子滤波——想象时维护 K 个粒子 {h_t^k, z_t^k} 跟踪先验分布，粒子转移仍为高斯但整体信念成为经验分布；(2) 隐空间束搜索——每个粒子分出 N 个由策略采样的候选动作，共 K*N 分支并行用世界模型传播；(3) 最小化自由能剪枝——因想象阶段无真实观测无法用 MLE 剪枝，改用 F_t^k = V_φ(h_t^k,z_t^k) + β·σ²_ens，即 critic 预测价值加先验模型集成方差（认知不确定性）来挑选分支。
实验：在 MPE SimpleTag（自定义追捕者内在双模态：靠近时随机切换 CHASE/INTERCEPT）上做 150 次 Bayesian Optimization 超参搜索（6 张 GPU），主要调 K、N、T 与学习率，架构超参固定；从搜索中选 6 个"决赛"配置（Baseline Dreamer K=1,N=1；Lite ProbDreamer K∈{2,4},N=1；Full ProbDreamer K∈{4,8},N∈{2,4}），在 100 个固定测试回合、5 个随机种子上评估。

Result: 表 1（0 为满分）：BaseDreamer-1 (K=1,N=1,T=16) 为 -9.21(±0.80)，BaseDreamer-2 (T=10) 为 -9.74(±0.79)；Lite ProbDreamer-1 (K=2,N=1,T=10) 最优为 -8.79(±0.68)；Lite-2 (K=4,T=22) 为 -9.43(±1.57)；Full-1 (K=2,N=4,T=10) 为 -53.78(±12.14)；Full-2 (K=8,N=1,T=22) 为 -26.84(±23.03)。Lite ProbDreamer 在 5 个种子中 4 个优于基线，平均得分提升 4.5%，回合回报方差降低 28%。定性上 ProbDreamer 能快速响应追捕者策略切换，而基线会短暂"僵住"，体现单峰高斯把互斥未来塌缩为瘫痪均值。
失败分析三点：(1) 粒子饱和——性能从 K=1→K=2 提升但粒子更多反而变差，可能因为双模态追捕者只需 2 个粒子，更多粒子在拟合噪声；(2) 剪枝机制失效——想象阶段缺乏真实观测校正，训练早期 critic 噪声导致其给不现实轨迹虚假高值并被优先选取，造成训练噪声和不收敛；(3) 集成坍塌——集成先验模型迅速趋于几乎相同预测，消融显示去掉好奇心项影响甚微。

Conclusion: 概率化的隐状态想象（尤其是以粒子滤波表示隐信念）能在保持连续隐变量梯度优势的同时并行展开多条"梦"，有效缓解多模态平均化问题，并在 MPE SimpleTag 上带来稳定且更鲁棒的策略；作者认为该方向值得探索非参数化世界模型。两大局限与后续方向：一是 MPE 全可观测域过于简单，双模态（K=2）即可饱和性能收益，需在部分可观测与混沌环境中检验最优粒子数 K 随环境复杂度的标度关系，K 可能需依领域知识谨慎选择；二是主动想象的核心瓶颈是缺乏真实观测来校正"梦"，自由能剪枝受噪声价值函数与集成坍塌所困，需发展内在刻画认知不确定性的架构（如用独立优化器与不同 episode 重放多样化集成、Monte-Carlo dropout 贝叶斯近似、或利用奖励/观测预测的分歧），以让智能体自主平衡探索与利用，接近人类认知中基于好奇心的主动学习机制。

Abstract: "Dreaming" enables agents to learn from imagined experiences, enabling more robust and sample-efficient learning of world models. In this work, we consider innovations to the state-of-the-art Dreamer model using probabilistic methods that enable: (1) the parallel exploration of many latent states; and (2) maintaining distinct hypotheses for mutually exclusive futures while retaining the desirable gradient properties of continuous latents. Evaluating on the MPE SimpleTag domain, our method outperforms standard Dreamer with a 4.5% score improvement and 28% lower variance in episode returns. We also discuss limitations and directions for future work, including how optimal hyperparameters (e.g. particle count K) scale with environmental complexity, and methods to capture epistemic uncertainty in world models.

</details>


### [116] [Count Bridges enable Modeling and Deconvolving Transcriptomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04730)
*Nic Fishman,Gokul Gowri,Tanush Kumar,Jiaqi Lu,Valentin de Bortoli,Jonathan S. Gootenberg,Omar Abudayyeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Count Bridges是一种针对生物计数数据的新型生成模型，模拟扩散风格框架处理整数数据，并通过EM方法实现从聚合测量直接训练，在单细胞RNA-seq去卷积和空间转录组学解析中展现优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生物检测（如RNA测序）产生整数计数值，但测量技术通常产生的是聚合在细胞集上的计数，而非单细胞分辨率的数据。现有的生成框架（如扩散和流匹配）虽然已扩展到非欧几里得和离散设置，但如何最好地建模整数数据或系统性地反卷积聚合观察仍不明确。

Method: 提出Count Bridges——一种基于整数的随机桥过程，作为扩散模型在计数数据中的精确可处理类比，具有封闭形式条件分布以实现高效训练和采样；并通过期望最大化(EM)风格的方法直接从聚合测量数据进行训练，将单位水平计数视为潜在变量。

Result: 在整数分布匹配基准测试中达到最先进性能，优于流匹配和离散流匹配基线；成功应用于两个大规模生物学问题：核苷酸分辨率的单细胞基因表达数据建模（用于bulk RNA-seq去卷积）以及将空间转录组学点解析为单细胞计数谱。

Conclusion: Count Bridges提供了整数计数数据生成建模和反卷积的原则性基础，在整数分布匹配基准测试中表现优于现有方法，并在单细胞基因表达去卷积和空间转录组学单细胞解析两个大规模生物学问题中得到验证。

Abstract: Many modern biological assays, including RNA sequencing, yield integer-valued counts that reflect the number of molecules detected. These measurements are often not at the desired resolution: while the unit of interest is typically a single cell, many measurement technologies produce counts aggregated over sets of cells. Although recent generative frameworks such as diffusion and flow matching have been extended to non-Euclidean and discrete settings, it remains unclear how best to model integer-valued data or how to systematically deconvolve aggregated observations. We introduce Count Bridges, a stochastic bridge process on the integers that provides an exact, tractable analogue of diffusion-style models for count data, with closed-form conditionals for efficient training and sampling. We extend this framework to enable direct training from aggregated measurements via an Expectation-Maximization-style approach that treats unit-level counts as latent variables. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on integer distribution matching benchmarks, comparing against flow matching and discrete flow matching baselines across various metrics. We then apply Count Bridges to two large-scale problems in biology: modeling single-cell gene expression data at the nucleotide resolution, with applications to deconvolving bulk RNA-seq, and resolving multicellular spatial transcriptomic spots into single-cell count profiles. Our methods offer a principled foundation for generative modeling and deconvolution of biological count data across scales and modalities.

</details>


### [117] [When Priors Backfire: On the Vulnerability of Unlearnable Examples to Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04731)
*Zhihao Li,Gezheng Xu,Jiale Cai,Ruiyi Fang,Di Wu,Qicheng Lao,Charles Ling,Boyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Unlearnable Examples在预训练模型场景下的根本性漏洞：预训练先验提供的语义表征使模型能够绕过扰动捕获真实特征。提出BAIT方法，通过双向优化将扰动与错误目标绑定，强制模型依赖扰动而非语义，从而恢复数据的不可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型提供了丰富的语义表征，使得即使数据受到精心设计的扰动保护，模型仍能绕过UEs引入的shortcuts并捕获真实特征，从而削弱了不可学习性的效果

Method: 提出BAIT（Binding Artificial perturbations to Incorrect Targets），采用双向优化框架：内层将扰动样本与真实标签对齐模拟标准数据-标签对齐，外层通过强制错误标签-扰动绑定来破坏这种对齐

Result: 在标准基准数据集和多个预训练 backbone 上的广泛实验表明，BAIT有效减轻了预训练先验的影响并保持了数据的不可学习性

Conclusion: BAIT通过双向优化机制成功绑定了错误标签与扰动，override了预训练模型的语义指导，使模型依赖注入的扰动而非真实语义，从而有效维持了数据的不可学习性。

Abstract: Unlearnable Examples (UEs) serve as a data protection strategy that generates imperceptible perturbations to mislead models into learning spurious correlations instead of underlying semantics. In this paper, we uncover a fundamental vulnerability of UEs that emerges when learning starts from a pretrained model. Crucially, our empirical analysis shows that even when data are protected by carefully crafted perturbations, pretraining priors still furnish rich semantic representations that allow the model to circumvent the shortcuts introduced by UEs and capture genuine features, thereby nullifying unlearnability. To address this, we propose BAIT (Binding Artificial perturbations to Incorrect Targets), a novel bi-level optimization formulation. Specifically, the inner level aims at associating the perturbed samples with real labels to simulate standard data-label alignment, while the outer level actively disrupts this alignment by enforcing a mislabel-perturbation binding that maps samples to designated incorrect targets. This mechanism effectively overrides the semantic guidance of priors, forcing the model to rely on the injected perturbations and consequently preventing the acquisition of true semantics. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks and multiple pretrained backbones demonstrate that BAIT effectively mitigates the influence of pretraining priors and maintains data unlearnability.

</details>


### [118] [Distribution-Conditioned Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04736)
*Nic Fishman,Gokul Gowri,Paolo L. B. Fischer,Marinka Zitnik,Omar Abudayyeh,Jonathan Gootenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Distribution-Conditioned Transport (DCT)通过学习源分布和目标分布的嵌入表示来调节传输映射，使模型能够泛化到 unseen distribution pairs。该框架支持半监督学习，可利用仅在一个条件下观察到的分布来改进预测，且与底层传输机制无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习中学习将源分布映射到目标分布是一个经典问题，但科学应用越来越需要能够泛化到训练中未见过的源分布和目标分布的模型。

Method: DCT框架通过学习源分布和目标分布的嵌入表示来调节传输映射，实现对未见分布对的泛化能力。该框架与底层传输机制无关，支持从flow matching到分布散度模型（如Wasserstein、MMD）的各种方法。

Result: 在合成基准测试和四个生物学应用中展示了DCT的实际性能优势：单细胞基因组学中的批次效应转移、质谱数据中的扰动预测、造血过程中的克隆转录动力学学习，以及T细胞受体序列演化建模。

Conclusion: DCT框架能够泛化到 unseen distribution pairs，支持从flow matching到基于分布散度的各种传输机制，并在四个生物学应用中展示了实际性能优势。

Abstract: Learning a transport model that maps a source distribution to a target distribution is a canonical problem in machine learning, but scientific applications increasingly require models that can generalize to source and target distributions unseen during training. We introduce distribution-conditioned transport (DCT), a framework that conditions transport maps on learned embeddings of source and target distributions, enabling generalization to unseen distribution pairs. DCT also allows semi-supervised learning for distributional forecasting problems: because it learns from arbitrary distribution pairs, it can leverage distributions observed at only one condition to improve transport prediction. DCT is agnostic to the underlying transport mechanism, supporting models ranging from flow matching to distributional divergence-based models (e.g. Wasserstein, MMD). We demonstrate the practical performance benefits of DCT on synthetic benchmarks and four applications in biology: batch effect transfer in single-cell genomics, perturbation prediction from mass cytometry data, learning clonal transcriptional dynamics in hematopoiesis, and modeling T-cell receptor sequence evolution.

</details>


### [119] [KindSleep: Knowledge-Informed Diagnosis of Obstructive Sleep Apnea from Oximetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04755)
*Micky C Nnamdi,Wenqi Shi,Cheng Wan,J. Ben Tamo,Benjamin M Smith,Chad A Purnell,May D Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KindSleep是一种深度学习框架，结合临床知识与单通道血氧仪信号和临床数据，用于精确诊断阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)。该框架首先从原始血氧信号中学习临床可解释概念，然后融合多模态临床数据估计呼吸暂停低通气指数(AHI)。在9,815例患者的大规模数据集中验证，R²达0.917，ICC为0.957，分类F1-score为0.827-0.941。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)影响全球近10亿人，传统多导睡眠图诊断资源密集且可及性受限，亟需准确、高效的替代诊断方案。

Method: KindSleep首先从原始血氧信号中学习识别临床可解释概念（如去饱和指数、呼吸紊乱事件），然后将这些AI衍生概念与多模态临床数据融合以估计AHI评分。

Result: KindSleep在三个大型独立数据集（SHHS、CFS、MrOS，总n=9,815）上表现出色：AHI估计R²=0.917，ICC=0.957；OSA严重程度分类加权F1-score达0.827-0.941，持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: KindSleep通过将深度学习预测建立在临床可解释概念层上，提供了更透明、可信的睡眠医学诊断工具，在三个独立大数据集上验证了优异的AHI估计性能和OSA严重程度分类性能。

Abstract: Obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) is a sleep disorder that affects nearly one billion people globally and significantly elevates cardiovascular risk. Traditional diagnosis through polysomnography is resource-intensive and limits widespread access, creating a critical need for accurate and efficient alternatives. In this paper, we introduce KindSleep, a deep learning framework that integrates clinical knowledge with single-channel patient-specific oximetry signals and clinical data for precise OSA diagnosis. KindSleep first learns to identify clinically interpretable concepts, such as desaturation indices and respiratory disturbance events, directly from raw oximetry signals. It then fuses these AI-derived concepts with multimodal clinical data to estimate the Apnea-Hypopnea Index (AHI). We evaluate KindSleep on three large, independent datasets from the National Sleep Research Resource (SHHS, CFS, MrOS; total n = 9,815). KindSleep demonstrates excellent performance in estimating AHI scores (R2 = 0.917, ICC = 0.957) and consistently outperforms existing approaches in classifying OSA severity, achieving weighted F1-scores from 0.827 to 0.941 across diverse populations. By grounding its predictions in a layer of clinically meaningful concepts, KindSleep provides a more transparent and trustworthy diagnostic tool for sleep medicine practices.

</details>


### [120] [ConTSG-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Conditional Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04767)
*Shaocheng Lan,Shuqi Gu,Zhangzhi Xiong,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTSG-Bench是一个用于条件时间序列生成的标准化基准测试框架，解决了该领域缺乏系统性评估工具的问题。该基准包含大规模对齐数据集，涵盖多种条件模态和语义抽象层次，并提供了生成质量和条件 adherence 的综合评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件时间序列生成在解决数据稀缺和因果分析方面具有重要作用，但该领域缺乏标准化、系统化的基准测试框架来评估不同条件下的生成模型。

Method: 构建了ConTSG-Bench基准，包含大规模、良好对齐的数据集，涵盖多样化的条件模态和语义抽象层次；使用综合评估指标对代表性生成方法进行系统评估。

Result: 定量基准测试和深入分析揭示了当前方法的特点和局限性，突出了关键挑战和有前景的研究方向，特别是在复杂条件下精确结构可控性和下游任务实用性方面。

Conclusion: 当前条件时间序列生成方法在精确结构可控性和复杂条件下的下游任务实用性方面存在不足，需要进一步研究解决这些关键挑战。

Abstract: Conditional time series generation plays a critical role in addressing data scarcity and enabling causal analysis in real-world applications. Despite its increasing importance, the field lacks a standardized and systematic benchmarking framework for evaluating generative models across diverse conditions. To address this gap, we introduce the Conditional Time Series Generation Benchmark (ConTSG-Bench). ConTSG-Bench comprises a large-scale, well-aligned dataset spanning diverse conditioning modalities and levels of semantic abstraction, first enabling systematic evaluation of representative generation methods across these dimensions with a comprehensive suite of metrics for generation fidelity and condition adherence. Both the quantitative benchmarking and in-depth analyses of conditional generation behaviors have revealed the traits and limitations of the current approaches, highlighting critical challenges and promising research directions, particularly with respect to precise structural controllability and downstream task utility under complex conditions.

</details>


### [121] [Distributional Reinforcement Learning with Information Bottleneck for Uncertainty-Aware DRAM Equalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04768)
*Muhammad Usama,Dong Eui Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高速存储系统中均衡器参数优化问题，提出融合信息瓶颈与CVaR的分布强化学习框架，通过信号压缩实现51倍加速，在240万波形上验证并实现最坏情况性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在三个关键问题：1) 眼图评估计算昂贵；2) 优化的是期望性能而非最坏情况性能；3) 部署决策缺少不确定性量化。

Method: 提出了一种分布风险敏感强化学习框架，整合信息瓶颈潜在表示与条件在险值(CVaR)优化。具体包括：1) 率-失真最优信号压缩实现51倍加速；2) 蒙特卡洛 dropout 量化认知不确定性；3) 分位数回归的分布强化学习实现显式最坏情况优化；4) PAC-贝叶斯正则化证明泛化边界。

Result: 在8个存储单元的240万波形上验证，4-tap和8-tap均衡器配置分别实现37.1%和41.5%的平均改进，最坏情况保证分别为33.8%和38.2%。相比Q-learning基线分别提升80.7%和89.1%。框架实现62.5%的高可靠性分类。

Conclusion: 该框架为生产规模均衡器优化提供了实用的解决方案，具有经过认证的最坏情况保证。通过在240万个波形上验证，实现了62.5%的高可靠性分类，消除大多数配置的手动验证需求。

Abstract: Equalizer parameter optimization is critical for signal integrity in high-speed memory systems operating at multi-gigabit data rates. However, existing methods suffer from computationally expensive eye diagram evaluation, optimization of expected rather than worst-case performance, and absence of uncertainty quantification for deployment decisions. In this paper, we propose a distributional risk-sensitive reinforcement learning framework integrating Information Bottleneck latent representations with Conditional Value-at-Risk optimization. We introduce rate-distortion optimal signal compression achieving 51 times speedup over eye diagrams while quantifying epistemic uncertainty through Monte Carlo dropout. Distributional reinforcement learning with quantile regression enables explicit worst-case optimization, while PAC-Bayesian regularization certifies generalization bounds. Experimental validation on 2.4 million waveforms from eight memory units demonstrated mean improvements of 37.1\% and 41.5\% for 4-tap and 8-tap equalizer configurations with worst-case guarantees of 33.8\% and 38.2\%, representing 80.7\% and 89.1\% improvements over Q-learning baselines. The framework achieved 62.5\% high-reliability classification eliminating manual validation for most configurations. These results suggest the proposed framework provides a practical solution for production-scale equalizer optimization with certified worst-case guarantees.

</details>


### [122] [Distributional Equivalence in Linear Non-Gaussian Latent-Variable Cyclic Causal Models: Characterization and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04780)
*Haoyue Dai,Immanuel Albrecht,Peter Spirtes,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性非高斯模型中潜在变量因果发现的等价性特征刻画问题，提出edge rank constraints工具，建立了任意潜在结构和循环下图分布等价的图形准则，开发了相应的模型恢复算法，这是首个无需结构假设的因果发现方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖强结构假设（如强制特定指标模式或限制潜在变量与其它变量的交互方式），缺乏对可识别内容的等价性特征刻画，这阻碍了通用、无结构假设方法的设计。

Method: 建立任意潜在结构和有向循环的图何时分布等价的图形准则；提出新工具edge rank constraints；提供遍历整个等价类的程序；开发从数据恢复模型的算法。

Result: 首次在任意参数设置下无需结构假设的等价性特征刻画；首次实现无需结构假设的因果发现方法。

Conclusion: 解决了潜在变量因果发现中的关键理论问题，为线性非高斯模型提供了通用的结构无关发现方法。

Abstract: Causal discovery with latent variables is a fundamental task. Yet most existing methods rely on strong structural assumptions, such as enforcing specific indicator patterns for latents or restricting how they can interact with others. We argue that a core obstacle to a general, structural-assumption-free approach is the lack of an equivalence characterization: without knowing what can be identified, one generally cannot design methods for how to identify it. In this work, we aim to close this gap for linear non-Gaussian models. We establish the graphical criterion for when two graphs with arbitrary latent structure and cycles are distributionally equivalent, that is, they induce the same observed distribution set. Key to our approach is a new tool, edge rank constraints, which fills a missing piece in the toolbox for latent-variable causal discovery in even broader settings. We further provide a procedure to traverse the whole equivalence class and develop an algorithm to recover models from data up to such equivalence. To our knowledge, this is the first equivalence characterization with latent variables in any parametric setting without structural assumptions, and hence the first structural-assumption-free discovery method. Code and an interactive demo are available at https://equiv.cc.

</details>


### [123] [Diffusion Policy through Conditional Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04790)
*Ben Liu,Shunpeng Yang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在on-policy强化学习中训练扩散策略的高效新方法，通过将策略迭代与扩散过程对齐，只需评估简单高斯概率即可计算动作对数似然，同时可自然处理熵正则化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在建模多模态行为方面表现出强大潜力，但在on-policy强化学习中应用时面临计算动作对数似然的困难，传统方法需通过整个去噪过程计算或近似，效率低下且占用内存。

Method: 通过将策略迭代与扩散过程对齐，提出一种新颖的高效方法训练扩散策略。该方法只需评估简单高斯概率即可完成训练，避免了复杂的去噪过程计算。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够产生多模态策略行为，在IsaacLab和MuJoCo Playground的多种基准任务上取得优异性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将扩散策略应用于on-policy强化学习，在保持多模态行为生成能力的同时，显著提高了计算效率，并为熵正则化的引入提供了自然途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been extensively employed in a wide range of decision-making problems, such as games and robotics. Recently, diffusion policies have shown strong potential in modeling multi-modal behaviors, enabling more diverse and flexible action generation compared to the conventional Gaussian policy. Despite various attempts to combine RL with diffusion, a key challenge is the difficulty of computing action log-likelihood under the diffusion model. This greatly hinders the direct application of diffusion policies in on-policy reinforcement learning. Most existing methods calculate or approximate the log-likelihood through the entire denoising process in the diffusion model, which can be memory- and computationally inefficient. To overcome this challenge, we propose a novel and efficient method to train a diffusion policy in an on-policy setting that requires only evaluating a simple Gaussian probability. This is achieved by aligning the policy iteration with the diffusion process, which is a distinct paradigm compared to previous work. Moreover, our formulation can naturally handle entropy regularization, which is often difficult to incorporate into diffusion policies. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method produces multimodal policy behaviors and achieves superior performance on a variety of benchmark tasks in both IsaacLab and MuJoCo Playground.

</details>


### [124] [Multilevel Training for Kolmogorov Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04827)
*Ben S. Southworth,Jonas A. Actor,Graham Harper,Eric C. Cyr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Kolmogorov-Arnold 网络（KAN）的多层训练（multilevel training）"框架：先证明样条基 KAN 与多通道 MLP（ReLU^{r-1} 激活）之间存在线性换基等价关系，该换基矩阵恰为 r 阶导数的前向有限差分离散；再分析该换基作为预条件子如何改变梯度下降的动力学（ReLU 基强烈偏好光滑模态，样条基因紧支撑而具局部性、层次间互补）；据此提出"properly nested hierarchy（恰当嵌套层级）"定义与几何式样条结点细化/插值算子，构造类多重网格的嵌套多层训练算法。数值实验显示，相对同等 FLOPs 下的常规 KAN/MLP 训练精度提升 2–3 个数量级（PINN 尤为显著），而用 ReLU 基做多层训练则几乎零增益，验证了"恰当嵌套 + 互补松弛"是多层机器学习成功的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 MLP 由仿射映射与逐元素非线性函数的复合构成，缺少由函数复合所保证的结构，使得难以像数值 PDE 领域的多重网格/多层方法那样获得算法级（而非并行化）的训练加速：此前多层机器学习工作（multilevel-in-layer、multigrid-in-time、非线性多重网格循环、feed-forward 网络细化的理论框架）要么只带来有限收敛改善，要么缺乏具备良好逼近性质（approximation properties）的层间层级与与粗层互补的网格专属"松弛"算子；根本障碍在于粗/细模型在同一维度空间中，难以定义粗表示与传输算子。相比之下，KAN 将学习到的激活函数展开在给定基（通常是 B 样条）上，从而提供了 MLP 所缺失的可利用结构；作者的目标是挖掘这一结构，把多重网格思想真正落到 KAN 的训练算法与理论解释上，并回答"什么架构—优化器配对才能既快又准"。

Method: 理论部分：(1) 定义 B 样条基 B_S^{[r]} 与幂 ReLU 基 B_R^{[r]}={ReLU(x-t_i)^{r-1}}，二者同为样条空间 S_r(T) 的基，故存在换基矩阵 A^{[r]}（Lemma 1–3），给出显式递推 A^{[r]}_{ij}=(1/(t_{i+r-1}-t_i))A^{[r-1]}_{ij}-(1/(t_{i+r}-t_{i+1}))A^{[r-1]}_{i+1,j}，均匀结点时 A^{[r]}=h^{1-r}(A^{[1]})^r/(r-1)! 为上三角 Toeplitz；由此 KAN 层等价于多通道 MLP 层，权重关系 W = W̃ ×_3 (A^{[r]})^T。(2) Lemma 4：A^{[r]} 是 r 阶导数的前向有限差分（强零 Dirichlet 边界），(A^{[r]})^T A^{[r]} 是 2r 阶导数（r=1 时即 [1,-2,1] 扩散模板）；其特征值约按 ℓ^{2r} 增长，最大/最小特征值比约 n^{2r}（n=10 时 r=1 差 100×，r=3 差 10^6×），特征向量按光滑度排序（符号变化数 0→n-1）。(3) 计算代价：Cox-de Boor 需 O(PQ(nr+r²))，ReLU 形式只需 O(PQ(n+r))，换基因带状仅 O(nr)，均匀结点可用卷积实现，实测墙钟加速随 r 增大。(4) 梯度几何（Section 3, Table 1）：换基在两个空间间构成预条件，样条基梯度下降等价于 (AA^T)-内积下的梯度下降，ReLU 基等价于 (A^TA)^{-1}-内积下的梯度下降；因 (AA^T)^{-1}∼(∂_x)^{-r}，ReLU 基强烈偏向光滑模态。附录给出 NTK 谱半径界 ρ(NTK_S)≤4ρ(NTK_R)，故可用相近学习率。(5) 多层框架（Section 4）：定义 properly nested hierarchy：g_c(x,u^(c)) = g_f(x,P u^(c))，保证插值到细网格不抹掉粗层进展（Proposition 1 给出子空间优化等价性）；传输算子 P = A_T^{[r]} I (A_{T'}^{[r]})^{-1}，对均匀二分细化有显式 P_ij = 2^{-r} C(r,s)（j=2i+s），并证明其与原 KAN 网格传输一致但成本低得多；Lemma 6 推广到任意"细化下嵌套"的激活函数基（B 样条/Chebyshev/小波）。Algorithm 1 给出由粗到细、逐层求解并用 P 延拓的嵌套多层优化（未做完整多重网格循环）。互补性论证：样条基权重具紧支撑，梯度更新局部化，可立即利用细化新增的振荡/低正则性表达力；ReLU 基则各层均偏好光滑模态，与细化互补性相反。数值部分：函数回归（L-BFGS，等 FLOPs 预算）、2D Poisson（变系数、界面跳跃，RBA 加权，Adam）、1D Burgers（指数循环学习率）、Allen-Cahn（残差傅里叶谱分析）。

Result: (1) 换基：KAN(样条) 与多通道 MLP(power ReLU) 前向等价，但训练动力学本质不同；换基矩阵即 r 阶微分算子的有限差分。(2) 计算：ReLU+换基实现比 Cox-de Boor 快约 r 倍（FLOPs），墙钟加速随阶数与结点数增长。(3) 回归（[2,5,1] KAN，同等 FLOPs）：样条基多层 MSE 3.67×10^{-5}，而样条粗模型 1.65×10^{-3}、细模型 2.54×10^{-3}、最好 MLP 3.33×10^{-4}；ReLU 基多层 1.06×10^{-2} 与粗模型 1.10×10^{-2} 几乎无差别，细模型更差 1.35；样条基相对 ReLU 基优 1–3 个数量级。(4) 2D Poisson PINN：样条基多层 KAN 比同规模 MLP 更快达到更小相对误差且误差噪声更小，损失在每次细化后出现"阶梯式"骤降（细化前已平台化）；ReLU 基多层停滞于 O(1) 相对误差以下。(5) 1D Burgers：多层 KAN 损失 2.40×10^{-5}，而粗 6.64×10^{-3}、细 4.07×10^{-3}、MLP 2.33×10^{-2}，提升 2–3 个数量级。(6) Allen-Cahn：样条基多层解明显更好，残差傅里叶谱能量随细化下降、活跃高频支撑变宽（定向改进高频模态）；同规模 MLP 与 ReLU 基 KAN 均失败，无法捕捉解结构。结论性对照：ReLU 基多层训练零增益，印证"细化后的细层优化必须与粗层互补"。

Conclusion: 本文给出并验证了一个原则：多层机器学习要取得算法级加速，必须同时具备（i）恰当嵌套的层级（插值不抵消粗层进展、细层问题是粗层的子空间优化）与（ii）跨层互补的松弛/优化动力学。KAN 的样条基天然满足二者——紧支撑带来局部化更新、可立即利用细化带来的新表达力；而换基后的多通道 MLP（ReLU 基）虽与 KAN 前向等价，却因预条件效应偏好光滑模态，故多层训练失效。据此构造的多层 KAN 训练在函数回归与 PINN（Poisson、Burgers、Allen-Cahn）上相对同等计算量的常规 KAN/MLP 取得 2–3 个数量级精度提升，同时残差谱分析支持"逐级定向改进高频模态"的解释。方法不依赖数据或训练技巧的定制，稳健可用。作者指出局限与后续方向：本文未实现完整的多重网格循环（nonlinear cycling/嵌套多层优化对非线性敏感），未来将研究循环策略、推广到其他架构与其他粗化/细化方式（如按层/按节点粗化，本文指出按层粗化难以满足嵌套条件），并补充了 NTK 谱分析、超参数与 ReLU 基对照实验等附录材料。

Abstract: Algorithmic speedup of training common neural architectures is made difficult by the lack of structure guaranteed by the function compositions inherent to such networks. In contrast to multilayer perceptrons (MLPs), Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (KANs) provide more structure by expanding learned activations in a specified basis. This paper exploits this structure to develop practical algorithms and theoretical insights, yielding training speedup via multilevel training for KANs. To do so, we first establish an equivalence between KANs with spline basis functions and multichannel MLPs with power ReLU activations through a linear change of basis. We then analyze how this change of basis affects the geometry of gradient-based optimization with respect to spline knots. The KANs change-of-basis motivates a multilevel training approach, where we train a sequence of KANs naturally defined through a uniform refinement of spline knots with analytic geometric interpolation operators between models. The interpolation scheme enables a ``properly nested hierarchy'' of architectures, ensuring that interpolation to a fine model preserves the progress made on coarse models, while the compact support of spline basis functions ensures complementary optimization on subsequent levels. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our multilevel training approach can achieve orders of magnitude improvement in accuracy over conventional methods to train comparable KANs or MLPs, particularly for physics informed neural networks. Finally, this work demonstrates how principled design of neural networks can lead to exploitable structure, and in this case, multilevel algorithms that can dramatically improve training performance.

</details>


### [125] [Missingness Bias Calibration in Feature Attribution Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04831)
*Shailesh Sridhar,Anton Xue,Eric Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了特征重要性评分中缺失偏差需要深度架构修改的传统观点，提出MCal方法——一种在冻结模型输出上微调线性头的轻量级事后校正方法，在多种医学基准上表现优于或等同于先前的重量级方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决流行解释方法中因缺失偏差导致的特征重要性评分不可靠问题，挑战了以往认为需要昂贵重训练或架构修改的假设

Method: 提出MCal方法，通过在冻结基模型的输出上微调一个简单的线性头来纠正缺失偏差，这是一个轻量级的事后校正方法

Result: 该简单校正方法一致地减少缺失偏差，在涵盖视觉、语言和表格领域的医学基准测试中表现具有竞争力，优于或与先前重量级方法相当

Conclusion: MCal通过在冻结基模型的输出上微调一个简单的线性头来纠正缺失偏差，这种轻量级的事后方法在各种医学基准测试中表现竞争力强，优于或与先前重量级方法相当

Abstract: Popular explanation methods often produce unreliable feature importance scores due to missingness bias, a systematic distortion that arises when models are probed with ablated, out-of-distribution inputs. Existing solutions treat this as a deep representational flaw that requires expensive retraining or architectural modifications. In this work, we challenge this assumption and show that missingness bias can be effectively treated as a superficial artifact of the model's output space. We introduce MCal, a lightweight post-hoc method that corrects this bias by fine-tuning a simple linear head on the outputs of a frozen base model. Surprisingly, we find this simple correction consistently reduces missingness bias and is competitive with, or even outperforms, prior heavyweight approaches across diverse medical benchmarks spanning vision, language, and tabular domains.

</details>


### [126] [Why Is RLHF Alignment Shallow? A Gradient Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04851)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了基于梯度的对齐方法只能产生"浅层"对齐，因为对齐梯度集中在决定伤害的位置，而在超出伤害范围的位置梯度消失为零。通过序列级别伤害的鞅分解，推导出对齐梯度的精确表征：位置t的梯度等于条件期望伤害与得分函数之间的协方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为什么大型语言模型的安全对齐是"浅层"的？即KL散度主要集中在早期token，而对齐目标无法在所有位置产生有效的梯度信号。这是一个关于对齐方法有效性的根本性问题。

Method: 使用鞅分解技术对序列级别伤害进行数学分析，精确表征对齐梯度的形式，并引入"伤害信息"(harm information)概念来量化每个位置对最终伤害的影响程度。

Result: 1) 位置t的对齐梯度等于条件期望伤害与得分函数之间的协方差；2) 超出伤害视界的位置（输出伤害已确定的位置）在训练时收到零梯度信号；3) 对齐模型与基础模型之间的KL散度集中在早期token；4) 平衡时的KL散度追踪伤害信息I_t；5) 提出基于恢复惩罚的目标，可在所有位置产生梯度信号。

Conclusion: 标准对齐目标无论优化质量如何都无法产生深度对齐。引入恢复惩罚目标可创建全位置梯度信号，为经验上成功的数据增强技术提供理论依据。

Abstract: Why is safety alignment in LLMs shallow? We prove that gradient-based alignment inherently concentrates on positions where harm is decided and vanishes beyond. Using a martingale decomposition of sequence-level harm, we derive an exact characterization of alignment gradients. The gradient at position $t$ equals the covariance between the conditional expected harm and the score function. This implies that positions beyond the harm horizon where the output's harmfulness is already determined receive zero gradient signal during training. This explains empirical observations that KL divergence between aligned and base models concentrates on early tokens. Consequently, standard alignment objectives cannot produce deep alignment, regardless of optimization quality. We introduce the concept of harm information $I_t$, which quantifies each position's influence on harm, and prove that equilibrium KL divergence tracks this quantity. Finally, we derive an objective based on recovery penalties that creates gradient signal at all positions, providing theoretical grounding for empirically successful data augmentation techniques.

</details>


### [127] [Differential Privacy in Two-Layer Networks: How DP-SGD Harms Fairness and Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04881)
*Ruichen Xu,Kexin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的"特征中心"(feature-centric)分析框架，刻画 DP-SGD 在两层 ReLU 卷积网络上的特征学习动力学，并证明所有测试损失界都由单一关键指标——特征噪声比 FNR（‖u_{i,j}‖₂/σ_n）——所支配。由此统一解释了 DP-SGD 的三大副作用：跨类/跨子群的 disparate impact（源于 FNR 不平衡）、同类内长尾数据受损更重、以及对抗鲁棒性下降（源于学到非鲁棒的类无关噪声与参数范数增长）。此外证明"公开预训练+私有微调"在特征分布偏移大时并不保证优于从零训练（甚至更差）。最后提出数据增强与分阶段网络冻结/剪枝来提升 FNR，并在合成数据与 MNIST/CIFAR-10 上验证了理论结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私学习（DP-SGD）在敏感数据上训练模型时，通过梯度裁剪与噪声注入获得严格隐私保证，但大量实证研究表明其存在三类系统性副作用：(1) 学到次优的"坏特征"，性能甚至不如手工特征；(2) 产生 disparate impact，不同类别/子群准确率显著不均；(3) 对抗鲁棒性下降。已有理论解释（Tran et al. 2021、Sanyal et al. 2022、Zhang & Bu 2022、Wu et al. 2024 等）普遍依赖凸性、Lipschitz 光滑性、二次可微或 NTK 区域等结构性假设，无法刻画现代非凸、非光滑的 ReLU 网络——因为 DP 噪声使参数远离初始化，且 ReLU/交叉熵/Softmax 高度非线性。另一方面，"公开预训练+私有微调"被视为缓解手段（Berrada et al. 2023 报告约 30% 准确率提升），但 Tramèr et al. 2024 指出预训练与微调数据分布常高度相似，该结论的普适性存疑。因此本文的核心问题是：能否在统一的特征学习框架下，同时解释 DP-SGD 的性能退化、公平性受损与鲁棒性下降，并厘清公开预训练何时真正有效？

Method: **模型与数据设定**：两层 ReLU CNN（每类 m 个神经元，核在两个 patch 间共享，第二层权重固定 1/m），二分类；数据由 (x^(1), x^(2)) 两个 patch 组成，一个为"特征 patch"（按概率 p_f 含多数特征 u_{y,maj}，否则含少数特征 u_{y,min}），另一个为高斯噪声 patch N(0, σ_p²H)（H 为特征正交补投影）；特征向量互相正交且多数特征范数更大。训练用 DP-SGD：裁剪阈值 C，噪声 N(0, σ_n²I)。

**核心指标（Def 3.3）**：FNR ℱ_{i,j}=‖u_{i,j}‖₂/σ_n；裁剪因子 Λ_{i,j}=C/(‖u_{i,j}‖₂+σ_p√d)；数据占比 γ_{i,j}（如 γ_{1,maj}=p_c p_f）。

**证明技术（三重挑战的应对）**：① ReLU 非光滑 → 放弃"leading neuron 主导"类分析（该方法仅适用 ReLU q>2），改为跟踪模型输出 F_i 的增量 Δ^{(t)}_y(x) 与 Δ^{(t)}_{3−y}(x)；② DP 噪声随机性破坏权重单调性 → 用输出增量而非权重范数来刻画特征学习（Prop. I.1）；③ Softmax/交叉熵非线性 → 用分段线性函数逐段上界 log 变换（Lemma I.2、Lemma J.8/J.9），得到损失递推不等式 (60)，再经几何级数求和（Lemma J.10）得最终界。

**主要结论**：Thm 3.4（测试损失上界，含 vanishing/generalization/privacy 三项）；Thm 3.5（期望测试损失下界）；Thm 3.7（对抗测试损失上界，含 ζ̄d^{1−1/p} 缩放）；Cor 4.1/4.2（类间与子群间 disparate impact）；Prop 4.4（预训练-微调：特征以角度 θ 旋转，初始化损失 L̃(θ) 随 θ 单调递增）；Sec 5 改进策略（数据增强、分阶段网络冻结/剪枝，Algorithm 2）。条件假设包括 d ≥ c₂log(n/δ)、B ≥ c₁n（大 batch）、‖u_{i,j}‖₂ ≥ c₃σ_p ≥ c₃σ_n（信噪比足够）、学习率上界，以及"非完美模型"假设 L ≥ s。

**实验**：合成数据（m=32，patch 维 100，σ_p=0.2，训练/测试各 450 样本，特征范数 4/2/1.5/0.5 与占比 44%/22%/22%/11% 制造特征差异与数据失衡）；MNIST（LeNet）与 CIFAR-10（CNN/ResNet-18），ε=3、α=10⁻⁵、C=0.1、batch 256，Opacus 实现；PGD 攻击（合成 ζ̄=0.02，真实 4/255）；padding 比例模拟特征/噪声比、旋转角模拟分布偏移、类别样本量模拟失衡、ViT 扩展实验。

Result: **1) 统一的测试损失刻画（Thm 3.4）**：L_{D_{i,j}}(W^{(T)}) ≤ exp(−Ω(Λ_{i,j}γ_{i,j}‖u_{i,j}‖₂²T/m))·L_{D_{i,j}}(W^{(0)}) + O(1/(√n·γ_{i,j}Λ_{i,j})) [泛化误差] + O(m/(Λ_{i,j}γ_{i,j}ℱ_{i,j})) [隐私保护误差]。上下界（Thm 3.5）均与 FNR 成反比：隐私误差项随训练步数 T 以 O(√T) 增长（因 σ_n²=Θ(T)），形成不可消除的残余误差。由于 0-1 精度的不连续性，隐私保护存在"良性→有害"的相变（Fig. 1）。

**2) disparate impact（Cor 4.1/4.2）**：三类来源——(a) 特征差异（FNR 随 ‖u‖₂ 增大而增大，长尾弱特征样本最易被误分）；(b) 梯度裁剪（Λ_{i,j}=C/(‖u‖₂+σ_p√d)，梯度范数大的类/群被更激进裁剪，特征学习更差）；(c) 数据失衡（隐私误差随 γ_{i,j} 增大而减小，少数群承受不成比例的错误率，对 ImageNet/SUN 等长尾数据尤其危险）。

**3) 对抗鲁棒性（Thm 3.7）**：对抗测试损失额外增加 O([T·C/m + √(Td)σ_n/m + √d σ₀]·ζ̄·d^{1−1/p})，其中 DP 噪声项以 O(√T) 增长。机制解释有二：DP 噪声迫使网络学到类无关的非鲁棒噪声特征；DP 噪声使参数范数随 T 增长，从而放大 <w, ζ>，提高扰动敏感性。

**4) 公开预训练+私有微调（Prop 4.4）**：微调损失上界中的初始化项 L̃(θ) 随特征旋转角 θ 单调递增；若 L̃ > L_{D_2}(W^{(0)})，预训练反而比从零训练更差。因此公开预训练不是"万灵药"，在特征分布偏移显著时可能失效。

**5) 实验验证**：合成数据上测试损失随 σ_n 增大、特征范数小的类/群损失更大且组间差距随噪声扩大；PGD 攻击下 DP 模型部分组鲁棒性显著下降；MNIST 上误分类样本多为"书写潦草"的长尾样本，同类内受损更重；padding 比例增大（等价于 FNR 下降）时 MNIST 准确率 97%→86%、对抗准确率 95%→1%，CIFAR-10 58%→46%、对抗 3%→0%；类别样本量从 3000 降到 100 时该类准确率 97%→71%；旋转角从 0° 增至 67.5° 时 CIFAR-10 ResNet 微调准确率 91%→37%；ViT 实验同样支持 FNR 的普适性；分阶段网络冻结（(1,10⁻⁵)-DP，77% 剪枝比例）把 MNIST LeNet 准确率从 95.11% 提升至 96.71%，数据增强在 CIFAR-10 上带来约 2% 提升。

Conclusion: 论文给出了一个可解释、可复用的统一理论：以"特征噪声比 FNR"为枢纽，把 DP-SGD 的性能损失、群体公平性受损（类间与类内长尾）与对抗鲁棒性下降归因到同一机制——为满足隐私而注入的噪声破坏了特征学习的最优性。FNR 越高（特征越强、噪声越小、群占比越大），隐私代价越小；反之，弱特征、长尾语义、数据稀疏的群体承受更大损失。理论上还纠正了"公开预训练必然有益"的直觉：其收益取决于预训练与微调数据的特征差异，偏移过大时预训练甚至可能劣于从零训练。实践建议为数据增强与分阶段网络冻结/剪枝以提升 FNR。局限方面，分析限于两层 ReLU CNN、结构化的多数/少数特征合成数据分布、以及"非完美模型"与 batch size 与 n 同阶等较强假设，尚未覆盖深层网络与真实复杂数据分布；结论也表明"DP 噪声在高维区可改善泛化"的另一视角与本文结论互补，二者的适用边界仍有待厘清。

Abstract: Differentially private learning is essential for training models on sensitive data, but empirical studies consistently show that it can degrade performance, introduce fairness issues like disparate impact, and reduce adversarial robustness. The theoretical underpinnings of these phenomena in modern, non-convex neural networks remain largely unexplored. This paper introduces a unified feature-centric framework to analyze the feature learning dynamics of differentially private stochastic gradient descent (DP-SGD) in two-layer ReLU convolutional neural networks. Our analysis establishes test loss bounds governed by a crucial metric: the feature-to-noise ratio (FNR). We demonstrate that the noise required for privacy leads to suboptimal feature learning, and specifically show that: 1) imbalanced FNRs across classes and subpopulations cause disparate impact; 2) even in the same class, noise has a greater negative impact on semantically long-tailed data; and 3) noise injection exacerbates vulnerability to adversarial attacks. Furthermore, our analysis reveals that the popular paradigm of public pre-training and private fine-tuning does not guarantee improvement, particularly under significant feature distribution shifts between datasets. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data corroborate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [128] [FedAFD: Multimodal Federated Learning via Adversarial Fusion and Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04890)
*Min Tan,Junchao Ma,Yinfu Feng,Jiajun Ding,Wenwen Pan,Tingting Han,Qian Zheng,Zhenzhong Kuang,Zhou Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedAFD框架，通过双层对抗对齐、粒度感知融合和相似度引导的集成蒸馏，解决多模态联邦学习中的模态/任务差异和模型异构性问题，在IID和非IID设置下实现优异的客户端与服务器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在三个关键问题：1) 忽视个性化客户端性能；2) 难以处理模态/任务差异；3) 模型异构性挑战。Multimodal Federated Learning (MFL)虽能在保护隐私前提下利用跨模态信息，但亟需解决这些核心问题。

Method: 提出统一MFL框架FedAFD，包含：1) 客户端：双层对抗对齐策略对齐局部与全局表示（模态内和跨模态），粒度感知融合模块自适应地将全局知识融入个性化特征；2) 服务器：相似度引导的集成蒸馏机制，基于特征相似性在共享公共数据上聚合客户端表示，并将融合知识蒸馏到全局模型。

Result: 在IID和非IID设置下的广泛实验表明，FedAFD在客户端和服务器端均达到优越性能和效率。

Conclusion: FedAFD通过创新性地结合对抗对齐、特征融合和蒸馏技术，有效解决了多模态联邦学习中的核心挑战，为隐私保护下的跨模态协作学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Federated Learning (MFL) enables clients with heterogeneous data modalities to collaboratively train models without sharing raw data, offering a privacy-preserving framework that leverages complementary cross-modal information. However, existing methods often overlook personalized client performance and struggle with modality/task discrepancies, as well as model heterogeneity. To address these challenges, we propose FedAFD, a unified MFL framework that enhances client and server learning. On the client side, we introduce a bi-level adversarial alignment strategy to align local and global representations within and across modalities, mitigating modality and task gaps. We further design a granularity-aware fusion module to integrate global knowledge into the personalized features adaptively. On the server side, to handle model heterogeneity, we propose a similarity-guided ensemble distillation mechanism that aggregates client representations on shared public data based on feature similarity and distills the fused knowledge into the global model. Extensive experiments conducted under both IID and non-IID settings demonstrate that FedAFD achieves superior performance and efficiency for both the client and the server.

</details>


### [129] [U-Parking: Distributed UWB-Assisted Autonomous Parking System with Robust Localization and Intelligent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04898)
*Yiang Wu,Qiong Wu,Pingyi Fan,Kezhi Wang,Wen Chen,Guoqiang Mao,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: U-Parking是一个分布式超宽带(UWB)辅助的自主泊车系统,结合了大语言模型(LLM)辅助规划、鲁棒融合定位和轨迹跟踪,能够在具有挑战性的室内环境中实现可靠的自动泊车,并通过实车演示进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有挑战性的室内环境中实现可靠的自主泊车面临诸多困难,包括精确定位、复杂轨迹规划和环境感知等挑战。现有泊车系统在室内场景下的可靠性和适应性有限。

Method: 该系统采用分布式架构,集成了以下关键技术:(1)UWB辅助定位技术;(2)大语言模型(LLM)辅助的规划模块;(3)鲁棒融合定位方法;(4)轨迹跟踪控制。通过实车演示验证系统性能。

Result: 系统在具有挑战性的室内环境中实现了可靠的自主泊车功能,验证了UWB辅助定位与LLM规划融合的有效性。

Conclusion: U-Parking系统成功地将UWB定位技术与LLM辅助规划相结合,为室内环境的自主泊车提供了一种可行的技术方案,具有较高的实用价值。

Abstract: This demonstration presents U-Parking, a distributed Ultra-Wideband (UWB)-assisted autonomous parking system. By integrating Large Language Models (LLMs)-assisted planning with robust fusion localization and trajectory tracking, it enables reliable automated parking in challenging indoor environments, as validated through real-vehicle demonstrations.

</details>


### [130] [EVMbench: Evaluating AI Agents on Smart Contract Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04915)
*Justin Wang,Andreas Bigger,Xiaohai Xu,Justin W. Lin,Andy Applebaum,Tejal Patwardhan,Alpin Yukseloglu,Olivia Watkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EVMbench是一个评估AI智能体检测、修补和利用智能合约漏洞能力的基准测试框架，基于117个来自40个代码库的漏洞构建，使用程序化评估方法在本地以太坊执行环境中进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公有区块链上的智能合约管理着大量价值，这些系统中的漏洞可能导致重大损失。随着AI智能体在读取、编写和运行代码方面的能力不断提升，需要评估它们在这一领域的表现，包括提升安全性和增加风险的两个方面。

Method: 构建EVMbench评估框架，包含117个精选漏洞来自40个代码库，采用基于测试和区块链状态的程序化评分方法，在本地以太坊执行环境中进行最真实的设置评估。

Result: 评估多个前沿AI智能体后发现，它们能够端到端地发现并利用真实区块链实例中的漏洞。

Conclusion: 发布了代码、任务和工具，以支持对这些能力的持续测量以及未来安全相关的研究工作。

Abstract: Smart contracts on public blockchains now manage large amounts of value, and vulnerabilities in these systems can lead to substantial losses. As AI agents become more capable at reading, writing, and running code, it is natural to ask how well they can already navigate this landscape, both in ways that improve security and in ways that might increase risk. We introduce EVMbench, an evaluation that measures the ability of agents to detect, patch, and exploit smart contract vulnerabilities. EVMbench draws on 117 curated vulnerabilities from 40 repositories and, in the most realistic setting, uses programmatic grading based on tests and blockchain state under a local Ethereum execution environment. We evaluate a range of frontier agents and find that they are capable of discovering and exploiting vulnerabilities end-to-end against live blockchain instances. We release code, tasks, and tooling to support continued measurement of these capabilities and future work on security.

</details>


### [131] [BandPO: Bridging Trust Regions and Ratio Clipping via Probability-Aware Bounds for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04918)
*Yuan Li,Bo Wang,Yufei Gao,Yuqian Yao,Xinyuan Wang,Zhangyue Yin,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Band-constrained Policy Optimization (BandPO)，通过引入Band算子替换PPO中的标准裁剪机制，解决固定边界约束低概率动作向上更新裕度的问题，从而改善高优势尾部策略的探索并缓解熵崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PPO中的标准裁剪机制存在关键瓶颈：固定边界严格约束低概率动作的向上更新裕度，导致高优势尾部策略被过度抑制，同时引发快速熵崩溃，限制了强化学习在大型语言模型中的稳定性和探索能力。

Method: 提出Band算子作为统一理论算子，将由f-散度定义的信任域投影到动态的、感知概率的裁剪区间。具体将映射表述为凸优化问题，保证全局最优数值解，并针对特定散度推导出闭式解。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上的广泛实验表明，BandPO一致性地优于标准裁剪和Clip-Higher方法，同时有效地缓解了熵崩溃问题。

Conclusion: BandPO通过动态概率感知的裁剪机制成功解决了PPO裁剪机制的探索瓶颈，理论分析与实验验证均表明其在LLM强化学习中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Proximal constraints are fundamental to the stability of the Large Language Model reinforcement learning. While the canonical clipping mechanism in PPO serves as an efficient surrogate for trust regions, we identify a critical bottleneck: fixed bounds strictly constrain the upward update margin of low-probability actions, disproportionately suppressing high-advantage tail strategies and inducing rapid entropy collapse. To address this, we introduce Band-constrained Policy Optimization (BandPO). BandPO replaces canonical clipping with Band, a unified theoretical operator that projects trust regions defined by f-divergences into dynamic, probability-aware clipping intervals. Theoretical analysis confirms that Band effectively resolves this exploration bottleneck. We formulate this mapping as a convex optimization problem, guaranteeing a globally optimal numerical solution while deriving closed-form solutions for specific divergences. Extensive experiments across diverse models and datasets demonstrate that BandPO consistently outperforms canonical clipping and Clip-Higher, while robustly mitigating entropy collapse.

</details>


### [132] [Semantic Communication-Enhanced Split Federated Learning for Vehicular Networks: Architecture, Challenges, and Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04936)
*Lu Yu,Zheng Chang,Ying-Chang Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出面向车联网边缘智能的语义通信增强型U形分裂联邦学习框架 SC-USFL：在保留 U-SFL"头+尾留端、骨干卸载边缘服务器"隐私优势的基础上，引入基于深度联合信源信道编码（Deep JSCC）的语义通信模块（SCM，参数预训练后冻结，仅作用于车端→边缘服务器的上行关键路径），并通过网络状态监视模块（NSM）依据实时信道状态自适应选择语义压缩率。实验在 CIFAR-10、AWGN 与 Rayleigh 信道下表明，SC-USFL 以轻微精度波动换取显著通信时延与负载下降，兼顾通信效率、标签隐私与学习性能，并给出多项开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车联网边缘智能（VEI）中，集中式学习面临通信开销大、时延高、隐私风险与动态拓扑下扩展性差的问题；联邦学习（FL）虽保护原始数据但客户端算力负担重、模型更新传输开销大；分裂学习（SL）降低客户端计算却带来高维" smashed data"传输与串行训练瓶颈；分裂联邦学习（SFL）兼顾并行训练与低客户端负载，但仍存在中间特征通信开销巨大、以及取决于切分方式（尤其是损失计算位置）的标签隐私泄露风险。同时，传统面向比特无误传输的通信协议对所有比特一视同仁，无法针对学习任务适配，且对车联网快速时变信道高度敏感，难以应对高冗余、高维中间特征的传输。此外，现有语义增强框架多基于标准 FL 而忽略模型切分与标签隐私问题，自适应传输方案多以 PSNR 等重建保真度为目标而非任务导向的优化目标。因此需要将语义通信深度嵌入具备隐私保证的专用 SFL 架构中，并实现信道自适应的压缩控制。

Method: 整体框架：SC-USFL = U-SFL 拓扑 + 语义通信模块（SCM）+ 网络状态监视模块（NSM）。(1) U-SFL 架构：模型分为三段——车端头模块（初始层）、边缘服务器体模块（计算密集的骨干中间层）、车端尾模块（最终层与损失计算），标签始终留在车端，结构性保证标签隐私，同时实现算力均衡；采用浅切分以降低车端能耗，由 SCM 承担浅卸载带来的通信时延补偿。(2) SCM：客户端由语义编码器 SE + 信道编码器 CE 构成，服务器由信道解码器 CD + 语义解码器 SD 构成，仅针对车端→ES 的上行链路做任务导向压缩与模拟符号传输；SCM 以 Deep JSCC 自编码器在模拟 AWGN 信道下端到端预训练后参数冻结，避免上行梯度传输开销，并抑制信道引起的梯度方差。(3) NSM：被动监测 SNR、信道衰落等 CSI，实时反馈给 SE，从预定义集合 {1/3, 1/6, 1/8, 1/12} 中动态选择语义压缩率（CR），开销可忽略。(4) 训练流程（每轮）：车端头提取中间特征→语义编码压缩并映射为模拟符号上行→服务器解码重建特征送入体模块前向→体模块输出特征下行→车端尾模块用私有标签计算损失并更新本地模型；各车端并行执行，ES 聚合体模块更新。(5) 实验设置：CIFAR-10 图像分类，车端头为 ResNet-50 + 分类尾，ES 体模块为 ViT-B/16；200 通信轮、3 本地 epoch、batch 64、Adam lr=1e-4；对比基线为 FL、无语义压缩的 USFL、SFL、集中式训练与本地训练；信道为 AWGN 与 Rayleigh；指标为测试精度、训练损失、每轮时延、任务成功率与 PSNR。

Result: 精度方面：FL/SFL/USFL 等基线可达到较高测试精度，但时延随车辆数增长呈"惩罚性"上升，因未压缩的高维中间特征传输成为严重瓶颈；SC-USFL 以任务导向语义压缩换取"轻微精度波动"，在资源受限车联网中更具实用性。压缩率与性能的权衡被明确量化：更强压缩（更低 CR，如 1/12）减少传输量但导致更高语义失真、更低 PSNR 与更高任务损失；CR=1/3 压缩最温和则精度最高。该趋势在不同 CR 与不同 SNR 下、以及在更具挑战性的 Rayleigh 衰落信道下保持一致，验证了框架的鲁棒性与有效性，也说明基于实时信道条件动态调节压缩率（自适应压缩）的必要性。结论性证据支持 SC-USFL 能在保持 U-SFL 隐私优势的前提下，有效平衡通信负载与任务性能。作者同时坦承三点局限：NSM 目前为离散动作空间（固定 CR 集合），粗粒度适应存在量化效率损失，逊于连续速率控制；评估假设完美 CSI，实际高移动性导致的 CSI 时延与估计误差会降低 NSM 决策精度；当前工作主要处理视觉数据，多模态异构数据需设计同步的多模态语义编码器。

Conclusion: 本文以 SC-USFL 作为案例研究，证明语义通信可有效缓解 SFL 的通信瓶颈：将"传输任务相关意义"替代比特级复制，SCM 在上行链路上仅传输低维语义向量并借助 Deep JSCC 提升抗噪与抗衰落鲁棒性；参数冻结设计兼顾了通信效率与训练稳定性；NSM 提供的信道感知自适应压缩是应对车联网动态性的关键设计原则。U 形拓扑在延迟、能耗、精度与隐私之间取得良好折中：浅切分降低车端能耗，卸载计算密集骨干提升精度，本地固定尾模块在结构上隔离真实标签保证标签隐私。论文进一步指出语义通信与 SFL 融合的开放方向：跨任务与跨模态的通用语义表征（基础模型、LiDAR 与 RGB 的跨模态注意力对齐）；语义通信的安全与隐私（用互信息估计量化模型反演等隐私边界、构建语义差分隐私、语义编码器的认证鲁棒性与主动对抗训练）；语义知识管理与利用（GNN 维护分布式语义知识图谱、条件熵编码传输"语义残差"）；信息新鲜度与价值（语义信息年龄 SAoI、以 Shapley 值度量语义信息价值 VoSI 以支持重要性感知调度）。总体而言，SC-USFL 为车联网边缘智能提供了兼顾通信效率、隐私与性能的可行方案。

Abstract: Vehicular edge intelligence (VEI) is vital for future intelligent transportation systems. However, traditional centralized learning in dynamic vehicular networks faces significant communication overhead and privacy risks. Split federated learning (SFL) offers a distributed solution but is often hindered by substantial communication bottlenecks from transmitting high-dimensional intermediate features and can present label privacy concerns. Semantic communication offers a transformative approach to alleviate these communication challenges in SFL by focusing on transmitting only task-relevant information. This paper leverages the advantages of semantic communication in the design of SFL, and presents a case study the semantic communication-enhanced U-Shaped split federated learning (SC-USFL) framework that inherently enhances label privacy by localizing sensitive computations with reduced overhead. It features a dedicated semantic communication module (SCM), with pre-trained and parameter-frozen encoding/decoding units, to efficiently compress and transmit only the task-relevant semantic information over the critical uplink path from vehicular users to the edge server (ES). Furthermore, a network status monitor (NSM) module enables adaptive adjustment of the semantic compression rate in real-time response to fluctuating wireless channel conditions. The SC-USFL framework demonstrates a promising approach for efficiently balancing communication load, preserving privacy, and maintaining learning performance in resource-constrained vehicular environments. Finally, this paper highlights key open research directions to further advance the synergy between semantic communication and SFL in the vehicular network.

</details>


### [133] [$\nabla$-Reasoner: LLM Reasoning via Test-Time Gradient Descent in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04948)
*Peihao Wang,Ruisi Cai,Zhen Wang,Hongyuan Mei,Qiang Liu,Pan Li,Zhangyang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出∇-Reasoner框架，通过在解码循环中集成可微分的文本优化（DTO），利用奖励模型的梯度信号来实时改进LLM推理策略。理论上证明了推理时在样本空间进行梯度下降以最大化奖励与KL正则化强化学习的对偶性。在数学推理基准上，该方法实现了超过20%的准确率提升，并减少了10-40%的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时间扩展方法通常依赖于低效且次优的离散搜索算法或试错式提示来改进在线策略，缺乏一种高效且系统性的方法来提升LLM的推理能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为∇-Reasoner的迭代生成框架，其核心是Differentiable Textual Optimization (DTO)。DTO利用来自LLM似然和奖励模型的梯度信号来优化文本表示，并将梯度下降纳入解码循环中以实时改进策略。该框架还整合了拒绝采样和加速设计来增强鲁棒性和加速解码。

Result: 在具有挑战性的数学推理基准上，∇-Reasoner实现了超过20%的准确率提升，同时与强基线相比减少了约10-40%的模型调用次数。

Conclusion: ∇-Reasoner通过将推理时的梯度下降与拒绝采样和加速设计相结合，在数学推理基准上实现了显著的性能提升，同时降低了计算成本。这项工作为LLM推理提供了一种从零阶搜索到一阶优化的范式转变，为增强LLM推理能力提供了一种经济有效的路径。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time compute for Large Language Models (LLMs) has unlocked unprecedented reasoning capabilities. However, existing inference-time scaling methods typically rely on inefficient and suboptimal discrete search algorithms or trial-and-error prompting to improve the online policy. In this paper, we propose $\nabla$-Reasoner, an iterative generation framework that integrates differentiable optimization over token logits into the decoding loop to refine the policy on the fly. Our core component, Differentiable Textual Optimization (DTO), leverages gradient signals from both the LLM's likelihood and a reward model to refine textual representations. $\nabla$-Reasoner further incorporates rejection sampling and acceleration design to robustify and speed up decoding. Theoretically, we show that performing inference-time gradient descent in the sample space to maximize reward is dual to aligning an LLM policy via KL-regularized reinforcement learning. Empirically, $\nabla$-Reasoner achieves over 20% accuracy improvement on a challenging mathematical reasoning benchmark, while reducing number of model calls by approximately 10-40% compared to strong baselines. Overall, our work introduces a paradigm shift from zeroth-order search to first-order optimization at test time, offering a cost-effective path to amplify LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [134] [Uncertainty-aware Blood Glucose Prediction from Continuous Glucose Monitoring Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04955)
*Hai Siong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于CGM数据的不确定性感知血糖预测模型，比较LSTM、GRU、Transformer三类序列架构，分别结合Monte Carlo Dropout与深度证据回归（Deep Evidential Regression, DER）两类不确定性量化方法，在HUPA-UCM（25名1型糖尿病患者）数据集上验证。结论：Transformer + 证据输出头（TEM）在全部实验中MARD最低、DTS误差网格A区准确率最高，且不确定性校准最佳（MCE最低、Spearman ρ约0.68与误差相关、ρ_z约0.34与临床风险分区相关），优于MC Dropout（过自信）与贝叶斯岭回归（欠自信），是可用于低/高血糖预警的候选方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续血糖监测（CGM）数据驱动的血糖预测可支持1型糖尿病的闭环胰岛素泵与饮食/运动剂量调整，但对低血糖（<70 mg/dL）与高血糖（>180 mg/dL）等不良事件，模型预测错误可能引发严重临床后果。既有工作多只关注预测精度，缺少对模型"何时不可靠"的可靠不确定性估计：例如Zhu et al. (2023)虽引入证据回归，但其置信带构造方式不正确、未使用任何校准指标、也未与其他不确定性量化方法比较；Manchanda et al. (2025) 则完全未涉及不确定性量化。因此本文聚焦于考察严谨的不确定性量化能否提升血糖预测系统（尤其是不良血糖事件识别）的有效性与临床安全性。

Method: 数据：HUPA-UCM公开数据集（Hidalgo et al., 2024），25名1型糖尿病患者，FreeStyle Libre 2 CGM（5分钟采样，每患者≥2周自由生活）+ Fitbit手环（心率、步数、卡路里）+ 餐食碳水、胰岛素剂量（bolus与basal）。使用其提供的预processed版本，无需额外清洗。数据按患者时间顺序切分60/20/20避免时间泄漏。输入为36×4的滑动窗口序列（3小时，4个特征：血糖、bolus胰岛素、碳水摄入，加心率/步数/卡路里/basal胰岛素之一），z-score标准化；预测视野 h_L∈{6,12}（30分钟、1小时）。模型：(1) 单层单向LSTM（hidden 128）+ FC头；(2) 3层双向GRU（每向hidden 40）+ 时间注意力 + FC头；(3) 因果Transformer编码器（2层，d_model 64，4头，FFN 128，GELU，含位置编码与因果mask）。不确定性：MC Dropout（测试时多次前向采样，MSE损失）与Deep Evidential Regression（输出层扩展为四元组(α,β,ν,γ)，NIG先验积分得t分布，σ_a²=β/(α−1)，σ_e²=β/((α−1)ν)，σ_p²=(1+1/ν)β/(α−1)；损失 L_evidential = L_data + 0.01·L_kl，其中L_kl为Tan et al. (2025)提出的乘以|y_obs−γ|的KL正则项）。基线：贝叶斯岭回归（MacKay）。训练300轮，Adam，lr=1e-4，batch 1024，NVIDIA A100。评估：精度用DTS误差网格（Klonoff et al., 2024）A区准确率与MARD；事件识别用低/高血糖敏感度、Brier、PR-AUC；不确定性校准用Spearman ρ（不确定性 vs 预测误差）、Spearman ρ_z（不确定性 vs DTS风险分区1–5序数）、可靠性图（ECP vs NCP）与MCE、预测区间覆盖率。统计检验用Friedman检验与Holm校正Wilcoxon符号秩检验（Kendall's W效应量）。

Result: 精度：所有神经网络模型的DTS A区准确率均高于岭回归基线，MARD均更低；Transformer证据模型(TEM)在全部实验中MARD最低（如心率输入30分钟MARD 4.14 vs 岭回归5.69；60分钟8.11 vs 10.10）、A区准确率基本最佳（30分钟约96.8–96.9%，60分钟约89.2%）。1小时视野相对30分钟平均A区准确率下降约8%、MARD上升约4%。低血糖敏感度：证据模型与岭回归最高（1小时约0.9，30分钟再高约5%）。校准：证据模型Spearman ρ约0.63–0.68（至少为对应Dropout模型的两倍），ρ_z约0.2（短视野）/0.3（长视野），TEM平均ρ_z约0.34最高；Dropout模型ρ约0.1–0.3、ρ_z约0.1；岭回归ρ约0.48–0.52但欠自信。MCE：证据模型最低（TEM 30分钟0.02–0.03，60分钟约0.004–0.014），Dropout与岭回归明显更高（0.07–0.23）。可靠性图显示岭回归欠自信、MC Dropout过自信、证据模型ECP贴近理想对角线。Brier最低、PR-AUC最高均由证据模型取得，TEM最优（低血糖PR-AUC约0.94–0.96，高血糖约0.99）。输入特征分析：Friedman检验显示30分钟MARD存在显著特征效应（χ²(3)=11.9, p<0.01，Kendall W=0.16，小效应），心率输入最优（Holm校正p≈0.01 vs 步数/卡路里；长视野对步数p≈0.04），其余指标无显著差异。预测图显示：即使点预测越过或未越过低/高血糖阈值，1σ/2σ不确定带仍能覆盖真实血糖，从而可作为动态预警阈值提高事件检出率。

Conclusion: 带证据输出头的Transformer（TEM）是本文验证中最有效的不确定性感知框架：既具备更优的临床精度（MARD、DTS A区准确率），又能给出良好校准、且与预测误差和临床风险分区显著相关的不确定性估计；相比之下MC Dropout过自信、贝叶斯岭回归欠自信。作者指出evidential模型在精度上也普遍优于其Dropout/无UQ对应模型，说明不确定性量化不仅提供可靠性信号，还与精度提升相关。展望：改用个体化（单患者）训练数据、更多输入特征组合的排列、以及将不确定带作为动态检测阈值在闭环系统中的实际部署验证。局限：数据集仅25名患者、单一数据集外部验证不足、1小时视野性能明显下降、未评估真实临床工作流效用。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate uncertainty-aware neural network models for blood glucose prediction and adverse glycemic event identification in Type 1 diabetes. We consider three families of sequence models based on LSTM, GRU, and Transformer architectures, with uncertainty quantification enabled by either Monte Carlo dropout or through evidential output layers compatible with Deep Evidential Regression. Using the HUPA-UCM diabetes dataset for validation, we find that Transformer-based models equipped with evidential output heads provide the most effective uncertainty-aware framework, achieving consistently higher predictive accuracies and better-calibrated uncertainty estimates whose magnitudes significantly correlate with prediction errors. We further evaluate the clinical risk of each model using the recently proposed Diabetes Technology Society error grid, with risk categories defined by international expert consensus. Our results demonstrate the value of integrating principled uncertainty quantification into real-time machine-learning-based blood glucose prediction systems.

</details>


### [135] [WaterSIC: information-theoretically (near) optimal linear layer quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04956)
*Egor Lifar,Semyon Savkin,Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究如何将密集线性层转换为低精度（量化）形式。提出了一种名为WaterSIC的新算法，通过类似"水填充"的信息论方法为权重矩阵的不同列分配不同的量化比特数，在Llama和Qwen系列LLM上实现了1-4比特量化的最新性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPTQ算法与信息论(IT)极限之间可能存在任意大的差距，需要一种能够接近IT极限的统一量化方法。

Method: 提出WaterSIC算法，核心创新在于为权重矩阵的不同列（输入特征）分配不同的量化比特数，模拟经典信息论中的"水填充"方案。该方法统一地适用于所有可能的输入激活协方差矩阵，与IT极限的差距不超过0.255比特。

Result: WaterSIC在Llama和Qwen系列大型语言模型上建立了1-4比特量化的新SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过借鉴经典信息论的水填充思想，WaterSIC算法能够有效缩小与IT极限的差距，为LLM量化提供了一种统一且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper considers the problem of converting a given dense linear layer to low precision. The tradeoff between compressed length and output discrepancy is analyzed information theoretically (IT). It is shown that a popular GPTQ algorithm may have an arbitrarily large gap to the IT limit. To alleviate this problem, a novel algorithm, termed ''WaterSIC'', is proposed and is shown to be within a rate gap of 0.255 bits to the IT limit, uniformly over all possible covariance matrices of input activations. The key innovation of WaterSIC's is to allocate different quantization rates to different columns (in-features) of the weight matrix, mimicking the classical IT solution known as ''waterfilling''. Applying WaterSIC to the Llama and Qwen family of LLMs establishes new state-of-the-art performance for all quantization rates from 1 to 4 bits.

</details>


### [136] [Mixture of Universal Experts: Scaling Virtual Width via Depth-Width Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04971)
*Yilong Chen,Naibin Gu,Junyuan Shang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yuchen Feng,Jiawei Sheng,Tingwen Liu,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Hua Wu,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOUE通过引入"虚拟宽度"缩放维度扩展了传统MoE架构，在固定计算预算下将深度转换为虚拟宽度，并通过结构化专家共享、深度感知负载均衡和连贯多步路由三个核心创新，在多个缩放 regimes 上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE（Mixture-of-Experts）虽然解耦了模型容量与每token计算，但其可扩展性仍受限于深度和宽度的物理维度。为克服这一限制，需要引入新的缩放维度。

Method: 提出MOUE（Mixture of Universal Experts），通过三个核心组件实现：（1）Staggered Rotational Topology用于结构化专家共享；（2）Universal Expert Load Balance用于深度感知的暴露校正；（3）Universal Router使用轻量级轨迹状态实现连贯的多步路由。在固定每token激活预算下，将深度转换为虚拟宽度。

Result: MOUE在缩放 regimes 上始终优于匹配的MoE基线高达1.3%，能够对现有MoE检查点进行渐进式转换并获得高达4.2%的收益，揭示了MoE架构的新缩放维度。

Conclusion: MOUE成功引入了"虚拟宽度"作为新的缩放维度，有效解决了传统MoE在深度和宽度维度上的物理限制，为MoE架构的可扩展性提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decouples model capacity from per-token computation, yet their scalability remains limited by the physical dimensions of depth and width. To overcome this, we propose Mixture of Universal Experts (MOUE),a MoE generalization introducing a novel scaling dimension: Virtual Width. In general, MoUE aims to reuse a universal layer-agnostic expert pool across layers, converting depth into virtual width under a fixed per-token activation budget. However, two challenges remain: a routing path explosion from recursive expert reuse, and a mismatch between the exposure induced by reuse and the conventional load-balancing objectives. We address these with three core components: a Staggered Rotational Topology for structured expert sharing, a Universal Expert Load Balance for depth-aware exposure correction, and a Universal Router with lightweight trajectory state for coherent multi-step routing. Empirically, MoUE consistently outperforms matched MoE baselines by up to 1.3% across scaling regimes, enables progressive conversion of existing MoE checkpoints with up to 4.2% gains, and reveals a new scaling dimension for MoE architectures.

</details>


### [137] [Functionality-Oriented LLM Merging on the Fisher--Rao Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04972)
*Jiayu Wang,Zuojun Ye,Wenpeng Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文把「权重空间模型融合」重新表述为 Fisher–Rao 流形上的（加权）Karcher/Fréchet 均值问题，即最小化合并模型与各专家之间的 KL 型函数距离，而非欧氏弦长；并用「分块单位球代理 + 保范数重缩放」实现轻量级定点迭代算法（两模型时退化为 SLERP，可自然扩展到 N>2）。在 Qwen2.5 家族 5 个基准、m=2…11 的合并实验与层级激活诊断中，该方法在平均分上全面领先 LERP/SLERP/TIES/DARE/DELLA/Model Stock/SCE/Arcee Fusion，并显著缓解表示坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型融合方法（线性平均、Task Arithmetic、TIES、DARE、DELLA 等）本质上都是欧氏参数空间启发式规则，作者指出三个缺陷：(1) 目标是合并「功能」（预测行为）而非参数，度量错位；(2) 当源模型距离更远或异质性更强时，欧氏混合会切过曲面曲率，引发「表示坍塌」——激活方差收缩、有效秩下降，准确率骤降；(3) 几何类方法（SLERP、ChipAlign）天然只适用于两模型插值，缺乏面向 N>2 的原则性目标。作者希望引入一个以功能距离为目标的、在多专家下仍有原则性定义且可扩展的融合框架。

Method: 目标函数为加权 Fréchet/Karcher 均值 θ*=argmin_θ Σ_i α_i d_FR²(θ, θ_i)；利用小位移下 d_FR² ≈ (θ-θ')ᵀF(θ)(θ-θ') ≈ 2KL(p_θ‖p_θ')，将其解释为最小化对教师模型的期望 KL，从而与 NLL/PPL 改善对齐。一阶条件为 Σ_i α_i Log_{θ*}(θ_i)=0，用 Riemannian 不动点迭代 v^(t)=Σ_i α_i Log_{θ^(t)}(θ_i)，θ^(t+1)=Exp_{θ^(t)}(η v^(t))。由于真实 Fisher–Rao 对数/指数映射不可计算，作者采用「球面代理」：把每个参数块（层/张量组）归一化到单位球面 S^{d-1}，用闭式球面 log/exp 计算球面 Karcher 均值（方向重心），再按源模型该块的平均范数重缩放，得到保范数的合并；并在 log map 内可选地施加对角/KFAC Fisher 预条件（自然梯度式），保护高 Fisher 敏感方向、减少敏感子空间的破坏性干扰。两模型等权且从 θ^(0) 初始化时，结果为测地中点，在球面代理下恰好退化为 SLERP。实现基于 MergeKit（新增 karcher.py 的 PR），免调参、免训练。

Result: 基准：GPQA-Diamond、HellaSwag、BBH、MMLU-Pro、MuSR（LM Evaluation Harness，Qwen2.5 家族）。m=2 时 Karcher 平均分 0.597，优于 LERP 0.577、SLERP 0.575、TIES 0.540、Arcee Fusion 0.538、DARE/DELLA 约 0.50–0.52。m=5 时 Karcher 0.610，显著领先 Model Stock 0.557、LERP 0.542、SCE 0.461，而 SLERP 0.239、TIES 0.253、DARE/DELLA 约 0.238–0.248（多数欧氏/裁剪类规则在 m≈5 出现明显断崖式坍塌）。m=2…11 全量结果中 Karcher 稳定在 0.593–0.615，随合并数量增加无退化。跨尺度（135M/360M/1.7B）两两合并：Karcher 0.246/0.282/0.401，均优于 Ties（0.240/0.271/0.391）、Slerp（0.230/0.274/0.395）、Lerp（0.245/0.269/0.398）。层级诊断（Table 7）显示 Karcher 在中深层保持更高激活方差与 EffRank/NumRank（如第 4 层方差 951、EffRank 42.4），而 TIES 出现极端方差坍塌（14.6）、Lerp 显著偏低（104），Model Stock 居中。

Conclusion: 把功能导向的模型融合形式化为 Fisher–Rao 流形上的 Karcher 均值，得到的算法用球面代理把 SLERP 从两模型推广到任意 N>2，兼具原则性目标、轻量实现与免调参特性；其收益主要体现在模型距离更远、异质性更强的区间，而这恰是欧氏融合最易失败的区间。作者自陈局限：依赖球面代理而非精确 Fisher–Rao 测地线，在强非线性区域可能有偏差；定点迭代依赖初始化、步长与停止准则，缺全局收敛保证；评测集中于单一模型家族与榜单式基准，泛化性待验证；权重空间方法固有的许可与安全对齐问题未解决。

Abstract: Weight-space merging aims to combine multiple fine-tuned LLMs into a single model without retraining, yet most existing approaches remain fundamentally parameter-space heuristics. This creates three practical limitations. First, linear averaging, task vectors, and related rules operate on Euclidean coordinates, even though the desired goal is to merge functionality, i.e., predictive behaviors across tasks. Second, when the source checkpoints are farther apart or more heterogeneous, Euclidean blends often trigger representation collapse, manifested as activation variance shrinkage and effective-rank degradation, which sharply degrades accuracy. Third, many geometry-inspired methods are most natural for two-model interpolation and do not extend cleanly to merging N>2 experts with a principled objective.
  We address these issues by formulating model merging as computing a weighted Karcher mean on the Fisher--Rao manifold, which is locally equivalent to minimizing a KL-based function distance between predictive distributions. We derive a practical fixed-point algorithm using a lightweight spherical proxy that preserves norms and generalizes directly to multi-expert merging. Across various benchmarks and collapse diagnostics, our method remains stable as the number and heterogeneity of merged models increase, consistently outperforming prior baselines.

</details>


### [138] [Lightweight and Scalable Transfer Learning Framework for Load Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04998)
*L. E. Garcia-Marrero,G. Petrone,E. Monmasson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RefQuery 是一个面向边缘设备的多电器、多任务非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）迁移学习框架：它把分解过程条件化在一个紧凑的“电器指纹”（参考窗口嵌入）上，仅需冻结预训练网络、每种电器只学习 E 维嵌入即可完成新家庭适配，从而以单一模型服务任意数量电器，兼顾精度、极小存储与快速实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非侵入式负荷监测（NILM）需从单一测点的总功率信号中还原各电器功耗，但深度模型跨域泛化能力差，因为不同住宅的电器特性、用电习惯与背景负荷差异显著。已有迁移学习方法存在若干缺陷：单电器范式需“一电器一模型”，存储随电器数线性增长；多电器范式输出层或子网络绑定固定电器集合，难以增量扩展；部分高准确率方法（如 SAMNet、MATNilm、PSSR-Net 的 ResNet50 主干）参数与推理开销过大，不适合算力/内存受限的边缘设备，且难以支撑实时运行。数据增强、元学习、自监督、域适应等通用性方法依赖源域多样性或大量无标签目标域聚合数据，无法保证在未见家庭上的鲁棒性。因此需要一种可增量扩展、适配代价极低、可在边缘端实时运行的条件化 NILM 方案。

Method: 三阶段框架。Stage I（源域训练）：以 REFIT 全部 20 栋住宅为源域，采样周期 8 s、窗口长度 L=599（seq2point 形式，中心点 t+⌊L/2⌋ 监督）。对每栋建筑每种电器构建参考库 R_{b,k}（取电器各 ON 区间中点居中的长度 L 窗口），ON 区间由 20 W 阈值生成掩码并经时长后处理（短于 60 s 的 ON 段清除，短于 300 s 的 OFF 间隙桥接）。每个总表窗口 q 配对一个采样参考窗口，经共享 1-D CNN 编码器 f_θ 提取 E 维嵌入并做 L2 归一化；编码器为 5 层小核(k=3)卷积+ReLU+same padding，最大池化，首层即做 stride-2 下采样以早期压缩时间维度。多任务预测头 h_φ 将 [e_r, e_q, (e_q−e_r)², e_q⊙e_r] 拼接为 4E 维，经两层宽度 H 的共享 FC 后分为分类支路（sigmoid 输出 ON/OFF）与回归支路（ReLU 输出非负增量 Δz），功率经门控 ŷ_z = Δz·ŷ_state + b_off（b_off=−μ/σ 锚定零瓦工作点）。损失为 L = L_MSE + L_BCE（等权）。Stage II（目标域适配）：完全冻结 f_θ 与 h_φ，仅为每种电器随机初始化并优化目标域参考嵌入 ẽ_r^(k)∈R^E，损失同式，每新增电器重复一次，适配参数仅为 E。Stage III（推理）：用冻结编码器提取查询嵌入并以已学嵌入条件化预测头，输出状态与 z 归一化功率，再反归一化回瓦特。超参为 E=H=128（对数网格 {32,64,128,256} 敏感性分析选出），源域 Adam lr=1e-3、batch 1024、patience 5 早停；适配 lr=1e-2、按训练损失早停。数据集为 REFIT（源）、REDD-1/3 与 UKDALE-1/2（目标），目标电器为冰箱、洗碗机、洗衣机、电热水壶、微波炉。基线为 SA-S2P（单电器 seq2point，冻结卷积特征仅调顶层）、MA-CNN（一对多多电器 CNN，重训顶层再微调共享层）、MA-TRF（Transformer+注意力多电器迁移，细调电器专属支路）。评测含 7 天适配与 1 天适配两种场景，指标为 MAE 与 F1。

Result: 精度方面（7 天适配）：UK-DALE-1 上 RefQuery 平均 MAE 7.35 最佳，较 Transformer 基线 MA-TRF 的 7.54 改善约 2.5%，并优于 SA-S2P(8.25)、MA-CNN(10.01)；UK-DALE-2 上平均 MAE 6.54 居第二，仅略逊 MA-TRF(5.13)，但平均 F1 0.738 最佳，较 MA-TRF 提升约 4%；REDD-1 上平均 F1 0.651 最佳，MAE 14.94 与最优 MA-TRF(14.28) 相差约 4–5%（DW 的 F1 提升约 6%、MAE 降低近 19%）；REDD-3 上 F、DW、MW 的 MAE 最优，平均 MAE 被 WM（约 5 kW 异常高功率）拉高而落后。有限数据（1 天适配）场景优势更明显：UKDALE-1 平均 MAE 8.53 最佳，较 MA-TRF 改善约 8%，F1 0.657 最高；REDD-1 MAE 17.77 略逊于 MA-TRF(16.59)，但平均 F1 高出约 20%。鲁棒性（7 天→1 天相对退化）：UKDALE-1 上 RefQuery 平均 MAE 退化 16.1%（较 MA-TRF 少约 31%），平均 F1 仅下降 3.7%（较 MA-TRF 少约 75%）；REDD-1 上 F1 退化减少约 70%。代价方面（CPU 限双核）：存储 1.18 MB，比 MA-TRF 少约 90%、比 MA-CNN 少约 50%、比 SA-S2P 少约 99.6%；适配时间 63.2 s，比 MA-TRF 快约 70×、比 MA-CNN 快约 78×、比 SA-S2P 快约 85×；推理 42.41 s，比 MA-TRF 快约 5.9×。可扩展性：每新增电器仅需约 1 KB 存储且无需更新共享参数，推理开销为 4.145 + K×0.165 MFLOPs/窗口，远低于 SA-S2P（K×101.289）与 MA-TRF（889.560+K×1142.726）。不足之处：微波炉（MW）因稀疏短时激活，F1 波动大且在 UKDALE-1 上低至 0.010；REDD-3 上 WM 绝对 MAE 明显偏高。

Conclusion: RefQuery 通过把 NILM 分解条件化在紧凑的电器指纹（参考嵌入）上，突破了“一电器一模型”与固定输出集两种范式：只需冻结网络并逐电器学习一个 E 维嵌入，即可实现电器的即插即用增量扩展，新增电器的存储开销近乎可忽略且不改动共享参数。实验表明它在 MAE 与 F1 上与单电器、多电器乃至 Transformer 类先进基线形成有竞争力的精度—效率折中，在标注稀缺（仅 1 天适配）时表现出最强的整体表现与显著更高的检测精度稳定性；模型体积、适配时间与推理开销大幅下降（适配最多快约 85×），为资源受限边缘设备上的可扩展、实时 NILM 提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring (NILM) aims to estimate appliance-level consumption from aggregate electrical signals recorded at a single measurement point. In recent years, the field has increasingly adopted deep learning approaches; however, cross-domain generalization remains a persistent challenge due to variations in appliance characteristics, usage patterns, and background loads across homes. Transfer learning provides a practical paradigm to adapt models with limited target data. However, existing methods often assume a fixed appliance set, lack flexibility for evolving real-world deployments, remain unsuitable for edge devices, or scale poorly for real-time operation.
